# Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin

ברטנורא על משנה עירובין

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin)

חיבור התשתית הקדום של חז״ל, המתעד מגוון דעות הלכתיות ממסורת התורה שבעל פה. נחתם בשנת 200 לספירה לערך.

16th-century commentary printed in most editions of the Mishnah, including summaries of talmudic discussions and legal conclusions.

Sections: Chapter-Mishnah-Comment

Location: Mishnah/Rishonim on Mishnah/Bartenura/Seder Moed

## Chapter 1


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:1:1)


**מָבוֹי.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ רָחָב שֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה אַמָּה, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא פָּתוּחַ מִשְּׁנֵי רָאשָׁיו לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, אִי נַמִּי רָחָב שֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה אַמָּה וְאֵינוֹ מְפֻלָּשׁ אֶלָּא רֹאשׁוֹ אֶחָד פָּתוּחַ לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים וְרֹאשׁוֹ אֶחָד סָתוּם. וּמִן הַתּוֹרָה שָׁרֵי לְטַלְטוֹלֵי בֵּיהּ בְּלֹא שׁוּם תִּקּוּן, וְרַבָּנָן הוּא דְּגָזוּר עֲלֵיהּ דִּלְמָא אָתֵי לְטַלְטוֹלֵי בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים גָּמוּר, וְשַׁרְיוּהּ בְּתַקַּנְתָּא דְּלֶחִי אוֹ קוֹרָה דְּתֶהֱוֵי לֵיהּ הֶכֵּירָא. וְאִם הֵנִיחַ אֶת הַקּוֹרָה לְמַעְלָה מֵעֶשְׂרִים אַמָּה יְמַעֵט, כְּלוֹמַר יַשְׁפִּיל אֶת הַקּוֹרָה עַד שֶׁתִּהְיֶה לְמַטָּה מֵעֶשְׂרִים, דִּלְמַעְלָה מֵעֶשְׂרִים לֹא שָׁלְטָא בָּהּ עֵינָא. וְאִם יֵשׁ בְּקוֹרָה [זוֹ] אֲמַלְתְּרָא, פֵּרוּשׁ צִיּוּרִים וּפִתּוּחִים, אֲפִלּוּ לְמַעְלָה מֵעֶשְׂרִים אַמָּה אֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לְמַעֵט, שֶׁעַל יְדֵי אוֹתָם הַצִּיּוּרִים שָׁלְטָא בָּהּ עֵינָא:


מבוי – which is not wide sixteen cubits, even though it is open from its two points into the public domain; alternatively, it is sixteen cubits wide but it is not open, but one head is open to the public domain and the other head is closed, but from the Torah, it is permitted to carry in it without any repair, but the Rabbis made a decree against it, lest they come to carry in full public domain and they permitted with an establishment of a stake fastened in the ground (by the side of a wall, serving as a mark or as a fictitious partition for the purpose of enabling those who live in an ally to move objects on the Sabbath) or a beam/post so that it will have recognition/a sign. But if he placed the beam above twenty cubits high, he should lower it, meaning to say, he should lower the beam so that it will be lower than twenty cubits high, for higher than twenty cubits, it is not discernible to the eye/the eye is attracted, and if [his] beam has a ceiling (see Talmud Eruvin 3a – for different opinions of the meaning of his word), which means drawings/embroidery and is open even higher than twenty cubits, he does not have to lower it, for through these drawings/embroidery, it is discernible to the eye/the eye is attracted.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:1:2)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר אֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ.** דְּטַעֲמָא דְּקוֹרָה לָאו מִשּׁוּם הֶכֵּירָא אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם מְחִצָּה, דְּאָמְרִינַן פִּי תִּקְרָה יוֹרֵד וְסוֹתֵם, וְכֵיוָן שֶׁכֵּן הוּא מַה לִּי בְּתוֹךְ עֶשְׂרִים מַה לִּי לְמַעְלָה מֵעֶשְׂרִים. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי יהודה אומר א"צ – for the reason of the beam is not because of recognition but because of a partition since we said, the mouth of the ceiling goes down and closes up, and since his is the case, what difference does it make within twenty [cubits] and/or higher than twenty [cubits], but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:1:3)


**וְהָרָחָב מֵעֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת יְמַעֵט.** דִּטְפֵי מֵעֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת לֹא מִקְּרֵי פֶּתַח אֶלָּא פִּרְצָה, וַאֲנַן פֶּתַח בָּעִינַן, הִלְכָּךְ יְמַעֵט רֹחַב כְּנִיסָתוֹ וְיַעֲמִידֶנּוּ עַל עֶשֶׂר אוֹ עַל פָּחוֹת:


והרחב מעשר אמות ימעט – for more than ten cubits, it is not called an opening, but rather a breach, and we require an opening. Therefore, he should reduce the width of the entrance and reduce it to ten [cubits] or less.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:1:4)


**וְאִם יֶשׁ לוֹ צוּרַת הַפֶּתַח.** בְּכָל מָקוֹם שֶׁאָמְרוּ חֲכָמִים צוּרַת פֶּתַח הוּא אֲפִלּוּ קָנֶה מִכָּאן וְקָנֶה מִכָּאן גְּבוֹהִים עֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים אוֹ יוֹתֵר וְקָנֶה עַל גַּבֵּיהֶן, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ נוֹגֵעַ בָּהֶן, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁגָּבוֹהַּ מֵהֶן יוֹתֵר מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים. וְכָל הָאַמּוֹת הַשְּׁנוּיוֹת בְּמִשְׁנָה זוֹ וּבְכָל עֵרוּבִין וְסֻכָּה וְכִלְאַיִם, הֵם אַמָּה בַּת שִׁשָּׁה טְפָחִים, וְכָל טֶפַח אַרְבַּע אֶצְבָּעוֹת בְּגֻדָּל, אֶלָּא שֶׁפְּעָמִים צָרִיךְ לִמְדֹּד בְּטֶפַח עָצֵב, כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁלֹּא יָשִׂים רֶוַח בְּאֶצְבְּעוֹת הַגֻּדָּל בֵּין זוֹ לְזוֹ אֶלָּא יִהְיוּ מְצֻמְצָמוֹת וְנוֹגְעוֹת זוֹ בְּזוֹ, וּפְעָמִים צָרִיךְ לִתֵּן רֶוַח בֵּין גֻּדָּל לְגֻדָּל שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיוּ נוֹגְעִים זֶה בָּזֶה, וְזֶהוּ הַנִּקְרָא טֶפַח שׂוֹחֵק. כֵּיצַד, בִּזְמַן שֶׁהַמְּדִידָה בְּטֶפַח עָצֵב הִיא לְחֻמְרָא, כְּגוֹן מָבוֹי שֶׁהוּא גָּבוֹהַּ מֵעֶשְׂרִים אַמָּה יְמַעֵט, וְסֻכָּה שֶׁהִיא גְּבוֹהָה מֵעֶשְׂרִים אַמָּה פְּסוּלָה, מוֹדְדִים בְּטֶפַח עָצֵב. וּבִזְמַן שֶׁהַמְּדִידָה בְּטֶפַח שׂוֹחֵק הוּא לְחֻמְרָא, כְּגוֹן מָבוֹי אֵין גָּבְהוֹ פָּחוֹת מֵעֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים, סֻכָּה אֵין גָּבְהָהּ פָּחוֹת מֵעֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים, מוֹדְדִין בְּטֶפַח שׂוֹחֵק, וְכֵן לְעוֹלָם:


ואם יש לו צורת פתח – in every place where the Sages stated “a form of a doorway” (in width), even if his a reed from here and a reed from there ten handbreadths high or more, and a reed on top of them, even though it doesn’t touch them and even though it is higher than them more than three handbreadths. And all of the cubits that are taught in this Mishnah and in all of [Tractates] Eruvin, and Sukkah and Kilayim, a cubit is six handbreadths and each handbreadth is four fingers with the thumb, but sometimes one needs to measure a “sorrowful” (pressed together) handbreadth (i.e., four fingers closely joined), meaning to say, that one should not make a space in the fingers with the thumb between one to the other, but rather, they would be pressed against each other and touching each other, and sometimes, one must make a space between each digit so that they do not touch each other and this is called the liberal (or smiling) handbreadth. How is this the case? At the time when the measurement Is with the “sorrowful” (pressed together) handbreadth, it is stringent such as an alley entrance which is higher than twenty cubits, he should lower it, and a Sukkah which higher than twenty cubits is invalid, we measure with a “sorrowful” handbreadth, but at the time when the measurement is with a liberal (or “smiling”) handbreadth which is stringent, such as an alleyway entrance, its height cannot be less than ten handbreadths; a Sukkah which is higher than ten handbreadths, we measure with the liberal handbreadth.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:2:1)


**הֶכְשֵׁר מָבוֹי.** הֲכָנָתוֹ וְהַזְמָנָתוֹ שֶׁל מָבוֹי לְטַלְטֵל בְּתוֹכוֹ עַל יְדֵי שִׁתּוּפֵי מְבוֹאוֹת:


הכשר מבוי – its preparation and designation of the alley to carry within it through a combination of alleys.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:2:2)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים לֶחִי וְקוֹרָה.** שְׁנֵיהֶן צָרִיךְ, דִּסְבִירָא לְהוּ מִן הַתּוֹרָה אַרְבַּע מְחִצּוֹת שְׁלֵמוֹת בָּעִינַן, וַאֲתַאי הֲלָכָה לְמֹשֶׁה מִסִּינַי וְאוֹקִימְתָּא לָרְבִיעִית אַלֶּחִי וְקוֹרָה:


ב"ש אומרים לחי וקורה – both of them (i.e., a stake and a beam) are necessary and that they hold from the Torah that we require four complete partitions and it was brought as a traditional interpretation of a written law [dating back to Moses as delivered from Sinai) and the particular application is a square-block of a stake and a crossbeam.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:2:3)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים אוֹ לֶחִי אוֹ קוֹרָה.** דְּמִן הַתּוֹרָה שָׁלֹשׁ מְחִצּוֹת [שְׁלֵמוֹת] בָּעִינַן וְתוּ לֹא, וַאֲתַאי הֲלָכָה לְמֹשֶׁה מִסִּינַי לְהוֹסִיף הָרְבִיעִית, אוֹ בְּלֶחִי מַשֶּׁהוּ אוֹ בְּקוֹרָה לְהֶכֵּר מְחִצָּה. וּמָבוֹי דְּאָיְרֵי בֵּיהּ הָכָא הוּא מָבוֹי סָתוּם מִשָּׁלֹשׁ רוּחוֹתָיו וְרוּחַ רְבִיעִית פְּתוּחָה לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וְאָרְכּוֹ יוֹתֵר עַל רָחְבּוֹ, דְּאִי אָרְכּוֹ כְּרָחְבּוֹ הָוֵי כְּחָצֵר שֶׁנִּפְרְצָה הַפְּתוּחָה לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים וְצָרִיךְ פַּס אַרְבָּעָה וּמַשֶּׁהוּ אוֹ שְׁנֵי פַסִּין שֶׁל כָּל שֶׁהֵן. וְכֵן חָצֵר שֶׁנִּפְרְצָה לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים וְאָרְכָּהּ יוֹתֵר עַל רָחְבָּהּ נִדּוֹנָה כְּמָבוֹי וְנִתֶּרֶת בְּלֶחִי אוֹ קוֹרָה. וּמָבוֹי שֶׁהֻכְשַׁר בְּלֶחִי חָלוּק מִמָּבוֹי שֶׁהֻכְשַׁר בְּקוֹרָה, דְּאִלּוּ מָבוֹי שֶׁהֻכְשַׁר בְּלֶחִי הָוֵי כְּאִלּוּ יֵשׁ לוֹ אַרְבַּע מְחִצּוֹת, וְהַזּוֹרֵק מֵרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים לְתוֹכוֹ חַיָּב, וּמָבוֹי שֶׁהֻכְשַׁר בְּקוֹרָה אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמֻּתָּר לְטַלְטֵל בְּתוֹכוֹ עַל יְדֵי שִׁתּוּף לֹא הָוֵי כִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד גְּמוּרָה, וְהַזּוֹרֵק מֵרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים לְתוֹכוֹ פָּטוּר, דְּקָיְמָא לָן קוֹרָה מִשּׁוּם הֶכֵּירָא, וְלֶחִי מִשּׁוּם מְחִצָּה:


ובה"א או לחי או קור – for from the Torah three [complete] partitions are required and further nothing else and it was brought as a traditional interpretation of a written law [dating back to Moses as delivered from Sinai] either a stake of some small size or a beam to be recognized as a partition. And the alley that we are speaking of here is a closed alley from three directions and the fourth direction is open to the public domain and its length is greater than its width, for it its length was like its width, it would be like a courtyard whose opening was breached to the public domain and one would need a board/bar of four handbreadths and a bit more (see Talmud Eruvin 5a) or two boards/bars of a bit of size and similarly, a courtyard which was breached into the public domain and its length was greater than its width, it is judged to be an alley which is permitted with a stake or a board. But an alley that was made valid with a stake is different than an alley made valid with a beam, for an alley which was made valid with a stake, it is as if it has four partitions and a person who throws [something] from the public domain into it is liable, but an alley made valid with a beam, even though it is permissible to carry within it through a combination, it is not like a completely private domain and a person who throws [something] from the public domain into it is exempt, for we hold that a beam is because of recognition and a stake because of a partition.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:2:4)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר לְחָיַיִם.** כְּבֵית שַׁמַּאי סְבִירָא לֵיהּ, וּבָעֵי לֶחִי מִכָּאן וּמִכָּאן. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


רבי אליעזר אומר לחיים – he holds like the School of Shammai that requires a stake from one side and the other, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:2:5)


**אָמַר תַּלְמִיד אֶחָד.** הוּא רַבִּי מֵאִיר:


אמר תלמיד אחד – He is Rabbi Meir.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:2:6)


**שֶׁהוּא פָחוֹת מֵאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת.** רֹחַב פִּתְחוֹ:


שהוא פחות מארבע אמות – the width of its opening.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:2:7)


**אָמַר רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא עַל זֶה וְעַל זֶה נֶחֱלָקוּ.** וְתַנָּא קַמָּא נַמִּי הָכִי קָאָמַר, דְּלֹא מַפְלִיג בֵּין רָחָב לְקָצָר. וּמְפָרֵשׁ תַּלְמוּדָא דְּאִיכָּא בֵּינַיְהוּ מָבוֹי שֶׁיֵּשׁ בְּרֹחַב פִּתְחוֹ פָּחוֹת מֵאַרְבָּעָה טְפָחִים, דְּחַד מִנַּיְהוּ סָבַר אֵין צָרִיךְ לֹא לֶחִי וְלֹא קוֹרָה, וְאִידָךְ סָבַר צָרִיךְ לֶחִי אוֹ קוֹרָה, וְלֹא הֻבְרַר מִתּוֹךְ דִּבְרֵיהֶם הֵי מִנַּיְהוּ סָבַר צָרִיךְ וְהֵי מִנַּיְהוּ סָבַר אֵין צָרִיךְ:


אמר רבי עקיבא על זה ועל זה נחלקו – The first Tanna/teacher also this is how it should be read: It does distinguish between wide and narrow and the Talmud explains that there is a difference between them: An alley that has less than four handbreadths in the width of its opening – one of them holds that it requires neither a stake nor a beam and the other one holds a stake or a beam but it is not made clear from their words which of them holds that it is required and which of them holds that it is not required.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:3:1)


**רְחָבָה כְּדֵי לְקַבֵּל אָרִיחַ.** שֶׁתְּהֵא רְאוּיָה לִבְנוֹת עָלֶיהָ בִּנְיָן לִקְבִיעוּת:


רחבה כדי לקבל אריח – that it would be appropriate to build upon it a fixed, permanent building.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:3:2)


**וְהָאָרִיחַ חֲצִי לְבֵנָה שֶׁל שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים.** נִמְצָא אָרִיחַ טֶפַח וּמֶחֱצָה רֹחַב:


והאריח חצי לבנה של שלשה טפחים – it is found that the אריח/small bricks/bond-timber is a handbreadth and-a-half wide.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:3:3)


**דַּיָּה לַקּוֹרָה בְּרֹחַב טֶפַח.** הָכִי קָאָמַר, כֵּיוָן שֶׁהַקּוֹרָה רְחָבָה טֶפַח, אוֹתוֹ חֲצִי טֶפַח הַנּוֹתָר, שֶׁהָאָרִיחַ רָחָב טֶפַח וּמֶחֱצָה, יָכוֹל לִמְרֹחַ בְּטִיט אֶצְבַּע מִכָּאן וְאֶצְבַּע מִכָּאן, וְעַל יְדֵי כֵן תִּהְיֶה רְחָבָה לְקַבֵּל אָרִיחַ, וְהָכִי מְפָרֵשׁ לַהּ בַּגְּמָרָא:


דיה לקורה ברוחב טפח – this is how it should be read: since the crossbeam is a handbreadth wide, that one-half handbreadth that remains – since the small brick is one-and-one-half handbreadths wide, it is possible to rub a salve of plaster a finger [width] here and a finger [width] from there and through this it will be wide enough to hold the small bricks and this is how it is explained in the Gemara (Talmud Eruvin 14a).



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:4:1)


**בְּרִיאָה לְקַבֵּל אָרִיחַ.** חֲזָקָה לִסְבֹּל כֹּבֶד הָאָרִיחַ שֶׁלֹּא תִּשָּׁבֵר. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


בריאה לקבל אריח – strong [enough] to endure the weight of the small bricks that it wouldn’t break. And this is the Halakha.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:5:1)


**הָיְתָה שֶׁל קַשׁ וְשֶׁל קָנִים כוּ'.** רַבִּי יְהוּדָה קָאָמַר לַהּ, וְלֵית הִלְכְתָא כְּוָתֵיהּ:


היתה של קש ושל קנים – Rabbi Yehuda stated this but the Halakha is not according to him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:5:2)


**עֲקֻמָּה.** שֶׁאֵין אָרִיחַ יָכוֹל לָנוּחַ עָלֶיהָ, וְכֵן עֲגֻלָּה אֵין אָרִיחַ נָח בָּהּ:


עקומה – the small bricks are not able to rest upon it (when it is curved) and similarly when it is round, a small brick cannot rest upon it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:5:3)


**אִם יֵשׁ בְּהֶקֵּפָהּ.** כְּלוֹמַר וְאֵיזוֹ הִיא מִדַּת הָעֲגֻלָּה לְהַכְשִׁיר, כָּל שֶׁיֵּשׁ בְּהֶקֵּפָהּ שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים, שֶׁצְּרִיכָה חוּט שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים לְהַקִּיפָהּ סָבִיב, בְּיָדוּעַ שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ רֹחַב טֶפַח אִם תֵּחָלֵק, שֶׁכֵּן מָצִינוּ בְּיָם שֶׁעָשָׂה שְׁלֹמֹה (דברי הימים ב ד) עֶשֶׂר בָּאַמָּה מִשְּׂפָתוֹ אֶל שְׂפָתוֹ עָגֹל סָבִיב וְקַו שְׁלֹשִׁים בָּאַמָּה יִסֹּב אוֹתוֹ סָבִיב, אַלְמָא לְכָל אַמָּה רֹחַב בְּעִגּוּל אִיכָּא שָׁלֹשׁ אַמּוֹת הֶקֵּף:


**if it has in its circumference:** Which is to say, what is the round measurement required to render [the beam] proper? Anything that has in its circumference three handbreadths, which requires a string of three handbreadths to encircle it around, it is known that it has a width (diameter) of one handbreadth, if you divide [it]. As so do we find with the basin that Shlomo made (II Chronicles 4:2), "ten ells from its edge to its edge and five ells of height and a line of thirty ells encircling it around." Hence for every ell in width (diameter), there are three ells in circumference.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:6:1)


**לְחָיַיִם שֶׁאָמְרוּ.** לְחָיַיִם שֶׁנֶּחְלְקוּ בָּהֶן רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר וַחֲכָמִים, דִּלְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר תַּרְתֵּי בָּעִינַן וּלְרַבָּנָן חֲדָא, זֶהוּ שִׁעוּרָן:


לחיים שאמרו – the stakes that Rabbi Eliezer and the Sages disputed about; for according to Rabbi Eliezer, two are required, and the Rabbis require only one and this is its measurement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:6:2)


**גָּבְהָן עֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים.** כְּשִׁעוּר גֹּבַהּ הַמָּבוֹי, דְּאֵין גָּבְהוֹ פָּחוֹת מֵעֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים. וַאֲפִלּוּ הָיָה הַמָּבוֹי גָּבוֹהַּ הַרְבֵּה, דַּיּוֹ בְּלֶחִי גָּבוֹהַּ עֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים:


גבהן עשרה טפחים – like the measure of the height of he alley for its height cannot be less than ten handbreadths, and even if the alley was much higher, it is enough with a stake that is ten handbreadths high.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:6:3)


**וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר רָחְבָּן שְׁלֹשָׁה.** דְּבָעֵי דָּבָר חָשׁוּב בִּמְחִצָּה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


ורבי יוסי אומר רחבן שלשה – and Rabbi Yosi states that its width is three [handbreadths] for it requires something important as a partition, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yosi.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:7:1)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹסֵר.** דְּחָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא תָּמוּת וְלֹא הָוֵי גְּבוֹהָה עֲשָׂרָה וְלֹא יָהֵיב אַדַּעְתֵּיהּ וְסָמִיךְ עֲלַהּ:


ורבי יוסי פוסל – for we suspect lest it die, and it would not be ten [handbreadths] high, and doesn’t give his attention and relies upon it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:7:2)


**וּמְטַמֵּא מִשּׁוּם גּוֹלֵל.** אִם עֲשָׂאָן גּוֹלֵל לְקֶבֶר, מְטַמֵּא לְעוֹלָם אִם נָגַע בּוֹ אָדָם וְכֵלִים, כְּאֹהֶל הַמֵּת, אֲפִלּוּ נִטַּל מִשָּׁם, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יט) וְכָל אֲשֶׁר יִגַּע עַל פְּנֵי הַשָּׂדֶה, וְדָרְשִׁינַן לְרַבּוֹת גּוֹלֵל וְדוֹפֵק. גּוֹלֵל הוּא כִּסּוּי הַקֶּבֶר:


ומטמא משום גולל – if they made it a stone placed on top of a burial cave it defiles forever and if a person or utensils came in physical contact with it, it is like the tent of the dead, even if he was removed from there, as it is written (Numbers 19:16): “And in the open, anyone who touches a person [who was killed or who died naturally, or human bone, or a grave, shall be unclean seven days].” But the Rabbis expound upon it to include the stone placed on top of the burial cave and the frame supporting the movable stone of a tomb. The "גולל"/the stone placed on top of a burial cave is the covering of the grave.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:7:3)


**וְרַבִּי מֵאִיר מְטַהֵר.** טַעֲמָא דְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר מִשּׁוּם דְּקָסָבַר כָּל מְחִצָּה הָעוֹמֶדֶת בְּרוּחַ חַיִּים אֵינָהּ מְחִצָּה. וְאֵינָהּ הֲלָכָה:


ור"מ מטהר – the reason of Rabbi Meir is because he holds that any partition made of something living is not a partition, and it is not the Halakha.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:7:4)


**וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי הַגְּלִילִי פוֹסֵל.** דִּכְתִיב (דברים כד) וְכָתַב לָהּ סֵפֶר, מַה סֵּפֶר אֵין בּוֹ רוּחַ חַיִּים, אַף כֹּל שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ רוּחַ חַיִּים. וְרַבָּנָן אִי כְּתִיב וְכָתַב לָהּ בַּסֵּפֶר כִּדְקָאָמַרְתְּ, הַשְׁתָּא דִּכְתִיב וְכָתַב לָהּ סֵפֶר, לְסִפּוּר דְּבָרִים הוּא דְּאָתָא. וַהֲלָכָה כְּתַנָּא קַמָּא. [וְאִם כָּתַב] לָהּ גֵּט בְּקֶרֶן שֶׁל פָּרָה וְנָתַן לָהּ אֶת הַפָּרָה, דְּהַשְׁתָּא אֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ קְצִיצָה לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּכְתַּב, כָּשֵׁר. אֲבָל אִם לֹא נָתַן לָהּ אֶת הַפָּרָה אֶלָּא אֶת הַקֶּרֶן, כֵּיוָן שֶׁצָּרִיךְ קְצִיצָה, לֹא מִגַּרְשָׁה בֵּיהּ:


ור"י הגלילי פוסל – as it is written (Deuteronomy 24:1): “”and he writes her a bill [of divorcement, hands it to her, and sends her away from his house].” Just as a scroll lacks a living spirit/life, so too, anything that does not have a living spirit/life. And the Rabbis: If it (i.e., the Torah) had written “and he writes her in a bill,” it would be as you said, but now that it is written [in the Torah]: “and he writes her a bill” – it comes to count things, but the Halakha is according to the First Tanna/teacher, [and if he wrote] her a bill of divorce on the horn of the heifer, and he gave her the heifer, for now it does not require cutting after it had been written, it is valid. But, if he did not give her the heifer, but rather the horn, since it requires cutting, one does not divorce by it.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:8:1)


**וְלֹא יִהְיוּ פְרָצוֹת יְתֵרוֹת עַל הַבִּנְיָן.** וַאֲפִלּוּ הֵן פְּרָצוֹת קְטַנּוֹת פְּחוּתוֹת מֵעֶשֶׂר, אִם הַפָּרוּץ מְרֻבֶּה עַל הָעוֹמֵד אֵינָהּ מְחִצָּה, וְאַף כְּנֶגֶד הָעוֹמֵד אָסוּר:


ולא יהיה פרצות יתרות על הבנין – and even if they are small breaches/gaps, less than ten [handbreadths], if the breach/gap is greater than the standing part, it is not a partition, and even if it corresponds to the standing part, it is prohibited.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:8:2)


**כָּל פִּרְצָה שֶׁהִיא כְעֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת מֻתֶּרֶת.** וּבִלְבַד שֶׁיְּהֵא הָעוֹמֵד רַב עָלֶיהָ, אִי נַמִּי פָּרוּץ כְּעוֹמֵד הָוֵי מְחִצָּה וְאַף כְּנֶגֶד הַפָּרוּץ מֻתָּר:


כל פרצה שהיא בעשר אמות מותרת – as long as the standing part greater than it; alternatively, what is breached is like the standing part, it is a partition and even corresponding to the breach/gap, it is permitted.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:8:3)


**יָתֵר מִכָּאן אָסוּר.** אֲפִלּוּ פִּרְצָה אַחַת יוֹתֵר מֵעֶשֶׂר אוֹסֶרֶת כָּל הַהֶקֵּף, וַאֲפִלּוּ הָעוֹמֵד מְרֻבֶּה, דְּלֹא עָבְדֵי אֱינָשֵׁי פֶּתַח יוֹתֵר מֵעֶשֶׂר וְהָוֵי פִּרְצָה. וְדַוְקָא דְּאֵין לָהּ צוּרַת פֶּתַח, אֲבָל אִית לַהּ צוּרַת פֶּתַח אֲפִלּוּ יוֹתֵר מֵעֶשֶׂר הָוֵי פֶּתַח:


יתר מכאן אסור – even one breach/gap that is greater than ten [cubits] prohibits the entire circumference, and even if the standing part is greater than it, for people don’t make openings greater than ten [cubits] and it would be a breach, and especially if it lacks the form of an opening, but if it has the form of an opening, even greater than ten [cubits], it is an opening.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:9:1)


**מַקִּיפִין שְׁלֹשָׁה חֲבָלִים.** אַשְּׁיָרָא שֶׁחָנְתָה בַּבִּקְעָה קָאֵי:


מקיפין שלשה חבלים – it refers to a caravan that camped in the valley


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:9:2)


**זֶה לְמַעְלָה מִזֶּה.** עַל גַּבֵּי יְתֵדוֹת סָבִיב, מִן הַחֶבֶל הַתַּחְתּוֹן לַקַּרְקַע פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה, וַהֲרֵי כְּאִלּוּ כֻּלּוֹ עוֹמֵד, דְּכָל פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה כְּלָבוּד דָּמִי, וּמִמֶּנּוּ לָאֶמְצָעִי פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה, הֲרֵי עוֹמֵד שִׁשָּׁה טְפָחִים פָּחוֹת שְׁנֵי מַשֶּׁהוּיִין בִּשְׁנֵי הָאֲוִירִין, וּמֵאֶמְצָעִי לָעֶלְיוֹן פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה, הֲרֵי תִּשְׁעָה טְפָחִים עוֹמֵד בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה הָאֲוִירִין פָּחוֹת שְׁלֹשָׁה מַשֶּׁהוּיִין:


זה למעלה מזה – on top of pegs surrounding from the lowest rope to the ground less than three [handbreadths] and behold it is like all of it is standing, for everything less than three [handbreadths] is considered like a “labud” (something solid/compact)/a legal fiction of considering separate parts as united, if the gap is less than three handbreadths (see Talmud Sukkah 16b), and from it to the middle [rope] is less than three [handbreadths], you have it that it stands as six handbreadths less the two small amounts in the two airspaces, and from the middle [rope] to the upper [rope], which is less than three [handbreadths], you have nine handbreadths standing in three airspaces less than three small amounts.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:9:3)


**שִׁעוּר עֳבִי הַחֲבָלִים יוֹתֵר עַל טֶפַח.** שְׁלֹשָׁה מַשֶּׁהוּיִין, כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהֵא בֵּין הַכֹּל עֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים:


שיעור עובי החבלים יותר על טפח – three small amounts in orde that there can be between everything ten handbreadths.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:10:1)


**מַקִּיפִים בְּקָנִים.** נְעוּצִים וְעוֹמְדִים, וְזוֹ הִיא מְחִצַּת שְׁתִי, אֲבָל שֶׁל חֲבָלִים הַוְיָא מְחִצָּה שֶׁל עֵרֶב:


מקיפין בקנים – wedged in and standing and this is a partition of warp but of ropes it would be a partition of woof.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:10:2)


**בְּשַׁיָּרָא דִּבְּרוּ.** הֵקֵלּוּ אֶצְלָן שֶׁאֵין צְרִיכִים אֶלָּא אוֹ שְׁתִי כְּגוֹן קָנִים, אוֹ עֵרֶב כְּגוֹן חֲבָלִים, אֲבָל לְיָחִיד לֹא הֵקֵלּוּ אֶלָּא עַד שֶׁיִּהְיֶה בָּהּ שְׁתִי וָעֵרֶב. וּשְׁלֹשָׁה בְּנֵי אָדָם הֲרֵי הֵן כִּשְׁיָרָא:


בשיירא דברו – they were lenient regarding them since they don’t need anything other either a warp such as reeds or a woof, such as ropes, but for an individual, they were not lenient , but rather until it has [both] warp and woof and three people [in it], they are like a caravan.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:10:3)


**כָּל מְחִצָּה שֶׁאֵינָהּ שֶׁל שְׁתִי וְשֶׁל עֵרֶב כוּ'.** רַבִּי יוֹסֵי בְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה פָּלֵיג עֲלֵיהּ דַּאֲבוּהּ, וְקָאֲמַר אֲפִלּוּ לִשְׁיָרָא בָּעֵי שְׁתִי וָעֵרֶב:


כל מחיצה שאינה של שתי וערב כו' - Rabbi Yosi the son of Rabbi Yehuda disputes on that of his father, and states that even for a caravan, it requires warp and woof.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:10:4)


**אֶחָד מִשְּׁנֵי דְבָרִים.** אוֹ שְׁתִי אוֹ עֵרֶב. וְאִיכָּא בֵּין רַבָּנָן קַמָּאֵי לְרַבָּנָן בַּתְרָאֵי, דְּרַבָּנָן קַמָּאֵי לֹא שָׁרוּ לְיָחִיד אֶלָּא דּוּמְיָא דִּשְׁיָרָא, בַּדֶּרֶךְ, הוֹאִיל וְאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לַעֲשׂוֹת מְחִצָּה רְאוּיָה, וְלֹא בַּיִּשּׁוּב. וְרַבָּנָן בַּתְרָאֵי אָמְרֵי אֶחָד מִשְּׁנֵי דְּבָרִים, בֵּין לְיָחִיד בֵּין לְרַבִּים בֵּין בַּדֶּרֶךְ בֵּין בַּיִּשּׁוּב. וְהִלְכְתָא כְּוָתַיְהוּ. אֶלָּא שֶׁאָדָם אֶחָד אוֹ שְׁנַיִם שֶׁשָּׁבְתוּ בַּמִּדְבָּר אֵין יְכוֹלִים לַעֲשׂוֹת מְחִצָּה גְּדוֹלָה יוֹתֵר מִבֵּית סָאתַיִם שֶׁהוּא מֵאָה אַמָּה אֹרֶךְ עַל חֲמִשִּׁים רֹחַב כַּחֲצַר הַמִּשְׁכָּן, וּשְׁלֹשָׁה בְּנֵי אָדָם אוֹ יוֹתֵר הֵן שְׁיָרָא וִיכוֹלִים לַעֲשׂוֹת מְחִצָּה גְּדוֹלָה וַאֲפִלּוּ כַּמָּה מִילִין, כְּפִי מַה שֶּׁצָּרִיךְ לָהֶם וְלִכְלֵי תַּשְׁמִישָׁם וְלִבְהֶמְתָּם, וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא יִשָּׁאֵר מָקוֹם פָּנוּי בְּתוֹךְ הַמְּחִצָּה יוֹתֵר מִבֵּית סָאתַיִם:


אחד משני דברים – either warp or woof and there is [a difference] between the earlier Rabbis and the latter Rabbis, for the earlier Rabbis did not permit for an individual other than something similar to a caravan on the way, since it cannot make an appropriate partition and it is not in a settlement, but the latter Rabbis stated that one of two things, whether for an individual or a group, whether on the road or in a settlement, and the Halakha is according to them, but that one person or two that dwelled in the wilderness are not able to make a partition larger than field requiring two Se’ah of seed which is one-hundred cubits long by fifty cubits wide like the courtyard of the Tabernacle and three people or ore are a caravan and they are able to make a large partition and even several miles according what they need and for the utensils that they use and for their animals, as long as there not remain a free space within the partition greater than the field requiring two Se’ah of seed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:10:5)


**פָּטְרוּ בַמַּחֲנֶה.** לַיּוֹצְאִים לַמִּלְחָמָה, וַאֲפִלּוּ לְמִלְחֶמֶת הָרְשׁוּת:


פטרו במחנה – for those who go out to war and even to an optional war [to gain territory outside the Land of Israel].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:10:6)


**מְבִיאִין עֵצִים.** וְאֵין חוֹשְׁשִׁים מִשּׁוּם גָּזֵל, וַאֲפִלּוּ כָּרְתוּ אוֹתָם בַּעֲלֵיהֶם וַעֲשָׂאוּם חֲבִילוֹת:


מביאין עצים – and we don’t suspect them of theft, and even if their owners cut them and made of them packages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:10:7)


**וּפְטוּרִים מֵרְחִיצַת יָדַיִם.** שֶׁלִּפְנֵי הַסְּעוּדָה, אֲבָל בַּמַּיִם שֶׁלְּאַחַר הַסְּעוּדָה חַיָּבִים, הוֹאִיל וְנִתְקְנוּ מֵחֲמַת הַסַּכָּנָה שֶׁל מֶלַח סְדוֹמִית הַמְסַמֵּא אֶת הָעֵינַיִם, חֲמִירָא סַכַּנְתָּא מֵאִסּוּרָא:


ופטורים מרחיצת ידים – [exempt from washing the hands] before the meal, but for the water [for washing] after the meal, they are obligated, since it was established on account of the danger of the Sodomite salt that blinds the eye, the danger is graver than the prohibition.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:10:8)


**וּמִדְּמַאי.** שֶׁאֵין צְרִיכִין לְהַפְרִישׁ תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר שֶׁל דְּמַאי, כְּגוֹן אִם לָקְחוּ תְּבוּאָה מֵעַם הָאָרֶץ:


ומדמאי – that they don’t have to separate the tenth of a tenth of doubtfully tithed produce such as if they took grain from an ignoramus [who does not observe the laws of tithing properly].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 1:10:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:10:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/1:10:9)


**וּמִלְּעָרֵב.** עֵרוּבֵי חֲצֵרוֹת, כְּגוֹן מֵאֹהֶל לְאֹהֶל בְּמַחֲנֶה מֻקָּף מְחִצּוֹת. אֲבָל עֵרוּבֵי תְּחוּמִין צְרִיכִין לְעָרֵב:


ומלערב – The Eruvim of courtyards such as from tent to tent in the camp surrounded by partitions, but the Eruvim of borders/limits they are required to make [those Eruvim].



## Chapter 2


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:1:1)


**עוֹשִׂין פַּסִּין לַבֵּירָאוֹת.** שֶׁבִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים. לְפִי שֶׁהַבֵּירָאוֹת רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד הֵן שֶׁעֲמֻקִּים עֲשָׂרָה, וְאֵין יְכוֹלִים לְמַלְּאוֹת מֵהֶן וּלְהוֹצִיא לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וְעוֹשִׂין פַּסִּין שֶׁיִּהְיוּ הַפַּסִּין עוֹשִׂין [חָלָל] סְבִיבוֹת הַבּוֹר רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד, וִימַלֵּא וְיוֹצִיא וְיָנִיחַ שָׁם וְתִכָּנֵס בְּהֶמְתּוֹ כֻּלָּהּ אוֹ רֹאשָׁהּ וְרֻבָּהּ וְיַשְׁקֶנָּה:


עושים פסין לביראות – [watering stations (especially in the land of Israel for Temple pilgrims)] that are in the public domain because these watering stations/wells are the private domain, for they are deep ten [handbreadths] and one cannot fill up from them and take it out in to the public domain and they make boards that will make [an empty space] around the cistern which is the private domain and he will fill up and take out and leave there, and his animal can go in completely, or its head and most of its body and gave it to drink.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:1:2)


**דְּיוּמְדִין.** שְׁנֵי עַמּוּדִין. שֶׁכָּל עַמּוּד מֵאֵלּוּ הָאַרְבָּעָה נִרְאֶה כִּשְׁנַיִם. דְּיוּ בִּלְשׁוֹן יָוָן שְׁנַיִם. וּכְשֶׁנּוֹעֵץ הָעַמּוּד הֶעָשׂוּי כִּשְׁנַיִם לִפְאַת דְּרוֹמִית מַעֲרָבִית נִמְצָא נוֹטֶה דָּפְנוֹ אֶחָד לַמִּזְרָח וְדָפְנוֹ אֶחָד לַצָּפוֹן, וְהַשֵּׁנִי לִפְאַת מַעֲרָבִית צְפוֹנִית נוֹטֶה צִדּוֹ אֶחָד לַמִּזְרָח וְצִדּוֹ אֶחָד לַדָּרוֹם, וּכְשֶׁנּוֹתֵן אַרְבַּעְתָּן לְאַרְבַּע הַפֵּאוֹת נִמְצָא לְכָל רוּחַ שְׁתֵּי אַמּוֹת דֹּפֶן אַמָּה כְּנֶגֶד אַמָּה וְהָרֶוַח בֵּינְתַיִם:


דיומדין – two pillars where each pillar from these four appears as two. "דיו" in the Greek language is “two.” But when one inserts the pillars that is made like two for the southwestern corner, it is found that one wall inclines towards the east, and one wall to the north and the second to the northwestern corner leans one side to the east and the other side to the south, and when you place all four of them in the four corners, it is found that direction is two cubits – one wall corresponding to a cubit and the space between them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:1:3)


**וְאַרְבָּעָה פְשׁוּטִין.** בָּאֶמְצַע נוֹתֵן לְכָל רוּחַ לוּחַ רְחָבָה אַמָּה. וּבִזְמַן שֶׁאֵין בֵּין פַּס לְפַס מֵאַרְבָּעָה דְּיוּמְדִין [אֵלּוּ] אֶלָּא עֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת אוֹ פָּחוֹת מוֹדֶה רַבִּי מֵאִיר דְּאֵין צָרִיךְ לְפַסִּין פְּשׁוּטִין, וְאִם יֵשׁ בֵּינֵיהֶן יוֹתֵר מִשְּׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה אַמָּה וּשְׁלִישׁ מוֹדֶה רַבִּי יְהוּדָה דְּצָרִיךְ פְּשׁוּטִין, לֹא נֶחְלְקוּ אֶלָּא מֵעֶשֶׂר עַד שְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה אַמָּה וּשְׁלִישׁ, דְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר אוֹמֵר צָרִיךְ פְּשׁוּטִים וְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר אֵין צָרִיךְ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


וארבעה פשוטים – in the middle, he places in each direction a board that is a cubit wide, and at the time when there isn’t between each board from these four corner-pieces made of rectangularly joined or of a block dug out In the shape of a trough, four of which corner-pieces form, in legal fiction, an enclosure of wells (see Talmud Eruvin 19a), but rather ten cubs or less, Rabbi Meir admits that there is no need for these plain boards, but if there is more than thirteen and one-third cubits between them, Rabbi Yehuda admits that they need plain boards; they do not dispute other than from ten until thirteen and one-third cubits as Rabbi Meir states that they require plain boards and Rabbi Yehuda states that they don’t, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:1:4)


**שְׁתֵּי רְבָקוֹת שֶׁל שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁלֹשׁ בָּקָר.** שֶׁרֹחַב כָּל בָּקָר וּבָקָר אַמָּתַיִם פָּחוֹת שְׁלִישׁ, נִמְצָא רֹחַב שִׁשָּׁה בָּקָר עֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת, וְזֶה הָרֶוַח שֶׁיָּכוֹל לִתֵּן בֵּין פַּס לְפַס לְדִבְרֵי רַבִּי מֵאִיר, וְאִם יֵשׁ רֹחַב יוֹתֵר מִזֶּה צָרִיךְ שֶׁיַּרְבֶּה בְּפַסִּין פְּשׁוּטִין:


שתי רבקות של שלש שלש בקר – for the width of each cattle is two cubits minus one-third, it is found that that the width of six cattle is ten cubits and that is the space that one can place between each and every board, according to Rabbi Meir. But if the width is greater than this, he needs to increase the number of plain boards.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:1:5)


**רְבָקוֹת.** לְשׁוֹן עֵגֶל מַרְבֵּק (שמואל א כח):


רבקות – from the language (I Samuel 28:24): “[The woman had] a stall-fed calf [in the house].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:1:6)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר שֶׁל אַרְבַּע אַרְבַּע.** הֵן שְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה אַמָּה וּשְׁלִישׁ:


ר"י אומר של ארבע ארבע – they are thirteen and one-third cubits.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:1:7)


**קְשׁוּרוֹת אֲבָל לֹא מֻתָּרוֹת.** דְּלֹא תֵּימָא כְּעֵין קְשׁוּרוֹת וְלֹא קְשׁוּרוֹת מַמָּשׁ, לְהָכִי תָּנָא אֲבָל לֹא מֻתָּרוֹת, לְהַחְמִיר וּלְמַעֵט רֶוַח שֶׁבֵּינְתַיִם:


קשורות אבל לא מותרות – that you should not say that they are sort of tied and not actually tied, for that reason it was taught in the Mishnah, “"אבל לא מותרות/but not slack or loosely to be stringent and to reduce the space that is between them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:1:8)


**אַחַת נִכְנֶסֶת וְאַחַת יוֹצֵאת.** רְבָקָה אַחַת שֶׁל בָּקָר נִכְנֶסֶת וּרְבָקָה אַחַת יוֹצֵאת, וְיֵשׁ יוֹתֵר רֹחַב מִשֶּׁאִלּוּ הָיוּ שְׁתֵּיהֶן יוֹצְאוֹת אוֹ שְׁתֵּיהֶן נִכְנָסוֹת. וְזוֹ קֻלָּא הִיא, דְּלֹא בָּעִינַן מְצֻמְצָמוֹת כָּל כָּךְ:


אחת נכנסת ואחת יוצאה – one team of cattle entering and one team leaving and there is greater width than if both of them would have been going out or both of them leaving and this is a leniency since we don’t require to be so squeezed in.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:2:1)


**מֻתָּר לְהַקְרִיב לַבְּאֵר.** הַפַּסִּין, וְלַעֲשׂוֹת הֶקֵּף קָצָר. וּבִלְבַד שֶׁיְּהֵא מִשְּׂפַת הַבְּאֵר עַד בֵּין הַפַּסִּין כְּדֵי רֹאשָׁהּ וְרֻבָּהּ שֶׁל פָּרָה שֶׁהֵן שְׁתֵּי אַמּוֹת, אֲבָל בָּצִיר מֵהָכִי לֹא, שֶׁמָּא יִמָּשֵׁךְ אַחַר פָּרָתוֹ וְיוֹצִיא הַדְּלִי חוּץ לַמְּחִצָּה:


מותר להקריב לבאר – the boards and to make a short circumference as long as there would be from the rim of the well until between the boards in order that the head and most of the body of the cow which is two cubits but not less than this, lest he be drawn after his cow and remove the bucket outside of the partition.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:2:2)


**מֻתָּר לְהַרְחִיק כָּל שֶׁהוּא.** לַעֲשׂוֹת הֶקֵּף גָּדוֹל כְּמוֹ שֶׁלִּבּוֹ חָפֵץ:


מותר להרחיק כל שהוא – to make a larger partition like what his heard desires.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:2:3)


**וּבִלְבַד שֶׁיַּרְבֶּה בְּפַסִּין.** דְּכָל מַה שֶּׁמַּרְחִיק מִן הַבּוֹר מַרְבֶּה בָּרֶוַח שֶׁבֵּין הַפַּסִּין, וְצָרִיךְ לְהַרְבּוֹת בְּפַסִּין עַד כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא בֵּין פָּשׁוּט לְפָשׁוּט [וּבֵין פָּשׁוּט לִדְיוּמֶד] יוֹתֵר מִשְּׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה אַמָּה וּשְׁלִישׁ, כְּדִבְרֵי רַבִּי יְהוּדָה שֶׁהֲלָכָה כְּמוֹתוֹ:


ובלבד שירבה בפסין – as long as he increases the boars, for all while he distances himself from the cistern, the space increases between the boards and he needs to increase the boards so that there won’t be between each and every plain board [and between a plain board and a corner-piece more than thirteen and one-third cubits according to the words of Rabbi Yehuda, and the Halakha is according to him.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:3:1)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר עַד בֵּית סָאתָיִם.** לֹא יַעֲשֶׂה הֶקֵּף גָּדוֹל לַבְּאֵר אֶלָּא עַד בֵּית סָאתַיִם:


רבי יהודה אומר עד בית סאתים – He should not make a large partition to the cistern other than up to a space of two Se’ahs (i.e., a Bet Se’ah is 2500 square cubits; two S’eah is 5000 square cubits).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:3:2)


**לְגִנָּה וּלְקַרְפֵּף.** שֶׁאֵין הֶקֵּפָן לְדִירָה:


לגנה ולקרפף – that their partitions are not for a living.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:3:3)


**קַרְפֵּף.** הֶקֵּף גָּדוֹל חוּץ לָעִיר לְהַכְנִיס שָׁם עֵצִים לְאוֹצָר:


קרפף – a large partition outside of the city to place in there wood for storage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:3:4)


**דִּיר.** שֶׁל בְּהֵמוֹת שֶׁעוֹשִׂין בַּשָּׂדוֹת הַיּוֹם כָּאן וּלְמָחָר כָּאן כְּדֵי לְזַבְּלָהּ בִּגְלָלֵי בְּהֵמָה:


דיר – of cattle that they make in the fields, today on one side, and on the morrow on the other side, to fertilize it with the manure of the cattle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:3:5)


**סַחַר.** [הֶקֵּף מְחִצָּה] לַבְּהֵמוֹת בָּעִיר. וְאִית דְּגָרְסֵי סַהַר, בֵּית הָאֲסוּרִים:


סחר – for cattle in the city and there are those that have the reading "סהר" – a prison.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:3:6)


**חָצֵר.** מָקוֹם מְגֻלֶּה שֶׁלִּפְנֵי הַבָּתִּים:


חצר – an open area in front of the houses.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:3:7)


**מֻקְצֶה.** רְחָבָה שֶׁאֲחוֹרֵי הַבָּתִּים, דְּכָל הָנֵי הֶקֵּפָן לְדִירָה הוּא. וְהָנֵי פַּסֵּי בֵּירָאוֹת נַמִּי הוֹאִיל וּמֵימֵיהֶן רְאוּיוֹת לִשְׁתִיַּת אָדָם תַּשְׁמִישׁ מְעַלְּיוּתָא הוּא:


מוקצה – a wide area in back of the houses and all of these surround the human dwelling place and these boards of the watering stations also, since their waters are appropriate for drinking by humans, it is a higher level of usage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:3:8)


**וּמֻתָּר לְהַרְחִיק כָּל שֶׁהוּא.** כְּלוֹמַר כְּשֵׁם שֶׁכָּל אֵלּוּ שֶׁהֻקְּפוּ לְדִירָה אֲפִלּוּ גְּדוֹלִים עַד עֶשֶׂר כּוֹרִים מֻתָּר לְטַלְטֵל בָּהֶם, כָּךְ מֻתָּר לְהַרְחִיק הַהֶקֵּף שֶׁל פַּסֵּי בֵּירָאוֹת כָּל מַה שֶּׁלִּבּוֹ חָפֵץ, וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


ומותר להרחיק כל שהוא – meaning to say that all of these that were surrounding living space, even large ones up to ten Kor, it is permissible to carry in them , so too, it is permissible to distance the circumference of the boards of the watering stations all that his heart desires, and such is the Halakha [as long as they add enough boards so that the space between two boards does not exceed thirteen and one-third cubits in every case].



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:4:1)


**אִם הָיְתָה דֶרֶךְ הָרַבִּים.** דֶּרֶךְ שֶׁרַבִּים בּוֹקְעִים בָּהּ מַפְסֶקֶת בֵּין הַפַּסִּים, יְסַלְקֶנָּה לַצְּדָדִים מִחוּץ לַפַּסִּים, שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיוּ הָרַבִּים מְהַלְּכִים בֵּין הַפַּסִּים, דְּמַשְׁווּ לַהּ כִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים וּמְבַטְּלִים הַמְּחִצָּה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


אם היתה דרך הרבים – the path that the public crosses through passes between the boards, he should divert it to the sides outside of the boards, so that the masses would not walk between the boards because they consider it like the public domain and annul the partition , but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:4:2)


**בּוֹר הָרַבִּים.** אִי פָּסְקֵי מַיָּא מַדְכְּרֵי אַהֲדָדֵי, שֶׁלֹּא הִתִּירוּ פַּסֵּי בֵּירָאוֹת אֶלָּא שֶׁיִּהְיוּ מַיִם מְצוּיִין לִבְהֵמוֹת עוֹלֵי רְגָלִים, וְכָל זְמַן שֶׁאֵין שָׁם מַיִם אֵין לַפַּסִּין תּוֹרַת מְחִצָּה:


בור הרבים – if the water ceases they remind each other that they did not permit boards for watering stations other than if water would be available for animals, pilgrims ascending [to the Temple] on Jewish holy days and whenever there is no water there, the boards do not have the status of a partition.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:4:3)


**בְּאֵר הַיָּחִיד.** נַמִּי שָׁרֵי, דִּבְאֵר מַיִם חַיִּים הֵן וְהָא לֹא פָּסְקֵי מַיָּא:


באר היחיד – also permitted for these are a well of living waters and the water did not cease.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:4:4)


**לִבְאֵר הָרַבִּים.** דְּאִיכָּא תַּרְתֵּי לִמְעַלִּיּוּתָא. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה. וְאֵין מֻתָּר לְמַלְאוֹת מַיִם וּלְהוֹצִיא מִן הַבְּאֵר עַל יְדֵי פַּסֵּי בֵּירָאוֹת אֶלָּא כְּדֵי לְהַשְׁקוֹת בְּהֶמְתָּן שֶׁל עוֹלֵי רְגָלִים וּבְאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל בִּלְבַד. אֲבָל לְהוֹצִיא מַיִם כְּדֵי לִשְׁתּוֹת הָאָדָם עַצְמוֹ אָסוּר, אֶלָּא יוֹרֵד לַבּוֹר וְשׁוֹתֶה, אוֹ עוֹשֶׂה מְחִצָּה גְּבוֹהָה עֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים:


לבאר הרבים – for there are two levels of perfection, and this is the Halakha, for it is not permitted to fill water or to remove from the well through the boards of watering-stations other than in order to provide water for cattle of Temple pilgrims, and in the Land of Israel only, but to remove water in order for a person himself to drink is prohibit, but he can go down to the cistern and drink or make a partition ten handbreadths high.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:5:1)


**וְעוֹד אָמַר רַבִּי יְהוּדָה בֶן בָּבָא.** מִשּׁוּם דְּאָמַר חֲדָא לְחֻמְרָא גַּבֵּי פַּסִּין, דְּאָמַר אֵין עוֹשִׂין אוֹתָן אֶלָּא לִבְאֵר הָרַבִּים, וְהַשְׁתָּא קָאָמַר חֻמְרָא אַחֲרִיתָא דְּאַף בְּמֻקָּף לְדִירָה לֹא שָׁרֵי טְפֵי מִבֵּית סָאתַיִם, מִשּׁוּם הָכִי תָּנָא וְעוֹד:


ועוד א"ר יהודה בן בבא – because he said one stringent thing regarding boards when he said that we don’t make them other than for a public well, and now he stated another stringency, for even if surrounding the dwelling, they did not permit more than from two Se’ah’s space (i.e., 70 2/3 cubits square); because of this it (i.e., the Mishnah) teaches "ועוד" /”and furthermore.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:5:2)


**שׁוֹמֵירָה.** סֻכַּת שׁוֹמְרִים. דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּהֻקַּף לְדִירָה, בֵּית סָאתַיִם הוּא דְּשָׁרֵי, טְפֵי לֹא:


שומירה – a booth/shed for watchmen and even though it surrounds the dwelling it is two Se’ah in size which is permitted, but not more.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:5:3)


**סְמוּכָה לָעִיר.** דְּהוֹאִיל וּקְרוֹבָה לְבֵיתוֹ דַּעְתּוֹ לְהִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בָּהּ תָּמִיד וּכְמֻקָּף לְדִירָה דָּמִי:


סמוכה לעיר – for since it is near to his house, his intention is to use it always and is like it surrounding the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:5:4)


**וּבִלְבַד שֶׁתְּהֵא שִׁבְעִים אַמָּה וְשִׁירַיִם עַל שִׁבְעִים אַמָּה וְשִׁירָיִם.** וְלֹא יוֹתֵר. וְתַנָּא קַמָּא נַמִּי דִּלְעֵיל דְּאָמְרוּ לוֹ לֹא אָמְרוּ בֵּית סָאתַיִם אֶלָּא לַקַּרְפֵּף וְלַגִּנָּה אֲבָל אִם הָיָה דִּיר אוֹ סַהַר אוֹ חָצֵר וְכוּ', כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא קָאֲמַר, דְּהֵיכָא דְּאִיכָּא בֵּית דִּירָה אֲפִלּוּ טוּבָא נַמִּי, וּכְשֶׁלֹּא הֻקַּף לְדִירָה בֵּית סָאתַיִם. וּמַאי בֵּינַיְהוּ, אָמְרִינַן בַּגְּמָרָא דְּאִיכָּא בֵּינַיְהוּ דָּבָר מֻעָט, שֶׁבֵּית סָאתַיִם יוֹתֵר עַל שִׁבְעִים אַמָּה וְשִׁירַיִם רְבוּעִים, וְתַנָּא קַמָּא שָׁרֵי בְּבֵית סָאתַיִם שְׁלֵמִים, וּלְרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא שִׁבְעִים אַמָּה וְשִׁירַיִם עַל שִׁבְעִים אַמָּה וְשִׁירַיִם וְתוּ לֹא. וְכֵיצַד יָדַעְנוּ דְּבֵית סָאתַיִם יוֹתֵר עַל שִׁבְעִים אַמָּה וְשִׁירַיִם רְבוּעִים, דְּהָא אָמְרִינַן בַּגְּמָרָא וְכַמָּה הֵן סָאתַיִם, כַּחֲצַר הַמִּשְׁכָּן, וַחֲצַר הַמִּשְׁכָּן כְּתִיב בֵּיהּ (שמות כז) אֹרֶךְ הֶחָצֵר מֵאָה בָּאַמָּה וְרֹחַב חֲמִשִּׁים בַּחֲמִשִּׁים, וְדָרְשִׁינַן מַאי חֲמִשִּׁים בַּחֲמִשִּׁים, אָמְרָה תּוֹרָה טֹל חֲמִשִּׁים שֶׁאָרְכּוֹ יָתֵר עַל רָחְבּוֹ וְסַבֵּב חֲמִשִּׁים הַנּוֹתָרִים לְשִׁעוּר שַׁבָּת, דַּהֲווּ לְהוּ שִׁבְעִים אַמָּה וְאַרְבָּעָה טְפָחִים מְרֻבָּעִים. כֵּיצַד, עֲשֵׂה מֵהֶם חָמֵשׁ רְצוּעוֹת שֶׁל עֶשֶׂר אַמָּה רֹחַב וְאָרְכָּן חֲמִשִּׁים, תֵּן אַחַת לְמִזְרָחָהּ וְאַחַת לְמַעֲרָבָהּ, הֲרֵי רָחְבָּהּ שִׁבְעִים וְאָרְכָּהּ חֲמִשִּׁים, שִׂים אַחַת לַדָּרוֹם וְאַחַת לַצָּפוֹן, הֲרֵי שִׁבְעִים עַל שִׁבְעִים, אֶלָּא שֶׁהַקְּרָנוֹת פְּגוּמִים לְכָל קֶרֶן וְקֶרֶן עֶשֶׂר עַל עֶשֶׂר מִפְּנֵי הַתּוֹסֶפֶת שֶׁהוֹסַפְתָּ. טֹל מִן הָרְצוּעָה הַחֲמִשִּׁית אַרְבַּע חֲתִיכוֹת שֶׁל עֶשֶׂר [עַל עֶשֶׂר] וְשִׂים לְאַרְבַּע קְרָנוֹת, וְנִתְמַלְּאוּ. טֹל עֶשֶׂר עַל עֶשֶׂר הַנּוֹתָרִים שֶׁהֵם שִׁשִּׁים טְפָחִים [עַל שִׁשִּׁים טְפָחִים] וַעֲשֵׂה אוֹתָם רְצוּעוֹת שֶׁל שְׁנֵי טְפָחִים, הֲרֵי שְׁלֹשִׁים רְצוּעוֹת אֹרֶךְ כָּל אַחַת עֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת, שֶׁהֵן שְׁלֹשׁ מְאוֹת אַמָּה. תֵּן שִׁבְעִים לְכָל רוּחַ, הֲרֵי שִׁבְעִים [אַמּוֹת] וְאַרְבָּעָה טְפָחִים עַל שִׁבְעִים אַמּוֹת וְאַרְבָּעָה טְפָחִים, אֶלָּא שֶׁהַקְּרָנוֹת פְּגוּמִים טְפָחַיִם עַל טְפָחַיִם, וְנִשְׁאֲרוּ בְּיָדְךָ עֶשְׂרִים אַמָּה, טֹל מֵהֶם שְׁמֹנָה טְפָחִים וְשִׂים לַקְּרָנוֹת וְנִתְמַלְּאוּ, וְנִשְׁאֲרוּ בְּיָדְךָ שְׁמֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה אַמּוֹת וְאַרְבָּעָה טְפָחִים אֹרֶךְ בְּרֹחַב טְפָחַיִם, וְהַיְנוּ דָּבָר מֻעָט, שֶׁאִם בָּאתָ לְחַלְּקָן וּלְהַקִּיף אֵין מַגִּיעַ תּוֹסֶפֶת לְרֹחַב שְׁנֵי שְׁלִישֵׁי אֶצְבַּע, דְּהָא בָּעֵי לְמֶעְבַּד מִנָּהּ רְצוּעוֹת שֶׁל רפ"ג [אַמּוֹת] אֹרֶךְ לְהַקִּיף הָאַרְבַּע רוּחוֹת. כָּךְ מָצָאתִי פֵּרוּשׁ שִׁיטָה זוֹ בְּפֵרוּשֵׁי רַשִׁ"י, וְנָכוֹן הוּא. וְהָרַמְבַּ"ם בִּקֵּשׁ חֶשְׁבּוֹנוֹת רַבִּים וְלֹא יָרַדְתִּי לְסוֹף דַּעְתּוֹ. וּבַמֶּה שֶׁאָמַר רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אֲפִלּוּ אֵין בָּהּ אֶחָד מֵאֵלּוּ מְטַלְטְלִים בְּתוֹכָהּ, הִלְכְתָא כְּוָתֵיהּ. אֲבָל בְּמַאי דְּפָלֵיג אַחֲכָמִים דְּאָמְרוּ לוֹ דִּלְעֵיל וְסָבַר דְּשִׁבְעִים אַמָּה וְשִׁירַיִם עַל שִׁבְעִים אַמָּה וְשִׁירַיִם וְתוּ לֹא, בְּהָא הֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים. דִּבְבֵית סָאתַיִם כַּחֲצַר הַמִּשְׁכָּן שָׁרֵי, שֶׁהוּא דָּבָר מֻעָט יוֹתֵר עַל שִׁבְעִים אַמָּה וְשִׁירַיִם רְבוּעִים:


ובלבד שתהא שבעים ושירים על שבעים ושירים – but not more, for the First Tanna/teacher also, above (Chapter 2, Mishnah 3), that they (i.e., the Rabbis) said to him: they did not state two Se’ah other than for a garden and an enclosed space (of seventy square cubits) outside of a town or a settlement, but if it was a shed (for cattle)/store-house or an enclosure (for cattle) or a courtyard, etc., it is according to Rabbi Akiva who stated it, for where there is a dwelling place, and even many of them also, and it is not surrounded by two Se’ah for a dwelling. And what is the difference between them? We stated in the Gemara (Talmud Eruvin 23b) that there is a small amount of difference between them, for two Se’ah is larger than seventy cubits and a fraction (i.e. 70 2/3 cubits) square for the first Tanna/teacher permits two complete Se’ah and Rabbi Akiva seventy cubits and a fraction by seventy cubits and a fraction (i.e. 70 2/3 cubits but no further. But how did we know that two Se’ah is more than seventy cuits and a fraction squared, for we said in the Gemara (Talmud Eruvin 23b) and how much are the two Se’ah? Like the courtyard of the Tabernacle/Mishkan and it is written about the courtyard of the Tabernacle (Exodus 27:18): “The length of the enclosure shall be a hundred cubits, and the width fifty throughout.” And we expound: What is "חמשים בחמשים" /”fifty throughout?” The Torah stated: Take fifty whose length is greater than its width and surround the fifty remaining to the measure of Shabbat which would be for them seventy cubits and four handbreadths square. How so? He made from them five strips of ten cubits wide and their length was fifty. Take one to the east and one to the west, its width is seventy and its length is fifty. Place one to the south and one to the north, one has seventy [cubits] by seventy [cubits], but the corners are impaired for each corner by ten by ten because of the addition that had been added. Take from the fifth strip four pieces of ten apiece and place them on the four corners and they will be filled. Take ten [cubits] by ten [cubits] that are remaining which are sixty handbreadths [by sixty handbreadths] and make of them strips of two handbreadths – that makes thirty strips the length of each one is ten cubits which are three-hundred cubits, put seventy in each direction that makes seventy [cubits] and four handbreadths by seventy cubits and four handbreadths, but the corners are impaired by two handbreadths by two handbreadths. And there remained in your hand twenty cubits: take from them eight handbreadths and place them on the corners and they will be filled, and there will remain in your hand eighteen cubits and four handbreadths length with the width of two handbreadths, and that is a small mount, and if you came to divide them and to surround [it], it does not reach the addition for the width two-thirds fingers for you have to make of it a strap of two hundred and eighty-three [cubits] length to encompass the four directions. Such I have found in the explanation of this approach in the commentary of Rashi, and it is correct. But Maimonides requested many calculations and I did not go down to the end of this knowledge and in what Rabbi Akiva said: Even if there isn’t even one of these things we carry in it, the Halakha is according to him, but what he is in dispute with the Sages and they said to him that above and he holds that seventy cubits and a fraction by seventy cubits and a fraction and no more – in this, the Halakha is according to the Sages. For the two Se’ah is like the courtyard of the Tabernacle is permitted for it is a small amount more than seventy cubits and a fraction squared.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:5:5)


**אִם הָיְתָה אָרְכָּהּ יוֹתֵר עַל רָחְבָּהּ.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּמִעֵט רָחְבָּהּ וְהוֹסִיף עַל אָרְכָּהּ וְאֵין בְּכֻלָּהּ אֶלָּא בֵּית סָאתַיִם, אֵין מְטַלְטְלִין, דִּמְרֻבָּע הוּא דְּשָׁרוּ רַבָּנָן הֵיכָא דְּלֹא הֻקַּף לְדִירָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי דְּפָלֵיג אַדְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר וְלֹא בָּעֵי מְרֻבָּעוֹת:


אם היתה ארכה יותר כעל רחבה – and even though he reduced its width and added on to its length, there isn’t in all of it other than two-Seah – we don’t carry for it is something squared that the Rabbis permitted where it is not surrounded for living. But the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yosi who disputes with Rabbi Eliezer and does not required squared.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:6:1)


**אֲפִלּוּ הִיא כְּבֵית כּוֹר.** אַפְּלֻגְתָּא דִּלְעֵיל קָאֵי אַגִּנָּה וְקַרְפֵּף:


אפילו היא כבית כור – on the dispute of above this refers regarding on a garden and an enclosure (see Mishnah three of this chapter).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:6:2)


**אַנְשֵׁי חָצֵר שֶׁשָּׁכַח אַחַד מֵהֶם וְלֹא עֵרֵב.** וּלְמָחָר בִּטֵּל רְשׁוּת חֲצֵרוֹ לַחֲבֵרָיו, שֶׁהָיָה אוֹסֵר עֲלֵיהֶן מִלְּהוֹצִיא מִבָּתֵּיהֶן לֶחָצֵר, שֶׁהֶחָצֵר בִּרְשׁוּת כֻּלָּן וְחֶלְקוֹ אוֹסֵר עָלֶיהָ. הוֹאִיל וּבִטֵּל רְשׁוּת חֲצֵרוֹ בִּטֵּל אַף רְשׁוּת בֵּיתוֹ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא פֵּרֵשׁ, וְנַעֲשָׂה אוֹרֵחַ אֶצְלָם, לְפִיכָךְ אַף בֵּיתוֹ מֻתָּר לָהֶם. אֲבָל אָסוּר לוֹ לְהַכְנִיס וּלְהוֹצִיא מִבֵּיתוֹ לֶחָצֵר, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא מֻתָּר לְהַכְנִיס וּלְהוֹצִיא מִבָּתֵּיהֶם לֶחָצֵר כִּשְׁאָר כָּל אָדָם הַנִּכְנָס לְבֵית חֲבֵרוֹ שֶׁמֻּתָּר לוֹ לְהוֹצִיא מִבֵּית חֲבֵרוֹ לֶחָצֵר, דְּהָא רְשׁוּתָא דְּחַד הוּא, מִכָּל מָקוֹם מִבֵּיתוֹ אָסוּר הוּא לְהוֹצִיא. וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן כֵּיוָן דְּבִטֵּל לְהוּ רְשׁוּת בֵּיתוֹ הֲרֵי בֵּיתוֹ כְּבָתֵּיהֶם, דִּבְשָׁעָה שֶׁמּוֹצִיא מִבֵּיתוֹ לֶחָצֵר חוֹזֵר וּמַחֲזִיק בִּרְשׁוּתוֹ הוּא וְאוֹסֵר עֲלֵיהֶן, כְּדִתְנַן בְּהַדָּר עִם הָעוֹבֵד כּוֹכָבִים, מִי שֶׁנָּתַן רְשׁוּתוֹ וְחָזַר וְהוֹצִיא בֵּין בְּשׁוֹגֵג בֵּין בְּמֵזִיד הֲרֵי זֶה אוֹסֵר:


אנשי חצר ששכח אחד מהם ולא עירב – and on the morrow, the domain of his court is nullified to his fellows for he has prohibited upon them to be able to remove from their homes to the courtyard, for the courtyard was in the domain of all of them and they his portion forbids upon them. Since the domain of is courtyard is nullified, even the domain of his home is nullified even though he did not specify this and he became a guest with them. Therefore, even his home is permitted to them, but forbidden to him to bring in and take out from his home to the courtyard, even though he is permitted to bring in and take out from their homes to the courtyard like all other people who enter the house of their fellow, that it is permitted to him to take out from the house of his fellow into the courtyard, for it is it is domain of one. Nevertheless, from his own house it is prohibited for him to take out, and we don’t say since he nullified for them the domain of his house, for his house is like their homes, that at the time that he takes something out from his house into the courtyard, he can go back and take hold of his domain, but he prohibits it upon them, as it is taught in the Mishnah (see Tractate Eruvin, Chapter 6, Mishnah 3), that a person who lives with idolaters, he who gave his domain and went back and removed something whether inadvertently or on purpose, he prohibits [his home to others].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:6:3)


**שֶׁיּוֹצְאִין בְּעַקְרַבְנִין בְּפֶסַח.** מִשּׁוּם מָרוֹר. וְעַקְרַבְנִין יֶרֶק שֶׁעָלִין שֶׁלּוֹ דּוֹמוֹת לְעַקְרָב. וַאֲנִי שָׁמַעְתִּי סִיב הַגָּדֵל סְבִיבוֹת הַדֶּקֶל:


שיוצאים בעקרבנין בפסח – because of Maror. And palm-ivy/prickly creepers on palm-trees is a green/herb whose leaves are similar to a scorpion, but I heard it is a fibrous substance/bast of a palm tree, that grows around the palm tree.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 2:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/2:6:4)


**וּבִקַּשְׁתִּי לִי חָבֵר.** לִשְׁלֹשָׁה דְּבָרִים הַלָּלוּ אִם שָׁמַע מִפִּיו, וְלֹא מָצָאתִי. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּאֶחָד מֵהֶן. דְּגַבֵּי גִּנָּה וְקַרְפֵּף לֹא שָׁרֵי טְפֵי מִבֵּית סָאתַיִם. וְאַנְשֵׁי חָצֵר שֶׁשָּׁכַח אֶחָד מֵהֶן וְלֹא עֵרֵב וּבִטֵּל רְשׁוּתוֹ וְלֹא בִּטֵּל בֵּיתוֹ, אַף לָהֶם אָסוּר בֵּיתוֹ, אֲבָל מִבָּתֵּיהֶם לֶחָצֵר מֻתָּר. וְעַקְרַבְנִין לָאו מָרוֹר הוּא וְאֵין אָדָם יוֹצֵא בּוֹ יְדֵי חוֹבָתוֹ בְּפֶסַח:


ובקשתי לי חבר – for these three things – if he heard from his mouth, and I di d not fine it, and the Halakha is not like one of them concerning a garden and an enclosure it is not permitted more than two-Seah/Bet Se’ahtayim. And the people of a courtyard where one of them forgot and did not make an Eruv in his domain, and did not annul his house, even to them, his house is forbidden, but from their homes to the courtyard is permitted. And the palm-ivy/prickly creepers on palm-trees are not Maror, and a person does not fulfill his religious obligation with them on Passover.



## Chapter 3


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:1:1)


**בַּכֹּל מְעָרְבִין.** עֵרוּבֵי תְּחוּמִין. דְּאִלּוּ עֵרוּבֵי חֲצֵרוֹת אֵין מְעָרְבִין אֶלָּא בַּפַּת. וְהַאי בַּכֹּל דִּתְנַן בְּמַתְנִיתִין, לָאו דַּוְקָא, כִּדְאָמְרִינַן בַּגְּמָרָא אֵין לְמֵדִין מִן הַכְּלָלוֹת אֲפִלּוּ בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בָּהֶן חוּץ, דְּהָא הָכָא תְּנַן בַּכֹּל מְעָרְבִין חוּץ מִן הַמַּיִם וּמִן הַמֶּלַח, וְאִיכָּא נַמִּי כְּמֵהִין וּפִטְרִיּוֹת דְּאֵין מְעָרְבִין וְלֹא מִשְׁתַּתְּפִים בָּהֶם, דְּלָאו מָזוֹן נִינְהוּ, כְּמוֹ מַיִם וּמֶלַח:


בכל מערבין – the joining of borders/עירובי תחומין (i.e., placing a small amount of food somewhere within the two-thousand cubit limit, establishing that location as one’s residence for Shabbat, and the two thousand cubits are measured from there), whereas the joining of courtyards/עירובי חצרות – is not made other than with bread (i.e., all the inhabitants of a serious of homes sharing a common courtyard place food in one place before Shabbat, they are considered as an extended household and one may carry within the courtyard), and this "בכל"/”with any” that is taught in our Mishnah , is not exact, as we state in the Gemara (Tractate Eruvin 27a): “one may not derive anything from the general statement, not even when an exception is stated (as there may be other exceptions),” for surely here it is taught in our Mishnah: “With any [food] do they prepare the Eruv… except for water and salt,” and there are also a kind of mushroom and truffles that we don’t make Eruvin and merging [of alleyways] with them (i.e., if several courtyards open into a common alleyway, it is prohibited for the inhabitants of the houses in the courtyards to carry within the alleyway unless he inhabitants of each house place food in one place for the duration of Shabbat together with the placement of a side pole placed at the entrance to the alleyway or a cross-beam placed over it to mark the entrance to the alleyway and that it is prohibited to carry outside the alleyway), and these are not food, like water and salt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:1:2)


**וּמִשְׁתַּתְּפִים.** שִׁתּוּפֵי מְבוֹאוֹת:


ומשתתפים – merging of alleyways.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 3:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:1:3)


**הַכֹּל נִקָּח בְּכֶסֶף מַעֲשֵׂר.** כְּדִכְתִיב (דברים יד) וְנָתַתָּ הַכֶּסֶף בְּכָל אֲשֶׁר תְּאַוֶּה נַפְשֶׁךָ:


הכל נלקח בכסף מעשר – as it is written (Deuteronomy 14:26): “and spend the money on anything you want – [cattle, sheep, wine, or other intoxicant or anything you may desire].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 3:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:1:4)


**חוּץ מִן הַמַּיִם וּמִן הַמֶּלַח.** דְּלָאו פְּרִי מִפְּרִי הוּא:


חוץ מן המים ומן המלח – for it is not fruit from fruit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 3:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:1:5)


**הַנּוֹדֵר מִן הַמָּזוֹן.** לֹא דְּאָמַר יֵאָסֵר עָלַי מָזוֹן, דְּאֵין נִקְרָא מָזוֹן אֶלָּא חֲמֵשֶׁת הַמִּינִים דְּזָיְנֵי וְסַעֲדֵי, אֶלָּא כְּגוֹן דְּאָמַר כָּל הַזָּן עָלַי קוֹנָם, וְכָל מִלֵּי זָיְנֵי וּמַשְׂבִּיעִים לְפִי שָׁעָה חוּץ מִן הַמַּיִם וְהַמֶּלַח:


הנודר מן המזון – not that he said: “that nourishment should be forbidden to me,” for it is not called “nourishment”/מזון – other than from five species that nourishes and satisfies (see Talmud Eruvin 30a), but rather, as for example, that he said, all that feed me – I take a vow of abstinence (as a substitute for the word “sacrifice”/קרבן ), and all words of support/nourishing and we impose an oath by hours, except from water and salt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 3:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:1:6)


**מְעָרְבִין לְנָזִיר בְּיַיִן.** דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּלָא חֲזִי לְדִידֵיהּ, חֲזִי לְאַחֲרִינֵי:


מערבין לנזיר ביין – and even though it is not proper for him (as Nazirites are forbidden to partake of wine as part of their vow), it is proper for others.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 3:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:1:7)


**וּלְיִשְׂרָאֵל בִּתְרוּמָה.** דְּהָא חַזְיָא לַכֹּהֲנִים:


ולישראל בתרומה – for this (i.e., heave-offering) is appropriate [only] for Kohanim.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 3:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:1:8)


**סוּמְכוֹס אוֹמֵר בְּחֻלִּין.** דְּמִידֵי דַּחֲזִי לֵיהּ בָּעִינַן. וּבַיַּיִן לַנָּזִיר לֹא פָלֵיג סוּמְכוֹס, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁאֶפְשָׁר שֶׁיִּשָּׁאֵל הַנָּזִיר עַל נִדְרוֹ וְיִהְיֶה הַיַּיִן מֻתָּר לוֹ בְּאוֹתָהּ שַׁבָּת, אֲבָל תְּרוּמָה אִי אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁתִּהְיֶה רְאוּיָה לְיִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ יִשָּׁאֵל הַמַּפְרִישׁ הַתְּרוּמָה עָלֶיהָ וַהֲרֵי הִיא כְּאִלּוּ לֹא הוּרְמָה, הֲרֵי הִיא חוֹזֶרֶת לְטִבְלָהּ וְאָסוּר לֶאֱכֹל מִמֶּנָּה עַד שֶׁיַּחְזֹר וְיַפְרִישׁ תְּרוּמָה אַחֶרֶת, וְאֵין מַפְרִישִׁין תְּרוּמָה בְּשַׁבָּת אֲפִלּוּ בֵּין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת, הִלְכָּךְ לֹא חַזְיָא לֵיהּ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּסוּמְכוֹס:


סומכוס אומר חולי – fo we require something that is appropriate to him, and regarding wine for a Nazirite, Sumchos does not dispute, because it is possible that the Nazirite will come before a scholar for absolution from his vow and the wine will [then] be permitted to him on that Shabbat, but Terumah/heave-offering, it is impossible that it will be appropriate for an Israelite (as Terumah is only for a Kohen), for even an Israelite who separates the heave-offering upon it, and behold it will be as if it had not been lifted up, for behold, it returns to eatables that are forbidden pending the separation of sacred gifts, and it is forbidden to eat from it until he returns and separates another heave-offering, for we don’t separate heave-offering on Shabbat, even at twilight; therefore, it is not appropriate for him. But the Halakha is not according to Sumchos.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 3:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:1:9)


**וּלְכֹהֵן בְּבֵית הַפְּרָס.** סְתָמָא הִיא, וְלָאו סוּמְכוֹס קָאֲמַר לַהּ.


ולכהן בבית הפרס – it is taught anonymously and is not Sumchos who said it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 3:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:1:10)


**בֵּית הַפְּרַס.** שָׂדֶה שֶׁנֶּחְרַשׁ בָּהּ קֶבֶר מֻתָּר לְכֹהֵן לִכָּנֵס שָׁם כְּשֶׁהוּא מְנַפֵּחַ וְהוֹלֵךְ וְרוֹאֶה שֶׁאֵינוֹ נוֹגֵעַ בְּעֶצֶם כִּשְׂעוֹרָה. וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי מֻתָּר לִתֵּן עֵרוּבוֹ שָׁם, שֶׁהֲרֵי יָכוֹל לְהִכָּנֵס בַּמָּקוֹם שֶׁנָּתַן שָׁם עֵרוּבוֹ וְהָוֵי הוּא וְעֵרוּבוֹ בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד:


בית הפרס – it is field in which a grave was ploughed, it is permitted for a Kohen to enter there when he is breathing and walking by, and sees that he doesn’t touch a bone the size of a barley-corn, and because of this, it is permitted to place his Eruv there, for he is able to enter in the place where he placed his Eruv, but he and his Eruv are in another place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 3:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:1:11)


**לָחוֹץ.** לַעֲשׂוֹת מְחִצָּה בֵּינוֹ לַקֶּבֶר שֶׁלֹּא יַאֲהִיל, כְּגוֹן שֶׁיִּכָּנֵס שָׁם בְּשִׁדָּה תֵּבָה וּמִגְדָּל, אַלְמָא חֲזִי לֵיהּ. וּפְלֻגְתָּא דְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה וְרַבָּנָן מוֹקְמִינַן בַּגְּמָרָא דַּאֲפִלּוּ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל פְּלִיגֵי רַבָּנָן עֲלֵיהּ דְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה וְאָמְרֵי דְּאֵין מַנִּיחִין עֵרוּבֵי תְּחוּמִין בְּבֵית הַקְּבָרוֹת, וְלֹא נָקַט כֹּהֵן אֶלָּא לְהוֹדִיעֲךָ כֹּחוֹ דְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה דַּאֲפִלּוּ בְּכֹהֵן שָׁרֵי. וּבְהָא קָמִפְלְגֵי, דְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה סָבַר אַף עַל גַּב דְּאָסוּר לֶהֱנוֹת וּלְהִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בְּבֵית הַקְּבָרוֹת, מֻתָּר לְהַנִּיחַ שָׁם עֵרוּב, דְּאֵין מְעָרְבִין עֵרוּבֵי תְּחוּמִין אֶלָּא לִדְבַר מִצְוָה וּמִצְווֹת לָאו לֶהֱנוֹת נִתְּנוּ, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמִּשְׁתַּמֵּר שָׁם הָעֵרוּב אַחַר קְנִיָּתוֹ, דְּהָא בֵּין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת קוֹנֶה וּכְבָר נַעֲשֵׂית הַמִּצְוָה וְהוּא מִשְׁתַּמֵּר שָׁם כָּל הַשַּׁבָּת, קָסָבַר רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אֵין אָדָם מַקְפִּיד עַל עֵרוּבוֹ אִם אָבַד אוֹ נִגְנַב לְאַחַר שֶׁקָּנָה. וְרַבָּנָן סָבְרֵי אָדָם מַקְפִּיד עַל עֵרוּבוֹ לְאַחַר שֶׁקָּנָה, וְרוֹצֶה שֶׁלֹּא יִגָּנֵב, הִלְכָּךְ כְּשֶׁהָעֵרוּב מִשְׁתַּמֵּר בְּבֵית הַקְּבָרוֹת כָּל הַשַּׁבָּת לְאַחַר שֶׁקָּנָה בֵּין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת וְנִגְמְרָה מִצְוָתוֹ נִמְצָא מִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בְּאִסּוּרֵי הֲנָאָה, שֶׁהַקְּבָרוֹת אֲסוּרִים בַּהֲנָאָה, לְפִיכָךְ לֹא יַנִּיחַ אָדָם עֵרוּב בְּבֵית הַקְּבָרוֹת. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


לחוץ – to make a partition between himself and the grave so that it would not cause Levitical uncleanness arising from being under the same shelter with, or forming a shelter over, a corpse, such, for example that he would enter there with a chest, ark, and/or a turret. So we see that is appropriate for him but the dispute of Rabbi Yehuda and the Rabbis we establish in the Gemara (Tractate Eruvin 30b-31a) that even regarding an Israelite, the Rabbis dispute upon that of Rabbi Yehudah, and they state that we don’t place the joining of borders (i.e., by placing a enough food for a small meal somewhere within one’s two thousand cubit limit, one establishes that location as one’s residence for Shabbat) in a cemetery but the Mishnah did not mention the Kohen, other to inform us of the strength of Rabbi Yehuda’s [opinion], for even a Kohen they permitted, and in this, they dispute, for Rabbi Yehuda holds that even though it is prohibited to benefit and to use the cemetery, it is permitted to place the Eruv there, for we don’t make a joining of borders other than for a matter of a commandment, but the commandments were not given to benefit from them but even though the Eruv guards after its acquisition , for at twilight it acquires and already the commandment has been performed and it guards there the entire Shabbat, Rabbi Yehuda holds that a person is not strict regarding his Eruv if he lost it or it was stolen after he acquired it, but the Rabbis hold that a person is strict regarding his Eruv after he acquired it and desires that it not be stolen; therefore, when the Eruv is guarded in the cemetery all of Shabbat after he acquired it at twilight and the commandment was completed, it was found that he is using something that is forbidden for benefit, and that the graves are forbidden for benefit and therefore, a person should not leave his Eruv in the cemetery and the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:2:1)


**מְעָרְבִין בִּדְמַאי.** בְּכִכָּר שֶׁלְּקָחָהּ מֵעַם הָאָרֶץ וְלֹא הִפְרִישׁ מִמֶּנָּה תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר שֶׁל דְּמַאי:


מערבין בדמאי – with a loaf that was taken from an ignoramus/illiterate (i.e., someone who does not observe the rules of tithing and Levitical uncleanness) and did not separate from it the tithe of the tithe of doubtfully tithed produce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 3:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:2:2)


**וּבְמַעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן כוּ'‏ אֲבָל לֹא בְטֶבֶל וְכוּ'.** כֻּלָּן מְפֹרָשִׁין בְּמַסֶּכֶת שַׁבָּת פֶּרֶק מְפַנִּין:


ובמעשר ראשון כו' אבל לא בטבל וכו' – all of these are explained in Tractate Shabbat, in the Chapter “They Clear Away” פרק מפנין(Chapter 18, Mishnah 1).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 3:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:2:3)


**הַשּׁוֹלֵחַ אֶת עֵרוּבוֹ בְּיַד חֵרֵשׁ כוּ'.** לְהוֹלִיכוֹ לְסוֹף אַלְפַּיִם אַמָּה. וְדַוְקָא בְּעֵרוּבֵי תְּחוּמִין אֵינוֹ עֵרוּב אִם שְׁלָחוֹ בְּיַד קָטָן, אֲבָל בְּעֵרוּבֵי חֲצֵרוֹת קָיְמָא לָן קָטָן גּוֹבֶה אֶת הָעֵרוּב:


השולח את ערובו ביד חרש כו' – to bring it to the end of two thousand cubits and specifically regarding the joining of borders/עירובי תחומין is not an Eruv if he sent it in the hand of a minor, but with עירובי חצרות /the joining of courtyards, it exists for us with a minor seizing the Eruv.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 3:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:2:4)


**בְּיַד מִי שֶׁאֵינוֹ מוֹדֶה בָעֵרוּב.** כְּגוֹן כּוּתִי אוֹ צְדוֹקִי:


ביד מי שאינו מודה בערוב – such as, for example, a Cuthean or a Sadducee (who does not recognized the validity of the Oral Torah).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 3:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:2:5)


**וְאִם אָמַר לְאַחֵר.** כָּשֵׁר, לְקַבְּלוֹ מִן הַפָּסוּל וְיוֹלִיכֶנּוּ הַכָּשֵׁר לְסוֹף אַלְפַּיִם אַמָּה:


ואם אמר לאחר – who is deemed fit to receive it from someone not eligible and the fit one will bring it to the end of two thousand cubits.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 3:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:2:6)


**הֲרֵי זֶה עֵרוּב.** וּבִלְבַד שֶׁיְּהֵא הוּא עוֹמֵד וְרוֹאֶה בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁהַפָּסוּל נוֹתְנוֹ לְיַד הַכָּשֵׁר, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ רוֹאֶה כְּשֶׁהַכָּשֵׁר מוֹלִיכוֹ, דַּחֲזָקָה שָׁלִיחַ עוֹשֶׂה שְׁלִיחוּתוֹ:


הרי זה עירוב – this is an Eruv as long as that he stands and sees at the time when the ineligible wone gives it to the hand of the one who is fit, even though he does not see it, for the fit one takes it for he performs with the presumption of an agent his mission.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:3:1)


**נְתָנוֹ בְאִילָן.** הָעוֹמֵד בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים וְיֵשׁ בּוֹ אַרְבָּעָה טְפָחִים עַל אַרְבָּעָה טְפָחִים אוֹ יוֹתֵר:


נותנו באילן – A person who stands in the public domain and it has in it four handbreadths by handbreadths or more.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 3:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/3:3:2)


**לְמַעְלָה מֵעֲשָׂרָה אֵין עֵרוּבוֹ עֵרוּב.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּהָאִילָן רָחָב אַרְבָּעָה, לְמַעְלָה מֵעֲשָׂרָה הָוֵי רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד, וְהוּא קָנָה שְׁבִיתָתוֹ בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וְכֵיוָן דְּאִי הָוֵי בָּעֵי לְמִשְׁקַל עֵרוּבֵיהּ וּמֵיכְלֵיהּ בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁהָעֵרוּב זוֹכֶה לוֹ, דְּהַיְנוּ בֵּין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת, לֹא מָצֵי שָׁקֵיל לֵיהּ דְּמַיְתֵי מֵרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, לֹא הָוֵי עֵרוּב:
