**אֵלּוּ מַאֲכִילִין.** מַנִּיחִין בְּהֶמְתָּן לֶאֱכֹל מִן הַתֶּבֶן, וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן דִּלְמָא מִמְעִיט הַתֶּבֶן מֵעֲשָׂרָה וַהֲוָה לֵיהּ חֲדָא רְשׁוּתָא וְאָסְרֵי אַהֲדָדֵי, וּמְטַלְטְלֵי בֶּחָצֵר וְלֹא מַסְקֵי אַדַּעְתַּיְהוּ, לְהָא לֹא חָיְשִׁינַן, דְּהָא אֲפִלּוּ אִמְּעִיט אִי לֹא הָוֵי הַמִּעוּט עַל פְּנֵי עֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת וְיוֹתֵר לֹא אָסְרֵי, דְּהָא עֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת פִּתְחָא הָוֵי וְאֵין בְּהֵמָה מְמַעֶטֶת כָּל כָּךְ בַּאֲכִילָתָהּ [בְּשַׁבָּת אֶחָד]:


אלו מאכילין – they place their cattle to eat from the straw, and we don’t suspect that perhaps the straw will be come less than ten [handbreadths] and that it would be one domain and they would be mutually prohibited [from eating] and would carry in the courtyard and not be aware of it, for this we do not suspect for even whether it would decrease or not, the minority is over the surface of ten cubits and more does not prohibit it, for ten cubits are an opening, and the cattle do not lessen it all that much in its eating [on one Sabbath].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:5:3)


**נִתְמַעֵט הַתֶּבֶן מֵעֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים.** עַל פְּנֵי כָּל אֹרֶךְ הֶחָצֵר אוֹ בְּמֶשֶׁךְ עֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת [וְעוֹד]:


נתמעט התבן מי' טפחים – over the surface of the entire length of the courtyard or amidst the ten cubits [and more].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:5:4)


**מְעָרְבִין אֶחָד.** אִם נִתְמַעֵט בְּחֹל לִפְנֵי הַשַּׁבָּת:


מערבין אחד – if it decreased on the weekday before [the onset of] Shabbat.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:6:1)


**מֵנִיחַ אֶת הֶחָבִית.** מִשֶּׁלּוֹ, אִם יִרְצֶה, וְאָז צָרִיךְ לְזַכּוֹת, דְּאִלּוּ מִשֶּׁלָּהֶם אֵין צָרִיךְ לְזַכּוֹת:


מניח את החבית – [he sets down the jar] of his own [food, wine, etc.] if he wishes, and then needs to grant possession/entitles, for whereas it if is theirs, he doesn’t need to grant possession/entitle [to them].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:6:2)


**וּמְזַכֶּה לָהֶם עַל יְדֵי בְנוֹ וּבִתּוֹ.** אוֹמֵר לָהֶם קַבְּלוּ חָבִית זוֹ וּזְכוּ בָּהּ לְשֵׁם כָּל בְּנֵי הַמָּבוֹי. וְהַזּוֹכֶה מַגְבִּיהַּ הָעֵרוּב בְּיָדוֹ טֶפַח מִן הַקַּרְקַע, דְּכָל הֵיכָא דְּמַנְחָא בִּרְשׁוּתֵיהּ לֹא הַוְיָא זְכִיָּה, וְאוֹמֵר זִכִּיתִי לָהֶם:


ומזכה להם ע"י בנו ובתן – he says to them: “Accept this jar and take possession of it in the name of all the members of the alleyway.” And the person who takes possession lifts up the Eruv with his hand a handbreadth from the ground, for wherever that he places it in his domain, there would be no taking of possession.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:6:3)


**שֶׁיָּדָן כְּיָדוֹ.** וְלָאו זְכִיָּה הִיא:


שידן כידו – this is not taking of possession [since the minor child or the Canaanite slave or maidservant are considered as property of the parent/master].



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:7:1)


**נִתְמַעֵט הָאֹכֶל.** מִשִּׁעוּרוֹ הַמְפֹרָשׁ לְקַמָּן בְּמַתְנִיתִין:


נתמעט האוכל – from its measurement that is explained further in our Mishnah (i.e., in Mishnah 8).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:7:2)


**מוֹסִיף וּמְזַכֶּה.** אִם בָּא לְהוֹסִיף מִשֶּׁלּוֹ אֲפִלּוּ מִמִּין אַחֵר שֶׁאֵינוֹ מִמִּין הַשִּׁתּוּף הָרִאשׁוֹן, מוֹסִיף וּמְזַכֶּה וְאֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לְהוֹדִיעָן, כֵּיוָן שֶׁנִּשְׁאַר קְצָת מִן הַמִּין הָרִאשׁוֹן אֵינוֹ נִרְאֶה כִּמְעָרֵב בַּתְּחִלָּה. אֲבָל אִם נוֹתֵן מִמִּין הַשִּׁתּוּף הָרִאשׁוֹן, אֲפִלּוּ כָּלָה לְגַמְרֵי מֵבִיא מֵאוֹתוֹ הַמִּין וּמְזַכֶּה וְאֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לְהוֹדִיעָן:


מוסיף ומזכה – if he comes to add from his own [food or wine], even from a different species [of food] from the first partnership/shared species, he adds and causes them to take possess and he doesn’t need to inform them (i.e., the other participants in the partnership alleyway), for since there remains a bit from the first species, it doesn’t appear like it an Eruv was made at the outset, but if he puts in from the partnership/shared species [of food], even if it was completely finished, he brings from the same species and he doesn’t need to inform them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:7:3)


**נִתּוֹסְפוּ עֲלֵיהֶם דִּיּוּרִים מוֹסִיף וּמְזַכֶּה וְצָרִיךְ לְהוֹדִיעַ.** וְדַוְקָא בִּזְמַן שֶׁיֵּשׁ לֶחָצֵר שְׁנֵי פְּתָחִים לִשְׁתֵּי מְבוֹאוֹת, שֶׁכְּשֶׁעוֹשֶׂה עֵרוּב עִם אַנְשֵׁי מָבוֹי זֶה אָסוּר לְהִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בְּמָבוֹי אַחֵר, לְפִיכָךְ צָרִיךְ לְהוֹדִיעַ, דִּלְמָא לֹא נִיחָא לְהוּ לִקְנוֹת בְּרוּחַ זוֹ וּלְהַנִּיחַ יָד בָּרוּחַ שֶׁכְּנֶגְדָהּ. אֲבָל אִם אֵין לוֹ פֶּתַח אֶלָּא לְמָבוֹי זֶה שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּתְּפוּ בּוֹ תְּחִלָּה אֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לְהוֹדִיעַ, דְּזָכִין לָאָדָם שֶׁלֹּא בְּפָנָיו וְאֵין חָבִין לָאָדָם אֶלָּא בְּפָנָיו:


נתוספו עליהם דיורים מוסיף ומזכה וצריך להודיע – and especially at the time when the courtyard has two openings for two alleyways, for when they make an Eruv with the people of this alleyway, it is forbidden to use another alleyway, therefore it is necessary to inform them, for perhaps it is not satisfactory for them to acquire in this direction and to effect a possession in the direction that is opposite it, but if it lacks an opening other than to this alleyway that they have participated/shared in from the outset, there is no need to inform, because you may obtain a privilege in behalf of a person in his absence, but you cannot act in his behalf to his disadvantage (see the conclusion of Tractate Eruvin, Chapter 7, Mishnah 11, where this phrase is used).



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:8:1)


**מְרֻבִּין.** שְׁמֹנָה עָשָׂר בְּנֵי אָדָם וּלְמַעְלָה:


מרובין – eighteen people or more.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:8:2)


**מֻעָטִין.** פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁמֹנָה עָשָׂר:


מועטין – less than eighteen [people].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:8:3)


**מְזוֹן שְׁתֵּי סְעוּדוֹת.** הֵן שְׁמֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה גְּרוֹגָרוֹת:


מזון שתי סעודות – which are eighteen dried figs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:8:4)


**לְהוֹצָאַת שַׁבָּת.** שִׁעוּר שֶׁל הוֹצָאַת שַׁבָּת. כִּי הַמּוֹצִיא אֳכָלִים בְּשַׁבָּת אֵינוֹ חַיָּב בְּפָחוֹת מִכִּגְרוֹגֶרֶת:


להוצאת שבת – the quantity of removal on Shabbat. For the person who removes food on Shabbat is not liable with less than the equivalent of a dried fig (see Tractate Shabbat, Chapter 7, Mishnah 4).



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:9:1)


**בִּשְׁיָרֵי הָעֵרוּב.** שֶׁנִּתְמַעֵט מִשִּׁעוּרוֹ:


בשירי העירוב – when it diminished from its quantity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:9:2)


**וְלֹא אָמְרוּ לְעָרֵב בַּחֲצֵרוֹת.** אַחַר שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּתְּפוּ בַּמָּבוֹי, אֶלָּא שֶׁלֹּא תִּשְׁתַּכַּח תּוֹרַת עֵרוּבֵי חֲצֵרוֹת מִתִּינוֹקוֹת שֶׁאֵינָן יוֹדְעִין שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּתְּפוּ בַּמָּבוֹי, הִלְכָּךְ מְקִילִינַן בֵּיהּ. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּטַעֲמָא דִּמְעָרְבִין עֵרוּבֵי חֲצֵרוֹת לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּתְּפוּ בַּמָּבוֹי הָוֵי כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא תִּשְׁתַּכַּח תּוֹרַת עֵרוּב מִן הַתִּינוֹקוֹת, מִכָּל מָקוֹם אֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי בִּשְׁיָרֵי עֵרוּב, דְּלֹא מְקִילִינַן בֵּיהּ כֻּלֵּי הַאי:


ולא אמרו אלא לערב בחצרות – after they made a partnership in an alleyway, but rather, so that the law of the joining of courtyards not be forgotten by children that do not know that they made a partnership in an alleyway, therefore, we are lenient with it. But even though that the reason that they make an Eruv in the joining of courtyards after they had made a partnership in an alleyway is in order that the law of Eruv would not be forgotten from the children, nevertheless, the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi, concerning what is added on [later] to the Eruv that it is not [part of the Eruv], we are lenient with it so much.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:10:1)


**בַּכֹּל מְעָרְבִין.** עֵרוּבֵי תְּחוּמִין, דְּאִלּוּ עֵרוּבֵי חֲצֵרוֹת אֵין מְעָרְבִין אֶלָּא בְּפַת. וּמִיהוּ בַּכֹּל מְעָרְבִין דְּאָמַר רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר הָכָא, לַאֲתוֹיֵי עֵרוּבֵי חֲצֵרוֹת שֶׁמְּעָרְבִין בְּפַת פְּרוּסָה וְאֵין צָרִיךְ כִּכָּר שְׁלֵמָה, וּפָלֵיג עֲלֵיהּ רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ וְאָמַר דַּוְקָא כִּכָּר שָׁלֵם הוּא עֵרוּב. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


בכל מערבין – the joining of borders/תחומין , whereas, if were the joining of courtyards, we don’t made an Eruv other than with [a loaf of] bread. But however, that Rabbi Eliezer stated here that we make an Eruv with everything includes joining of courtyards with a half-a-loaf of bread and we don’t need a complete loaf, but Rabbi Yehoshua disputes him and states that specifically, an Eruv is a complete loaf. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:10:2)


**אֵין מְעָרְבִין בָּהּ.** וְטַעֲמָא מִשּׁוּם אֵיבָה, שֶׁבָּאִים בְּנֵי הֶחָצֵר לִידֵי מַחֲלֹקֶת לוֹמַר אֲנִי נוֹתֵן שְׁלֵמָה וְהוּא נוֹתֵן פְּרוּסָה:


אין מערבין בה – and the reason is because of enmity/hostility that the members of the courtyard come to a dispute to state: “I gave a full loaf, and he [only] gave one-half/part of [a loaf].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:10:3)


**כִּכָּר בְּאִסָּר וְהוּא שָׁלֵם מְעָרְבִין בּוֹ.** נוֹתְנִין אוֹתוֹ בָּעֵרוּב עִם שְׁאָר כִּכָּרוֹת שֶׁל בְּנֵי חָצֵר, וּבִלְבַד שֶׁיִּהְיֶה בֵּין כֻּלָּם כִּגְרוֹגֶרֶת לְכָל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד. וְרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ דְּכִכָּר שָׁלֵם כְּאִסָּר מְעָרְבִין בּוֹ לְבַדּוֹ לְכָל בְּנֵי הֶחָצֵר וְאֵין צָרִיךְ שִׁעוּר:


ככר מאיסר והוא שלם מערבין בו – they place it in the Eruv with the rest of the loaves of the members of the courtyard, as long as there will be among all of them the equivalent of a dried fig for each and every one. But Maimonides explained (see Hilkhot Eruvin, Chapter 1, Halakha 8), that a full loaf [of bread] the size of an Issar’s worth (i.e., 8 pennies), we make an Eruv with it alone for all the members of the courtyard and it does not require a [specific] quantity.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:11:1)


**נוֹתֵן אָדָם מָעָה לְחֶנְוָנִי.** הַמּוֹכֵר יַיִן, וְדָר עִמּוֹ בַּמָּבוֹי:


נותן אדם מעה לחנוני – the person who sells win, and lives with him in the alleyway.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:11:2)


**אוֹ לְנַחְתּוֹם.** הַמּוֹכֵר כִּכָּרוֹת וְדָר עִמּוֹ בֶּחָצֵר, שֶׁיִּזְכֶּה לוֹ בָּעֵרוּב עִם חֲבֵרָיו, אִם יָבֹאוּ בְּנֵי מָבוֹי לִקְנוֹת מִמְּךָ יַיִן לַשִּׁתּוּף, אוֹ בְּנֵי חָצֵר לִקְנוֹת כִּכָּר לָעֵרוּב, שֶׁיִּהְיֶה לִי חֵלֶק בּוֹ:


או לנחתום – the person who sells loaves [of bread] and lives with him in the courtyard, that he should obtain a privilege for him in the Eruv with his fellows. If the members of the alleyway come to acquire from you wine in partnership, or the members of the courtyard to acquire loaves [of bread] for the Eruv, that I will have a portion in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:11:3)


**לֹא זָכוּ לוֹ מְעוֹתָיו.** שֶׁאֵין מָעוֹת קוֹנוֹת עַד שֶׁיִּמְשֹׁךְ. וַאֲפִלּוּ עֵרֵב זֶה הַחֶנְוָנִי לְכָל הָאֲחֵרִים וְזִכָּה גַּם לָזֶה, אֵינוֹ עֵרוּב, שֶׁהֲרֵי לֹא נִתְכַּוֵּן לְזַכּוֹת לוֹ בְּמַתְּנַת חִנָּם כִּשְׁאָר הַמְזַכִּין בָּעֵרוּב אֶלָּא שֶׁיִּקְנֶה בְּמָעָה, וַהֲרֵי אֵינָהּ קוֹנָה לוֹ, דְּמָעוֹת אֵינָן קוֹנוֹת, וְנִמְצָא מְעָרֵב לוֹ בִּמְעוֹתָיו:


לא זכו לו מעותיו – for money does not purchase until he “pulls,” and even that this storekeeper made an Eruv for all of the others and to effect a possession also to that one, it is not an Eruv, for behold, he did not intend to cause him possess through a gift for nothing like the rest who effect a possession in the Eruv, other than that he should acquire it through a M’ah, for behold, he does not acquire it for him, for money does not acquire and it is found that the makes an Eruv for him with his money.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:11:4)


**וּמוֹדִים בִּשְׁאָר כָּל אָדָם.** בַּעַל הַבַּיִת שֶׁאָמַר לוֹ חֲבֵרוֹ הֵילָךְ מָעָה זוֹ וְזַכֵּה לִי בָּעֵרוּב וְהָלַךְ וְזִכָּה לוֹ, שֶׁקָּנָה עֵרוּב, דְּהוֹאִיל וְאֵין בַּעַל הַבַּיִת רָגִיל לִמְכֹּר כִּכָּרוֹת לֹא נִתְכַּוֵּן זֶה אֶלָּא לַעֲשׂוֹתוֹ שָׁלִיחַ וְנַעֲשָׂה כְּאוֹמֵר לוֹ עָרֵב לִי:


ומודים בשאר כל אדם – the houseowner whose fellow said to him: “Here is this M’ah; and effect possession for me in the Eruv, and he went and obtained the privilege for, for he acquired an Eruv. But since the houseowner does not customarily sell loaves of bread, he did not intend for this, but rather, to make him an agent and it would be made like one who said to him – “make an Eruv for me.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:11:5)


**שֶׁאֵין מְעָרְבִין לְאָדָם.** כְּשֶׁמְּעָרְבִין לוֹ מִשֶּׁלּוֹ אֶלָּא מִדַּעְתּוֹ. הִלְכָּךְ גַּבֵּי חֶנְוָנִי כִּי אָמַר לוֹ זַכֵּה לִי לֹא נִתְכַּוֵּן אֶלָּא לִקְנוֹת מִמֶּנּוּ וְלֹא סָמַךְ עָלָיו שֶׁיֵּעָשֶׂה שְׁלוּחוֹ, וּמָעוֹת אֵינָן קוֹנוֹת וְלֹא סָמְכֵיהּ דַּעְתֵּיהּ, נִמְצָא מְעָרֵב לוֹ שֶׁלֹּא מִדַּעְתּוֹ:


שאין מערבין לאדם – when they make an Eruv for him from on his own, but rather [it should be done] with his knowledge. Therefore, regarding the storekeeper, when he said to him, “effect possession [in the Eruv],” he did not intend other than to acquire from him, but he did not rely upon him that he should become his agent, but money does not purchase/acquire, and he didn’t rely upon him; it is found that he is enabling possession [of the Eruv] for him without his knowledge/consent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 7:11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/7:11:6)


**עֵרוּבֵי תְחוּמִין.** חוֹב הוּא, שֶׁמַּפְסִיד לַצַּד הָאַחֵר, וְשֶׁמָּא אֵין נוֹחַ לוֹ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


עירובי תחומין – it is a liability that he loses to the side of the other, and perhaps it is not pleasing to him. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.



## Chapter 8


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:1:1)


**כֵּיצַד.** לְכָל מִי שֶׁיֵּלֵךְ לְבֵית הָאֵבֵל וְכוּ'. דְּמִילֵי דְּמִצְוָה נִינְהוּ, וְקַיְמָא לָן אֵין מְעָרְבִין עֵרוּבֵי תְּחוּמִין אֶלָּא לִדְבַר מִצְוָה:


כיצד. לכל מי שילך לבית האבל וכו' – that these are matters [pertaining to] a Mitzvah, and we hold that one does not make a joining of borders/עירובי תחומין other than for a Mitzvah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:1:2)


**וְכֹל מִי שֶׁקִּבֵּל עָלָיו.** לִסְמֹךְ עַל עֵרוּב זֶה מֻתָּר:


וכל מי שקבל עליו – to rely upon this Eruv, it is permitted.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:1:3)


**וּמִשֶּׁחֲשֵׁכָה אָסוּר.** וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי כְּשֶׁלֹּא הוֹדִיעוּהוּ מִבְּעוֹד יוֹם, אֲבָל הוֹדִיעוּהוּ מִבְּעוֹד יוֹם אֲפִלּוּ לֹא קִבֵּל עָלָיו לִסְמֹךְ עַל עֵרוּב זֶה בְּוַדַּאי אֶלָּא מִשֶּׁחֲשֵׁכָה, מֻתָּר:


ומשחשיכה אסור – and these words [apply] when they didn’t inform him while it was still daylight [on Friday before Shabbat], but if he informed him while it was still daylight [on Friday], even if he hadn’t accepted upon himself to rely upon this Eruv for certain, other than once it became dark, it is permitted.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:2:1)


**מְזוֹן שְׁתֵּי סְעוּדוֹת לְכָל אֶחָד.** מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁקּוֹנֶה שְׁבִיתָתוֹ שָׁם צָרִיךְ לְהַנִּיחַ שָׁם מָזוֹן שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לוֹ לְשַׁבָּת:


מזון שתי סעודות לכל אחד – because he acquires his Sabbath camp/place to be the center of Sabbath movements there, he needs to leave there some food that he needs for Shabbat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:2:2)


**אֵלּוּ וָאֵלּוּ מִתְכַּוְּנִים לְהָקֵל.** רַבִּי מֵאִיר סָבַר בְּשַׁבָּת אָכִיל אֱינַשׁ טְפֵי מִשּׁוּם דְּבָסִים תַּבְשִׁילֵיהּ. וְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה סָבַר כֵּיוָן דִּבְשַׁבָּת אָכִיל שָׁלֹשׁ סְעוּדוֹת אֵינוֹ מַרְבֶּה לֶאֱכֹל בְּכָל סְעוּדָה, וּשְׁתֵּי סְעוּדוֹת שֶׁל חֹל הָוֵי טְפֵי מִשְּׁתֵּי סְעוּדוֹת שֶׁל שַׁבָּת:


אלו ואלו מתכוונים להקל – Rabbi Meir holds that on Shabbat a person eats more because it is the basis of his cooking (which is why he preferred as his standard the food that he consumes on a weekday). But Rabbi Yehuda holds that since on Shabbat he eats three meals, he doesn’t eat a great deal at each meal, but the two meals of a weekday is more than two meals of the Sabbath [day].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:2:3)


**כִּכָּר בְּפֻנְדְּיוֹן.** כִּכָּר הַלָּקוּחַ בְּפֻנְדְּיוֹן כְּשֶׁמּוֹכְרִין אַרְבָּעָה סְאִין חִטִּין לְסֶלַע. וְאַרְבָּעָה סְאִין הֵן עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה קַבִּין, וְהַסֶּלַע עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה מָעִין, נִמְצָא קַב לְכָל מָעָה, וְהַמָּעָה שְׁנֵי פֻנְדְּיוֹנִין, הֲרֵי כִּכָּר הַנִּמְכָּר בַּשּׁוּק בְּפֻנְדְּיוֹן הוּא חֲצִי קַב, וְהַחֶנְוָנִי לוֹקֵחַ הַחֵצִי לִשְׂכַר הָאֲפִיָּה וְהַטְּחִינָה, נִמְצָא כִּכָּר הַלָּקוּחַ מִן הַחֶנְוָנִי בְּפֻנְדְּיוֹן הוּא רֶבַע קַב שֶׁהֵם שֵׁשׁ בֵּיצִים, שֶׁהַקַּב עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע בֵּיצִים, וְהֵם שִׁעוּר שְׁתֵּי סְעוּדוֹת שֶׁל עֵרוּב לְדִבְרֵי רַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן בְּרוֹקָא. וַהֲלָכָה כְּמֹתוֹ:


ככר בפונדיון – a loaf that is purchased for a Pundiyon (equal to 16 perutot/pennies or 2 Issar) – when they sell four S’ah of wheat to a Sela, and four S’ah are twenty-four Kabin, and the Sela is twenty-four M’ah, it is found that it is a Kab for each M’ah and the M’ah is two Pundiyon, behold that a loaf that is sold in the marketplace for a Pundiyon which is one-half of a Kab, and the storekeeper buys half for the cost of the baking and the milling, it is found that the loaf that is purchased from the storekeeper for a Pundiyon is one-quarter of a Kab which is six eggs, for the Kab is twenty-four eggs in volume, and they are the measurement of two meals of the Eruv according to Rabbi Yohanan ben Berokah. And the Halakha is according to him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:2:4)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן.** מְמַעֵט בַּשִּׁעוּר וְאוֹמֵר שֶׁדַּי לְעָרֵב בִּשְׁתֵּי יָדוֹת שֶׁל כִּכָּר שֶׁל שְׁלֹשָׁה כִּכָּרוֹת לְקַב. וְכִכָּר שָׁלֵם שֶׁל שְׁמֹנֶה בֵּיצִים הוּא שְׁלִישִׁית הַקַּב. וּשְׁתֵּי יְדוֹתֶיהָ שֶׁהֵם חָמֵשׁ בֵּיצִים וְיוֹתֵר הָווּ שְׁתֵּי סְעוּדוֹת:


רבי שמעון – [he] reduces the requisite measure and states that it is enough to make an Eruv with two-thirds of a loaf of the size of three loaves to the Kab. And a complete loaf [of bread] of eight eggs is one-third of a Kab. But two-thirds of it which are five eggs and more (i.e., one-third) is for two meals.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:2:5)


**חֶצְיָהּ לְבַיִת הַמְנֻגָּע.** סְתָם מַתְנִיתִין הִיא, וְהָכִי קָאָמַר, חֲצִי כִּכָּר שָׁלֵם שֶׁשִּׁעֲרוּ בּוֹ בֵּין לְמַר וּבֵין לְמַר, הוּא שִׁעוּר לִשְׁהִיַּת בַּיִת הַמְנֻגָּע, שֶׁהַנִּכְנָס לְבַיִת שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ נֶגַע צָרַעַת אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא טָמֵא מִיָּד אֵינוֹ טָעוּן כִּבּוּס בְּגָדִים עַד שֶׁיִּשְׁהֶה כְּדֵי אֲכִילָה, וַחֲצִי כִּכָּר זוֹ הָוֵי כְּדֵי אֲכִילָה. וּלְרַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן בְּרוֹקָה דְּאָמַר כִּכָּר שָׁלֵם הָוֵי רֶבַע קַב שֶׁהֵם שִׁשָּׁה בֵּיצִים, נִמְצָא חֶצְיָהּ שָׁלֹשׁ בֵּיצִים, וְהוּא פְּרַס דִּבְכֻלֵּי תַּלְמוּדָא. וּלְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן דְּאָמַר כִּכָּר שָׁלֵם הוּא שֶׁל שְׁלִישִׁית הַקַּב שֶׁהֵם שְׁמֹנֶה בֵּיצִים, הָוֵי חֶצְיָהּ אַרְבַּע בֵּיצִים, וְהוּא פְּרַס דִּבְכֻלֵּי תַּלְמוּדָא אַלִּיבָּא דְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן סָבַר דִּבְכִכָּר שָׁלֵם יֵשׁ שָׁלֹשׁ סְעוּדוֹת, דְּהָא שְׁתֵּי יְדוֹתֶיהָ קָאָמַר רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן דַּהֲוֵי שְׁתֵּי סְעוּדוֹת, הָנֵי מִלֵּי בְּעֵרוּב דְּנִתְכַּוְּנוּ בּוֹ לְהָקֵל וְאֵין צָרִיךְ שְׁתֵּי סְעוּדוֹת שְׁלֵמוֹת, אֲבָל בְּכָל שְׁאָר דּוּכְתֵי סְבִירָא לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן דְּאֵין סְעוּדָה פְּחוּתָה מֵחֲצִי כִּכָּר שֶׁל שְׁלִישִׁית הַקַּב, וּבָעִינַן שֶׁיִּשְׁהֶה כְּדֵי אֲכִילַת חֲצִי כִּכָּר זוֹ שֶׁהֵם אַרְבַּע בֵּיצִים:


חציה לבית המנוגע – this [section] is an anonymous Mishnah, and this is what he said: Half of a full loaf [of bread] that they estimated/measured whether according to the one (Rabbi Yohanan ben Beroka) or the other (Rabbi Shimon), is the measurement for a delay/pause in a leprous house, for a person who enters into a house where there is within it the plague of leprosy, even though becomes immediately ritually defiled, he does not require the washing of his clothes until he waits in order to eat, and this half-loaf [of bread] is in order that he can eat. But for Rabbi Yohanan ben Beroka who stated that a full loaf which is a quarter of a Kab – being the volume of six eggs, it is found that half of that is three eggs, and it this is a piece/certain quantity of bread [or half a loaf] (see Tractate Negaim, Chapter 13, Mishnah 9), that is in the entire Talmud. But for Rabbi Shimon who stated that a full loaf [of bread] is one-third of a Kab, which is eight eggs, that half is four eggs, and this is a piece of bread/half a loaf in all of the Talmud according to Rabbi Shimon. But even though that Rabbi Shimon holds that in a full loaf [of bread] are three meals, for the two-thirds that Rabbi Shimon mentions – is for two meals, these words concern an Eruv that they intended for it, to be lenient, and there is no need for two complete meals. But in all the rest of the places, Rabbi Shimon thinks that a meal is not less than one-half of a loaf of one-third of a Kab, and we require that he wait/delay [the time it takes] in order that he can eat this half-loaf which is four eggs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:2:6)


**חֲצִי חֶצְיָהּ לִפְסֹל אֶת הַגְּוִיָּה.** דְּהָאוֹכֵל אֳכָלִים טְמֵאִים כַּחֲצִי פְּרַס נִפְסָל גּוּפוֹ מִדְּרַבָּנָן מִלֶּאֱכֹל בִּתְרוּמָה. וַחֲצִי פְּרַס הָוֵי בֵּיצָה וּמֶחֱצָה לְרַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן בְּרוֹקָה, וּשְׁתֵּי בֵּיצִים לְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


חצי חציה לפסול את הגויה – that the person who consumes impure foods equivalent to one-half of a piece of bread, his body is defiled according to the Rabbis from eating heave-offering/Terumah (if he is a Kohen). And half of a piece of bread is an egg-and-a-half [in volume] according to Rabbi Yohanan ben Beroka and two eggs [in volume] according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:3:1)


**מִרְפֶּסֶת.** מָקוֹם גָּבוֹהַּ בַּחֲצַר הַבַּיִת, וְהַרְבֵּה פִּתְחֵי עֲלִיּוֹת פְּתוּחִין לָהּ, וְכֻלָּם יוֹצְאִין בְּסֻלָּם אֶחָד לֶחָצֵר וּמִמֶּנּוּ לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵן אֵין אוֹסְרִין הוֹאִיל וּמִרְפֶּסֶת גְּבוֹהָה עֲשָׂרָה, וּבִלְבַד שֶׁיְּעָרְבוּ בְּנֵי מִרְפֶּסֶת לְעַצְמָן בַּמִּרְפֶּסֶת, דְּהַוְיָא רֶגֶל הַמֻּתֶּרֶת בִּמְקוֹמָהּ וְאֵינָהּ אוֹסֶרֶת:


מרפסת (porch/gallery) – a high place in the courtyard of the house and many openings of upper chambers are open to it and everyone descends on one ladder to the courtyard and from it into the public domain, but even though they are not forbidden, for since the gallery/porch is ten [handbreadths] high, as long as the people/members of the gallery will make an Eruv for themselves on the gallery/porch that there will be foot-traffic that is permissible in its place, and doesn’t prohibit (other than in its own place – see Tractate Eruvin, Chapter 6, Mishnayot 9-10).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:3:2)


**שֶׁשָּׁכְחוּ וְלֹא עֵרְבוּ.** אֵלּוּ עִם אֵלּוּ. אֲבָל עֵרְבוּ אֵלּוּ לְעַצְמָן וְאֵלּוּ לְעַצְמָן:


ששכחו ולא עירבו – these (i.e., the people of the courtyard) with those (i.e., the people of the gallery/porch). But, each [group] made their own Eruv (i.e., one for the people of the courtyard and another for the people of the gallery) for themselves.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:3:3)


**כֹּל שֶׁגָּבוֹהַּ עֲשָׂרָה.** אִם יֵשׁ בֶּחָצֵר תֵּל אוֹ עַמּוּד גָּבוֹהַּ עֲשָׂרָה סָמוּךְ לַמִּרְפֶּסֶת וְאֵין מִרְפֶּסֶת גָּבוֹהַּ מִן הָעַמּוּד עֲשָׂרָה, דְּנִיחָא תַּשְׁמִישְׁתֵּיהּ דְּעַמּוּד לִבְנֵי הַמִּרְפֶּסֶת, רְשׁוּת מִרְפֶּסֶת שׁוֹלֶטֶת בּוֹ וּמִשְׁתַּמְשִׁין הֵן בּוֹ וְלֹא בְּנֵי הֶחָצֵר:


כל שגבוה י' – if there is a mound/hill or a pillar that is ten [handbreadths] high near the gallery and the gallery is not ten [handbreadths] higher than the pillar, for the usage of the of the pillar is satisfactory to those in the gallery, the domain of the gallery reigns over it and they use it and not those who are in the courtyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:3:4)


**פָּחוֹת מִכָּאן לֶחָצֵר.** אַף לֶחָצֵר קָאָמַר, שֶׁשְּׁתֵּי הָרְשֻׁיּוֹת שׁוֹלְטִין בּוֹ, הַמִּרְפֶּסֶת וְהֶחָצֵר, וְאִם לֹא עֵרְבוּ זֶה עִם זֶה שְׁנֵיהֶם אֲסוּרִים בּוֹ:


פחות מכאן לחצר – it is stated even to the courtyard, that two domains have power there – the gallery and the courtyard, but if they did not make an Eruv one with the other, both are forbidden to use it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:3:5)


**חֻלְיַת הַבּוֹר.** שְׂפַת הַבּוֹר שֶׁל מַעְלָה, אִם גָּבוֹהַּ עֲשָׂרָה מִן הֶחָצֵר רְשׁוּת מִרְפֶּסֶת שׁוֹלֶטֶת בּוֹ. וְהוּא שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הַבּוֹר מָלֵא עַד שְׂפָתוֹ הָעֶלְיוֹנָה מִדְּבָרִים שֶׁאָסוּר לְטַלְטְלָם בְּשַׁבָּת, דְּהַשְׁתָּא אִי אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁתֶּחְסַר מִגָּבְהָהּ, דּוּמְיָא דְּסֶלַע שֶׁאֵינוֹ חָסֵר, וַהֲרֵי הוּא שָׁוֶה לְקַרְקָעִית הַמִּרְפֶּסֶת, אֲבָל אִם אֵין הַבּוֹר מָלֵא אוֹ אֲפִלּוּ מָלֵא מִדְּבָרִים שֶׁאֶפְשָׁר לִקַּח מֵהֶם בְּשַׁבָּת וּלְחַסְּרוֹ, כֵּיוָן דְּכִי חַסְרָא אֲסִירָא, כִּי לֹא חַסְרָא נַמִּי אֲסִירָא, וְאִם לֹא עֵרְבוּ הַמִּרְפֶּסֶת וְהֶחָצֵר זֶה עִם זֶה שְׁנֵיהֶם אֲסוּרִים בּוֹ:


חולית הבור (the entrenchment around the well – increasing its capacity – see Tractate Eruvin 78a) – the rim of the cistern of above, if it is ten [handbreadths] taller from the courtyard, the domain of the gallery reigns over it. But this is so if the cistern will be filled until its upper rim from things that are forbidden to carry them in the Sabbath, so that now, it is impossible that it will lessen from its height, which is similar to a rock that is not missing, for behold, it is equivalent to the bottom of the gallery. But if the cistern is not full or even if is full from things that it is possible to take from them on Shabbat and to lessen it, since by lessening it, it makes it forbidden, but if he doesn’t lessen it, it is also forbidden, for if they didn’t make an Eruv of the gallery and the courtyard with each other, both are forbidden to it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:3:6)


**אֲפִלּוּ גָבוֹהַּ עֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים לֶחָצֵר.** אַף לֶחָצֵר כְּמוֹ לַמִּרְפֶּסֶת, וּשְׁנֵיהֶם אֲסוּרִים בּוֹ אִם לֹא עֵרְבוּ זֶה עִם זֶה:


אפי' גבוהה עשרה טפחים לחצר – even to the courtyard, like to the gallery, and both are forbidden to use if they did not make an Eruv with each other.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:4:1)


**הַנּוֹתֵן עֵרוּבוֹ.** עֵרוּבֵי חֲצֵרוֹת:


הנותן עירובו – the joining of courtyards.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:4:2)


**בְּבֵית שַׁעַר.** מָקוֹם סָמוּךְ לְשַׁעַר הֶחָצֵר וּרְגִילִין לְהוֹשִׁיב שָׁם שׁוֹמֵר שֶׁלֹּא יִכָּנְסוּ בְּנֵי רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים לֶחָצֵר:


בבית השער – a place adjacent to the gate of the courtyard and they customarily place there a guard so that people from the public domain should not enter into the courtyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:4:3)


**וְהַדָּר שָׁם.** בְּבֵית שַׁעַר שֶׁבֶּחָצֵר אֵינוֹ אוֹסֵר עַל בַּעַל הֶחָצֵר וְאֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לִתֵּן אֶת הַפַּת, דְּלָאו דִּירָה הִיא:


והדר שם – [the person who lives there] in the gate house that is in the courtyard, does not prohibit upon an owner in the courtyard and he doesn’t have to place bread [there], for it is not a living space.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:4:4)


**וְהַדָּר שָׁם אוֹסֵר עָלָיו.** אִם הִשְׁאִיל בַּעַל הַבַּיִת בֵּית הַתֶּבֶן שֶׁלּוֹ לָדוּר שָׁם, אוֹסֵר עָלָיו, הוֹאִיל וּפָתוּחַ לֶחָצֵר:


והדר שם אוסר עליו (a person who lives [in a shed for straw, cattle, wood or storage] who does not share in the Eruv -does prohibit him [from carrying in the courtyard]) – if the houseowner lent out his straw shed to dwell there, he prohibits him [from carrying] – since it is open to the courtyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:4:5)


**אִם יֶשׁ שָׁם תְּפִיסַת יָד שֶׁל בַּעַל הַבַּיִת.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ לְבַעַל הַבַּיִת מָקוֹם בְּדִירָתוֹ שֶׁל זֶה, שֶׁנּוֹתֵן שָׁם כֵּלָיו לְהַצְנִיעָן, אֵינוֹ אוֹסֵר עָלָיו. וְדַוְקָא כְּשֶׁאֵין שָׁם דִּיּוּרִין אֲחֵרִים, אֲבָל אִם יֵשׁ שָׁם דִּיּוּרִין אֲחֵרִים אוֹסֵר עֲלֵיהֶן, דַּיְקָא נַמִּי דְּקָתָנֵי אֵינוֹ אוֹסֵר עָלָיו, עָלָיו אֵינוֹ אוֹסֵר, אֲבָל עַל דִּיּוּרִים אֲחֵרִים אוֹסֵר וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁבַּעַל הַבַּיִת עֵרֵב עִמָּהֶן. וְאִם הַכֵּלִים הַלָּלוּ שֶׁנּוֹתֵן שָׁם בַּעַל הַבַּיִת הֵם דְּבָרִים הַנִּטָּלִים בְּשַׁבָּת, אֵין זוֹ תְּפִיסַת יָד, וְאוֹסֵר עָלָיו, דְּאִי בָּעֵי שָׁקֵיל לְהוּ וְשָׁדֵי לְהוּ אַבְרַאי. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


אם יש שם תפיסת יד של בעל הבית (right of storage) – that the house owner has a place in the dwelling in this (i.e., right of storage in, for example, the shed for straw), that he places there his utensils to hide them, it doesn’t forbid him [from carrying objects in the courtyard] , and especially when there aren’t other dwellers there, but if there are other dwellers there, it forbids him; you can also deduce it as it is taught [in the Mishnah] that it does not prohibit him [if he lives in the gatehouse, portico or gallery] – upon him it does not prohibit, but not upon other dwellers it does prohibit, and even though the owner of the house made an Eruv with them. But if these utensils that the owner of the house places there are things that are taken on Shabbat, this is not the right of storage and it prohibits him (i.e., the tenant), but if he wishes, he can take them and cast them outside. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:5:1)


**הֲרֵי זֶה אוֹסֵר.** דְּדִירָה בְּלֹא בְּעָלִים שְׁמָהּ דִּירָה:


הרי זה אוסר – that a dwelling without owners is called a dwelling.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:5:2)


**אֵינוֹ אוֹסֵר.** דְּלָאו שְׁמָהּ דִּירָה:


אינו אוסר – because it is not called a dwelling.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:5:3)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי.** נַמִּי סְבִירָא לֵיהּ דְּלָאו שְׁמָהּ דִּירָה, מִיהוּ נָכְרִי אוֹסֵר, שֶׁמָּא יָבֹא בְּשַׁבָּת. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


ר' יוסי – [Rabbi Yossi] holds that it is not called a dwelling, however, a heathen prohibits, lest he return/come home on the Sabbath. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:5:4)


**אֵצֶל בִּתּוֹ.** דַּוְקָא בִּתּוֹ, דְּעָבֵיד אֱינַשׁ דְּדָיַּר עִם חֲתָנוֹ, אֲבָל בְּנוֹ אֵין אָדָם מַסִּיחַ דַּעְתּוֹ מִבֵּיתוֹ לָדוּר אֵצֶל בְּנוֹ, שֶׁמָּא תִּתְקוֹטֵט כַּלָּתוֹ עִמּוֹ וְיֵצֵא. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


אצל בתו – especially his daughter, for a person acts [in such a way] that he lives with his father-in-law, but one’s son, a person does not dismiss from mind his [own] house (i.e., to go back home) to dwell with his son, for perhaps his daughter-in-law will quarrel with him and he will leave. But the Halakha is according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:6:1)


**בּוֹר שֶׁבֵּין שְׁתֵּי חֲצֵרוֹת.** חֶצְיוֹ בָּזֶה וְחֶצְיוֹ בָּזֶה, אֵין מְמַלְּאִין מִמֶּנּוּ, שֶׁזֶּה מְמַלֵּא מֵרְשׁוּת חֲבֵרוֹ וְאָסוּר אִם לֹא עֵרְבוּ הַחֲצֵרוֹת זוֹ עִם זוֹ:


בור שבין שתי חצרות – half of it (i.e., the cistern) in this one (i.e., courtyard) and half of it in that onel we don’t fill from it [on Shabbat], for this one (i.e., courtyard) fills from the domain of his neighbor and prohibits it if the courtyards did not make an Eruv between each other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:6:2)


**בֵּין מִלְמַעְלָה.** שֶׁהָיוּ תִּשְׁעָה טְפָחִים שֶׁל מְחִצָּה לְמַעְלָה מִן הַמַּיִם וְטֶפַח אֶחָד מְשֻׁקָּע בַּמַּיִם:


בין מלמעלה – that there were ten handbreadths of the partition above from the water and one handbreadth sunk in the water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:6:3)


**בֵּין מִלְּמַטָּה.** שֶׁהָיָה תִּשְׁעָה טְפָחִים מְשֻׁקָּעִים בְּתוֹךְ הַמַּיִם וְטֶפַח אֶחָד נִרְאֶה לְמַעְלָה:


בין למטה – that there were nine handbreadths sunk within the water and one handbreadth that appeared above it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:6:4)


**בֵּין מִתּוֹךְ אֹגְנוֹ.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין הַמְּחִצָּה נוֹגַעַת בַּמַּיִם כְּלָל, דְּקֻלָּא הִיא שֶׁהֵקֵלּוּ חֲכָמִים בְּמַיִם דְּאָמְרִינַן גּוּד אַחִית מְחִצָּתָא, וּבִלְבַד שֶׁתְּהֵא בְּתוֹךְ אֹגֶן הַבּוֹר, שֶׁתֵּרָאֶה כְּהַפְסָקָה:


בין מתוך אוגנו (whether within its rim) – even though its partition does not touch the water, the general principle is a leniency that the Sages were lenient with the water that they said, “imagine that its partition continued downward” (see Tractate Eruvin 87a), as long as it would be within the rim of the cistern that it would appear like an interruption.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:6:5)


**מִן הַכֹּתֶל שֶׁבֵּינֵיהֶם.** שֶׁמַּפְסֶקֶת בֵּין הַחֲצֵרוֹת וּמַפְסֶקֶת עַל פְּנֵי הַבּוֹר, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינָהּ נִכְנֶסֶת תּוֹךְ אָגְנוֹ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


מן הכותל שביניהם – that interrupts between the courtyards and interrupts oer the face of the cistern and even on it the mouth that does not enter into the rim. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:7:1)


**אַמַּת הַמַּיִם.** אֲפִלּוּ בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד כַּרְמְלִית הִיא:


אמת המים – even in the private domain, it is a Karmelit (i.e., an intermediate domain between a private domain and a public domain, established by the Sages. The Sages extended the prohibition against carrying on Shabbat from a private domain to a public domain or carerying within the public domain to include certain areas which resemble a public domain. Any open area larger than four square handbreadths that is not a public thoroughfare is in the category of a Karmelit, i.e., fields, seas, rivers, alleyways and lanes. On Shabbat it is prohibited to caerry four cubits within a karmelit or to transfer from a private or public domain to a karmelit and vice versa).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:7:2)


**עָשׂוּ לָהּ מְחִצָּה.** בְּתוֹךְ אֹגְנָהּ עַל פְּנֵי רָחְבָּהּ, שֶׁהוּא נִכָּר שֶׁבִּשְׁבִיל הַמַּיִם נַעֲשֵׂית:


עשו לה מחיצה – with the rim over the face of its width which is recognized that it was made for the water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:7:3)


**בָּאַמָּה שֶׁל אָבֵל.** שֵׁם הָעִיר, וְהָיְתָה עוֹבֶרֶת בְּתוֹךְ הַחֲצֵרוֹת וּמְמַלְּאִין מִמֶּנָּה בַּחֲצֵרוֹת, דִּמְהַנְיָא לַהּ מְחִצּוֹת כֹּתֶל הֶחָצֵר הַתְּלוּיוֹת עָלֶיהָ. וְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה לְטַעֲמֵיהּ, דְּפָלֵיג נַמִּי אַבּוֹר, וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּמוֹתוֹ:


מעשה באמה של אבל – the name of a city, that would pass within the courtyards and they would fill from it in the courtyards for the partitions of the wall of the courtyard that were suspended upon it were beneficial. Bug Rabbi Yehuda, according to his reasoning who argued also regarding a cistern, but the Halakha is not according to him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:7:4)


**שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה בָהּ כַּשִּׁעוּר.** כַּרְמְלִית. שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה עָמֹק עֲשָׂרָה אוֹ לֹא הָיָה רָחָב אַרְבָּעָה, שֶׁאֵין הַמַּיִם נַעֲשִׂין רְשׁוּת לְעַצְמָן לְהֵחָשֵׁב כַּרְמְלִית אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן עָמֹק עֲשָׂרָה וְרָחָב אַרְבָּעָה. עָשׂוּ לָהּ בַּכְּנִיסָה וְלֹא בַּיְצִיאָה לֹא מְהַנֵּי מִידֵי, דְּהָא מְחֻבֶּרֶת לַמַּיִם שֶׁחוּץ לֶחָצֵר בִּיצִיאָתוֹ וְנַעֲשֵׂית הַכֹּל כַּרְמְלִית. [עָשׂוּ לָהּ בַיְצִיאָה וְלֹא בַּכְּנִיסָה נַמִּי לֹא מְהַנֵּי, דְּהָא מְחֻבֶּרֶת לַמַּיִם שֶׁלְּמַעְלָה חוּץ לְחָצֵר זוֹ. אֲבָל עָשׂוּ לָהּ בַּכְּנִיסָה וּבַיְצִיאָה מְהַנֵּי, שֶׁנִּרְאֶה] כְּאִלּוּ מַתְחִילִין בְּחָצֵר זוֹ:


שלא היה בה שיעור – A Karmelit, that was not ten [handbreadths] deep or was not four [handbreadths] wide, where the waters are not made into a domain into themselves to be considered a Karmelit unless it is ten [handbreadths] deep and four [handbreadths] wide. If they made it at the entrance but not at the exit, it does not benefit at all, for it connected to the water that is outside of the courtyard in its departure, and it all becomes a Karmelit; [but, if they made it – (i.e., the partition) at the exit but not at the entrance it also does not benefit, for it is connected to the water that is above outside of this courtyard. But if they made it at its entrance and at its exit, it benefits, that it appears] as if they begin in this courtyard.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:8:1)


**גְּזֻזְטְרָא.** כְּעֵין דַּף אוֹ מָקוֹם מְקֹרֶה הַיּוֹצֵא מֵרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד וּבוֹלֵט עַל גַּבֵּי הַמַּיִם:


גזוזטרא (balcony)- it is similar to board/plank or a place of its source that juts out from the private domain and protrudes over the water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:8:2)


**אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן עָשׂוּ מְחִצָּה.** כָּל סְבִיבוֹת הַגְּזוּזְטְרָא אוֹ סְבִיב נֶקֶב אַרְבָּעָה עַל אַרְבָּעָה הֶחָקוּק בְּאֶמְצְעִיתָהּ, דְּאָמְרִינַן גּוּד אַחִית מְחִצָּתָא:


אא"כ עשו מחיצה – all around he balcony or around the perforation four [handbreadths] by four [handbreadths] that is hollowed out in its idle, for we state “imagine that the partition continued downward (see again Talmud Eruvin 87a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:8:3)


**בֵּין מִלְמַעְלָה.** לְמַעְלָה מִן נֶקֶב הַגְּזוּזְטְרָא:


בין מלמעלה – above from the the perforation of the balcony.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:8:4)


**בֵּין מִלְּמַטָּה.** מְחֻבֶּרֶת לַגְּזֻזְטְרָא מִתַּחְתֶּיהָ. וְרַמְבַּ"ם פֵּרֵשׁ מִלְּמַטָּה עַל הַמַּיִם כְּנֶגֶד נֶקֶב הַגְּזוּזְטְרָא, דְּאָמְרִינַן גּוּד אַסִּיק כְּאִלּוּ הִגִּיעַ הַמְּחִצָּה לַנֶּקֶב שֶׁבַּגְּזֻזְטְרָא שֶׁמְּמַלְּאִין מִמֶּנּוּ:


בין מלמטה – attached to the balcony from below it But Maimonides explained that from below on he water corresponding to the perforation of the balcony, as we stated, “imagine that the partition continued upward [prolonged to reach the ceiling” (Talmud Eruvin 89a and Sukkah 4b), as if the partition reached to the perforation that is in the balcony that we fill [water] from it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:8:5)


**זוֹ לְמַעְלָה מִזּוֹ.** וְנֶקֶב שֶׁמְּמַלְּאִין מִמֶּנּוּ בָּעֶלְיוֹנָה מְכֻוָּן כְּנֶגֶד נֶקֶב הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה:


זו למעלה מזו – and the perforation that we fill fro at the top is directed opposite the lower perforation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:8:6)


**עָשׂוּ לָעֶלְיוֹנָה.** שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּתְּפוּ בְּנֵי הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה עִם בְּנֵי הָעֶלְיוֹנָה לַעֲשׂוֹת מְחִצָּה בָּעֶלְיוֹנָה וְלֹא עָשׂוּ בַּתַּחְתּוֹנָה:


עשו לעליונה – that the members of he lower area made a partnership with those who lived in the upper area to make a partition in the upper area, but not in the lower area.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:8:7)


**שְׁתֵּיהֶן אֲסוּרוֹת עַד שֶׁיְּעָרֵבוּ.** שֶׁכֵּיוָן שֶׁבְּנֵי הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה שֻׁתָּפִין בִּמְחִצָּה זוֹ שֶׁל עֶלְיוֹנָה אוֹסְרִין עֲלֵיהֶם. וְאִם עָשׂוּ לַתַּחְתּוֹנָה וְלֹא עָשׂוּ לָעֶלְיוֹנָה אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין לָעֶלְיוֹנָה שֻׁתָּפוּת בַּמְּחִצָּה שֶׁל תַּחְתּוֹנָה שְׁתֵּיהֶן אֲסוּרוֹת. הָעֶלְיוֹנָה אֲסוּרָה שֶׁהֲרֵי אֵין לָהּ מְחִצָּה, וְהַתַּחְתּוֹנָה הוֹאִיל וְיֵשׁ דֶּרֶךְ לָעֶלְיוֹנָה עָלֶיהָ שֶׁגַּם הִיא מְמַלֵּאת מַיִם מִשָּׁם אוֹסֶרֶת עָלֶיהָ עַד שֶׁיְּעָרְבוּ עִמָּהּ. וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי כְּשֶׁשְּׁתֵּי הַגְּזוּזְטְרָאוֹת מֻפְלָגוֹת זוֹ מִזּוֹ, שֶׁיֵּשׁ גָּבוֹהַּ עֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים אוֹ יוֹתֵר בֵּין זוֹ לְזוֹ, אֲבָל אִם שְׁתֵּיהֶן בְּתוֹךְ עֲשָׂרָה, אֲפִלּוּ עָשׂוּ שְׁתֵּיהֶן מְחִצָּה לְעוֹלָם אוֹסְרוֹת זוֹ עַל זוֹ עַד שֶׁיְּעָרְבוּ:


שתיהן אסורות עד שיערבו – for since those who dwelled below are partners in this partition of the upper level, it forbids them. But if they made [a partition] for the lower section, but not make one for the upper area, even though for the upper area there was no partnership in the partition of the lower area, both of them are prohibited. The upper area is prohibited for they have no partition, and the lower araa, even that there is a path to the upper area through it, for it too fills waer from there, it forbids it until they make an Eruv with it. But these words, that the two balconies are removed from each other when it is ten handbreadths high or more between one and the other, but if both of them are within ten [handbreadths] even if both of them made a partition, they will alwas forbid one on the other until they make an Eruv.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:9:1)


**אֵין שׁוֹפְכִין לְתוֹכָהּ מַיִם בְּשַׁבָּת.** דִּבְאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת, הַמַּיִם שֶׁאָדָם עָשׂוּי לְהִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בָּהֶן בְּכָל יוֹם נִבְלָעִים שָׁם בִּמְקוֹמָם וְאֵין יוֹצְאִין לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, פָּחוֹת מֵאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת, אֵין נִבְלָעִים בִּמְקוֹמָם וְיוֹצְאִין לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים:


אין שופכין לתוכה מים בשבת – that in the four cubits, the water that a person is used to using on each day is absorbed there in their place, and doesn’t go out into the public thoroughfare, but less than four cubits, the waters are not absorbed in their place and go out to the public thoroughfare.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:9:2)


**עוּקָה.** גֻּמָּא:


עוקה – hole.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:9:3)


**מַחֲזֶקֶת סָאתַיִם.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהָאָדָם עָשׂוּי לְהִסְתַּפֵּק סָאתַיִם מַיִם בְּכָל יוֹם:


מחזקת סאתים – because a person usually consumes two Seah of water a day (from the supply in the cistern – see Talmud Eruvin 88a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:9:4)


**מִן הַנֶּקֶב וּלְמַטָּה.** שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הֶחָלָל שֶׁל הָעוּקָה מַחֲזִיק סָאתַיִם קֹדֶם שֶׁיַּגִּיעוּ הַמַּיִם אֶל הַנֶּקֶב שֶׁעַל שְׂפָתָהּ שֶׁבּוֹ מְקַלְּחִין לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים:


מן הנקב ולמטה – so that the hollow of the hole supports two Seah prior to the water reaching the perforation that is on its rim that causes an uninterrupted flow into the public thoroughfare.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:9:5)


**בֵּין מִבִּפְנִים בֵּין מִבַּחוּץ.** בֵּין שֶׁעָשָׂה הָעוּקָה בֶּחָצֵר בֵּין שֶׁעֲשָׂאָהּ בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים:


בין מבפנים בין מבחוץ – whether he made the hole in the courtyard or whether it was made in the public thoroughfare.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:9:6)


**צָרִיךְ לִקְמוֹר.** לַעֲשׂוֹת עָלֶיהָ כְּמִין כִּפָּה מְכֻסָּה מִלְּמַעְלָה כְּדֵי שֶׁתְּהֵא חֲלוּקָה מֵרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים:


צריך לקמור – to make upon it a kind of arch covering from above in order that it is separated from the public thoroughfare.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:10:1)


**בִּיב.** חָרִיץ הֶעָשׂוּי כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּקַלְּחוּ הַמַּיִם [שֶׁשּׁוֹפְכִין] בֶּחָצֵר לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים. וְהוּא קָמוּר בְּמֶשֶׁךְ אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, דִּבְאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת יֵשׁ שִׁעוּר לִבְלֹעַ בָּהֶן סָאתַיִם מַיִם שֶׁאָדָם מִסְתַּפֵּק בְּכָל יוֹם:


ביב (pipe, canal) – a trench that is made in order that the water will cause an uninterrupted flow [when they pour] it in the courtyard to the public thoroughfare, and It is arched/covered over through four cubits to the public thoroughfare, for in four cubits there is a limit to absorb in them the two Seah of water that a person consumes on each day.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:10:2)


**שׁוֹפְכִין לְתוֹכוֹ.** דְּתָיְמֵי מַיָּא וְאֵין יוֹצְאִין לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים:


שופכין לתוכו – that the water terminates and does not go to the public thoroughfare (see Tractate Eruvin 88a-b).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:10:3)


**לֹא יִשְׁפֹּךְ עַל פִּי הַבִּיב.** דְּכֵיוָן דִּבְגַוֵּיהּ שָׁפִיךְ מְקַלְּחֵי לְהֶדְיָא וְנַפְקֵי לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וְהָרוֹאֶה אוֹמֵר שֶׁשְּׁפָכָן סָמוּךְ לִיצִיאָתוֹ לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


לא ישפוך על פי הביב – for it is poured by a living person, it flows in an uninterrupted flow explicitly to the public thoroughfare, and a person who sees it states that they poured them near his exit to the public thoroughfare. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:10:4)


**מִצְטָרְפִין לְאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת.** אִם יֵשׁ בֶּחָצֵר עִם הָאַכְסַדְרָה אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת עַל אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת מִצְטָרְפִין, וּמֻתָּר לִשְׁפֹּךְ לְתוֹכָהּ מַיִם וְאֵין צָרִיךְ עוּקָה:


מצטרפין לד' אמות – if there is in the courtyard with the covered place in front of the house that is four cubits by four cubits, they combine, and it is permitted to pour water into it and there is no need for a cavity/pit.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:11:1)


**דְּיוֹטָאוֹת.** עֲלִיּוֹת זוֹ כְּנֶגֶד זוֹ וְחָצֵר פְּחוּתָה מֵאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת לִפְנֵיהֶן שֶׁשּׁוֹפְכִין בָּהּ מֵימֵיהֶן:


דיוטות upper compartments – opposite each other with a common yard between them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:11:2)


**מִקְצָתָן.** בְּנֵי הַדְּיוּטָא הָאַחַת עָשׂוּ עוּקָה בֶּחָצֵר וּבְנֵי הָאַחֶרֶת לֹא עָשׂוּ:


מקצתן – the members of the upper stories one opposite the other made a cavity in the courtyard and the members of the other did not do so.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 8:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/8:11:3)


**וְאֶת שֶׁלֹּא עָשׂוּ עוּקָה אֲסוּרִין.** כָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא עֵרְבוּ. דְּאִי שָׁרֵית לְהוּ לְהָנֵי עַל יְדֵי עוּקָה דְּהָנָךְ, אָתוּ לְאַפּוֹקֵי מָאנֵי עִם הַשּׁוֹפְכִים מִן הַבָּתִּים וּלְהוֹרִידָן לֶחָצֵר בְּהֶדְיָא עַד שְׂפַת הָעוּקָה בְּרַגְלֵיהֶם, וְקָא מְטַלְטֵל בְּחָצֵר דְּלֹא עֵרְבוּ בָּהּ:


ואת שלא עשו עוקה אסורין – all the time that they didn’t make an Eruv. For if they permitted them to those through the cavity of the others, they would come to exclude the utensils/objects with those who pour from the houses and to lower them to the courtyard explicitly until the rim of the cavity with their feet. But they carry in the courtyard that they didn’t make an Eruv for.



## Chapter 9


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:1:1)


**כָּל גַּגּוֹת הָעִיר רְשׁוּת אַחַת.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁדִּיּוּרִים חֲלוּקִים לְמַטָּה לִשְׁנֵי בְּנֵי אָדָם, מִכָּל מָקוֹם הַגַּגּוֹת שֶׁאֵין תַּשְׁמִישָׁן תָּדִיר אֵין בָּהֶן חֲלֻקַּת רְשׁוּת, וְכֵלִים שֶׁשָּׁבְתוּ בְּגַג זֶה מֻתָּר לְהוֹצִיאָן לָזֶה:


כל גגות העיר רשות אחד – and even though that the inhabitants below are divided into two [kinds of] people, nevertheless, the roofs whose usage is not frequent, they do not have the division of the domain and utensils that were kept for the Sabbath on this roof, it is permitted to reove them to the other [roof].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:1:2)


**וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא גַג גָּבוֹהַּ מֵחֲבֵרוֹ עֲשָׂרָה.** דְּאִם חָלוּק מֵהֶן בְּגֹבַהּ עֲשָׂרָה אָסוּר לְטַלְטֵל מִמֶּנּוּ לַגַּגִּין, גְּזֵרָה מִשּׁוּם תֵּל גָּבוֹהַּ עֲשָׂרָה וְרָחָב אַרְבָּעָה בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, דְּלֹא לֵיתֵי לְכַתֵּף עָלָיו:


ובלבד שלא יהא גג גבוה מחבירו י' -that if it is divided from them at the height of ten [handbreadths] it is forbidden to carry from it to the [other] roofs, as a decree because of a mound/hill that is ten [handbreadths] high and four [handbreadths] wide in the public thoroughfare [which is the private domain] that they would not come to carry upon him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 9:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:1:3)


**כָּל אֶחָד רְשׁוּת לְעַצְמוֹ.** וְאִם לֹא עֵרְבוּ דִּיּוּרִים שֶׁלְּמַטָּה אָסוּר לְטַלְטֵל מִזֶּה לָזֶה:


כל אחד רשות לעצמו – if the inhabitants who are below did not make an Eruv, it is prohibited to carry from this [roof] to that [roof].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 9:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:1:4)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן.** מֵקֵל מִכֻּלָּן וְקָאָמַר, גַּגּוֹת וַחֲצֵרוֹת וְקַרְפֵּיפוֹת שֶׁאֵינָן יוֹתֵר מִבֵּית סָאתַיִם, הוֹאִיל וְכֻלָּן אֵין תַּשְׁמִישָׁן מְיֻחָד וְתָדִיר, רְשׁוּת אַחַת הֵן וּמְטַלְטְלִין מִזֶּה לָזֶה בְּלֹא עֵרוּב, וַאֲפִלּוּ הָיוּ הַגַּגּוֹת גְּבוֹהוֹת יוֹתֵר מֵעֲשָׂרָה אוֹ נְמוּכוֹת יוֹתֵר מֵעֲשָׂרָה, דְּלֵית לֵיהּ לְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן עֵרוּבֵי חֲצֵרוֹת אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם כְּלֵי הַבַּיִת:


ר' שמעון – is more lenient than all of them, and he states, that roofs and courtyards and outside areas [of seventy cubits added to city limits for purposes of Sabbath distances] which ae not more than a field requiring one Seah of seed since for all of hem their usage is not unique and frequent, they are one domain, and we carry from one to the other without an Eruv, and evn if there roofs that were higher than ten [handbreadths] or much lower than ten [handbreadths], for Rabbi Shimon does not hold by [the concept] of עירובי חצרות/borders of courtyards except because of the utensils of the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 9:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:1:5)


**לַכֵּלִים שֶׁשָּׁבְתוּ בְּתוֹכָן.** כֵּלִים שֶׁשָּׁבְתוּ בְּאַחַת מֵהֶן, מוֹצִיאִים אוֹתָן מִזֶּה לָזֶה:


כלים ששבתו בתוכן – utensils that were spending Shabbat/made their Sabbath camp in one oft hem, we remove them one to the other.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 9:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:1:6)


**אֲבָל לֹא לַכֵּלִים שֶׁשָּׁבְתוּ בְתוֹךְ הַבָּיִת.** וֶהֱבִיאָן לֶחָצֵר עַל יְדֵי שֶׁעֵרְבוּ בְּנֵי הֶחָצֵר, אָסוּר לְהוֹצִיאָן מֵחָצֵר זוֹ לְחָצֵר אַחֶרֶת אִם לֹא עֵרְבוּ שְׁתֵּי הַחֲצֵרוֹת יַחַד. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ולא לכלים ששבת בתוך הבית – and he brought them out to the courtyard through the members of the courtyard making an Eruv, it is prohibited to remove them from this courtyard to another courtyard if they didn’t make an Eruv for the two courtyards together. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:2:1)


**הַגָּדוֹל מֻתָּר.** לְהוֹצִיא לוֹ כְּלֵי בַּיִת שֶׁלְּמַטָּה, וְאֵין בְּנֵי הַקָּטָן אוֹסְרִין עָלָיו. דִּלְגַבֵּי גָּדוֹל הַךְ פִּרְצָה פִּתְחָא הִיא וְשָׁרֵי בְּאוֹתָן גִּפּוּפִים הָעוֹדְפִים מְעַט מִכָּאן וּמְעַט מִכָּאן כְּמַעֲקֶה שֶׁסְּבִיב הַגַּגּוֹת, וַחֲשִׁיבָא סְתִימָה, וּכְגוֹן שֶׁאֵין הַפִּרְצָה יוֹתֵר מֵעֶשֶׂר. אֲבָל קָטָן אָסוּר לְהוֹצִיא לוֹ כְּלֵי הַבַּיִת, דִּבְנֵי גָּדוֹל אָסְרֵי עֲלֵיהּ, שֶׁהֲרֵי נִפְרָץ לוֹ בִּמְלֹאוֹ:


הגדול מותר – to remove for himself a house utensil that is below, but the members of the small [roof] are forbidden on it. For relating to the large [roof] this breach is an opening and it is permitted with those railings/rims that are a bit larger from this side and a bit from that side, like a railing that is around the roofs and is considered a closing/cover. But, as for example, that the breach is not more than ten [handbreadths]. But a small [roof], it is prohibited to remove for him utensils of the house for the members of the large [roof] forbid him, for behold it was broken through in its entirety.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:2:2)


**הַגְּדוֹלָה מֻתֶּרֶת.** בִּשְׁבִיל הַגִּפּוּפִין שֶׁנִּשְׁאֲרוּ לָהּ מִכָּאן וּמִכָּאן הָוֵי הַךְ פִּרְצָה כְּפֶתַח:


הגדולה מותרת – [the large courtyard is permitted] for the railings/rims that remained tto it from one side and from the other, for this breach is an opening.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 9:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:2:3)


**וְהַקְּטַנָּה אֲסוּרָה.** שֶׁהֲרֵי נִפְרְצָה בִּמְלֹאָהּ. וְדַוְקָא כְּשֶׁנִּפְרְצָה קֹדֶם הַשַּׁבָּת, אֲבָל אִם נִפְרְצָה בְּשַׁבָּת אֲפִלּוּ הַקְּטַנָּה מֻתֶּרֶת, שֶׁכֵּיוָן שֶׁהֻתְּרָה לְמִקְצָת שַׁבָּת קֹדֶם שֶׁנִּפְרְצָה הֻתְּרָה לְכֻלָּהּ שַׁבָּת:


והקטנה אסורה – for behold, it was breached entirely. And specifically when it was breached prior to Shabbat, but if was breached on Shabbat, even the small courtyard is permitted [for carrying], for sice it was permitted for part of Shabbat prior to its being breached, it was permitted for all of Shabbat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 9:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:2:4)


**חָצֵר שֶׁנִּפְרְצָה לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים.** שֶׁנָּפַל הַכֹּתֶל הַפּוֹנֶה לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים בִּמְלֹאוֹ אוֹ בְּיוֹתֵר מֵעֶשֶׂר:


חצר שנפרצה לרה"ר – that the wall fell that is facing into the public thoroughfare completely, or more than ten [handbreadths].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 9:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:2:5)


**חַיָּב.** דְּכִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים הִיא:


חייב – it is just like the public thoroughfare.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 9:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:2:6)


**פָּטוּר.** אֲבָל אָסוּר, דְּלָאו רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים הִיא אֶלָּא כַּרְמְלִית. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


פטור – [he is exempt] but also forbidden, for it is not the public thoroughfare, but rather a Karmelit. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:3:1)


**מִשְּׁתֵּי רוּחוֹתֶיהָ.** מֵרוּחַ אַחַת שֶׁתּוֹפֶסֶת שְׁתֵּי רוּחוֹתֶיהָ, כְּגוֹן שֶׁנִּפְרְצָה בְּקֶרֶן זָוִית, אֲפִלּוּ אֵין בַּפִּרְצָה עֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת, הָכָא בְּקֶרֶן זָוִית לֹא חָשֵׁיב כְּפֶתַח, דְּפִתְחָא בְּקֶרֶן זָוִית לֹא עָבְדֵי אֱינָשֵׁי. וּפִרְצָה בְּיוֹתֵר מֵעֶשֶׂר אֲפִלּוּ מֵרוּחַ אַחַת אוֹסֶרֶת:


משתי רוחותיו – from one direction that takes hold of two directions, as for example, when the corner piece is breached, even when there aren’t ten cubits in the breach here in the corner piece it is not considered as an opening, for an opening in the corner piece, people don’t use. But a breach that is greater than ten [cubits], even from one direction [only], prohibits.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 9:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:3:2)


**וְכֵן בַּיִת שֶׁנִּפְרַץ מִשְּׁתֵּי רוּחוֹתָיו.** שֶׁנִּפְרַץ בְּקֶרֶן זָוִית וְנָפַל קְצָת מִכֹּתֶל זֶה וּקְצָת מִכֹּתֶל זֶה, וְאֵין הַתִּקְרָה פְּרוּשָׂה וּמְתוּחָה עַל מְקוֹם הַפִּרְצָה, אֲבָל אִם הַתִּקְרָה פְּרוּשָׂה עַל מְקוֹם הַפִּרְצָה אֵין הַפִּרְצָה אוֹסֶרֶת בַּבַּיִת, דְּאָמְרִינַן פִּי תִקְרָה יוֹרֵד וְסוֹתֵם:


וכן בית שנפרץ מב' רוחותיו – that it was breached in the corner piece and part of this wall and part of the other wall fell, and the ceiling/roofing is not spread and fastened on the place of the breach, but if the roofing is spread over the place of the breach, the breach does not prohibit in the house, as we said, the mouth of the roof descends and stops up/closes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 9:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:3:3)


**מֻתָּרִין לְאוֹתָהּ שַׁבָּת.** הוֹאִיל וְהֻתַּר מִקְצָתוֹ:


מותרין לאותה שבת – for since it was permitted for part of it (i.e., the Sabbath).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 9:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:3:4)


**לֶעָתִיד לָבֹא.** לַשַּׁבָּת הַבָּאָה:


לעתיד לבא – for next Shabbat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 9:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:3:5)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר אִם מֻתָּרִין כוּ'.** כְּלוֹמַר כְּשֵׁם שֶׁאֲסוּרִין לֶעָתִיד לָבֹא כָּךְ אֲסוּרִין לְאוֹתָהּ שַׁבָּת. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי, וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן הֻתְּרָה מִקְצָת שַׁבָּת הֻתְּרָה כֻּלָּהּ אֶלָּא לְעִנְיַן עֵרוּב, שֶׁדָּבָר שֶׁהֻתַּר עַל יְדֵי עֵרוּב לְמִקְצָת שַׁבָּת, וְנוֹלַד בְּאוֹתָהּ שַׁבָּת דָּבָר שֶׁהָיָה רָאוּי שֶׁעַל יָדוֹ יִתְבַּטֵּל הָעֵרוּב, אֵין הָעֵרוּב בָּטֵל, שֶׁכֵּיוָן שֶׁהֻתְּרָה מִקְצָת שַׁבָּת הֻתְּרָה כֻּלָּהּ, אֲבָל מָקוֹם שֶׁהָיוּ בּוֹ מְחִצּוֹת קֹדֶם הַשַּׁבָּת וְנִפְרְצוּ מְחִצּוֹתָיו בְּשַׁבָּת לֹא אָמְרִינַן בֵּיהּ הוֹאִיל וְהֻתְּרָה מִקְצָת שַׁבָּת הֻתְּרָה כֻּלָּהּ:


ר' יוסי ומר אם מותרים כו' – meaning to say, in the same way that it is prohibited [to carry] for the next Shabbat, so it is prohibited for that Shabbat. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi. But we don’t say that [since] it was permitted for part of the Sabbath, it is permitted for all [of it], other than regarding the matter of the Eruv. For something that was permitted via [construction] of an Eruv for part of Shabbat, and it resulted that on that Shabbat something that was worthy through it that the Eruv would be abolished/undone, the Eruv is not abolished, for since it was permitted for part of the Shabbat, it was permitted for all of it, but in a place which had partitions prior to the Sabbath and its partitions were breached on the Sabbath, we don’t say regarding it, that since it was permitted for a portion of the Sabbath, it is permitted for all of it.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:4:1)


**עַל גַּבֵּי שְׁנֵי בָתִּים.** וְהֵם בִּשְׁנֵי צִדֵּי רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, מְטַלְטְלִין תַּחַת הָעֲלִיָּה, דְּפִי תִקְרָה מִכָּאן וּמִכָּאן יוֹרֵד וְסוֹתֵם:


על גבי שני בתים – and they are on two sides of the public thoroughfare, we carry underneath the upper room, for the mouth of the ceiling from each side descends and closes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:4:2)


**וְכֵן גְּשָׁרִים הַמְפֻלָּשִׁים.** וְיֵשׁ לָהֶם מְחִצּוֹת מִלְּמַטָּה בִּשְׁנֵי הַצְּדָדִים:


וכן גשרים המפולשים (bridges under which there is an open passage) – and they have partitions from below on the two sides.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 9:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/9:4:3)


**מְעָרְבִין לְמָבוֹי הַמְפֻלָּשׁ.** הוֹאִיל וְאִיכָּא שְׁתֵּי מְחִצּוֹת, דְּקָסָבַר מָבוֹי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ שְׁתֵּי מְחִצּוֹת מִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא הָוֵי רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


מערבין למבוי המפולש (for an alleyway which is a thoroughfare) – for since there are two partitions, that he (i.e., Rabbi Yehuda) holds that an alleyway that has two partitions, according to the [laws of the] Torah is a private domain. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



## Chapter 10


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:1:1)


**הַמּוֹצֵא תְפִלִּין.** בַּשָּׂדֶה בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁאֵינָן מִשְׁתַּמְּרִים:


המוצא תפילין – in a field in a place where they (i.e., the Tefillin) are not guarded.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:1:2)


**זוּג זוּג.** אֶחָד בָּרֹאשׁ וְאֶחָד בַּזְּרוֹעַ כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁלּוֹבֵשׁ בַּחֹל, וְהַיְנוּ זוּג, וְחוֹזֵר תָּמִיד וּמַכְנִיס זוּג זוּג עַד שֶׁיַּכְנִיס אֶת כֻּלָּן. קָסָבַר שַׁבָּת זְמַן תְּפִלִּין הוּא, אֶלָּא שֶׁחֲכָמִים גָּזְרוּ עֲלֵיהֶן גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא תִּפָּסֵק רְצוּעָה וִיבִיאֵם בְּיָדוֹ, וְאִם יִלְבַּשׁ יוֹתֵר מִזּוּג אִיכָּא בַּל תּוֹסִיף וְאָתֵי אִסּוּר בַּל תּוֹסִיף וּמַשְׁוֵי לְהוּ עֲלֵיהּ כְּמַשְּׂאוֹי, וְרַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל סָבַר שַׁבָּת לָאו זְמַן תְּפִלִּין הוּא, וְהַיְנוּ טַעֲמָא דְּשָׁרוּ לֵיהּ רַבָּנָן בְּהַצָּלָה מִשּׁוּם דְּתַכְשִׁיט הוּא, הִלְכָּךְ מַכְנִיסָן שְׁנֵי זוּגוֹת שְׁנֵי זוּגוֹת, דִּשְׁנֵי זוּגוֹת הָוֵי תַּכְשִׁיט, לְפִי שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ מָקוֹם יֵשׁ בָּרֹאשׁ לְהָנִיחַ בּוֹ שְׁנֵי תְּפִלִּין, וְכָל כַּמָּה דַּהֲוֵי תַּכְשִׁיט שָׁרֵי. וּמִשּׁוּם בַּל תּוֹסִיף לֵיכָּא הָכָא, דְּאֵין כָּאן מִצְוַת תְּפִלִּין כְּלָל אֶלָּא כִּשְׁאָר מַלְבּוּשׁ דְּעָלְמָא, וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל:


זוג זוג – one for the head and another for the arm, in the manner that one dons them on weekdays, and that is as a pair, and then he always returns them and brings them in pair by pair until he brings in all of them, as he (i.e., the anonymous Tanna of our Mishnah) holds that Shabbat is a time for Tefillin, but the Sages made a decree upon them lest a strap breaks and he brings them with his hand, and if he were to don more than one pair, there is [the problem] of “not adding [to the commandment]” and this prohibition of “not adding” compares them upon him as a burden. But Rabban Gamaliel holds that Shabbat is not a time for Tefillin and that is the reason why the Rabbis permit saving them because it is adornment; hence, we bring them in two pairs at a time. For two pairs are an adornment since we taught that where there is on the head a place to wear two Tefillin, and all the while that they are an adornment, it is permitted, but because of [the prohibition of]“not adding” doesn’t apply here, for there is no commandment of Tefillin here at all, other than the rest of mere clothing. But the Halakha is not according to Rabban Gamaliel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:1:3)


**בִּישָׁנוֹת.** שֶׁנִּכָּר הַקֶּשֶׁר, דְּוַדַּאי תְּפִלִּין הֵן וְיֵשׁ בָּהֶן קְדֻשָּׁה, וְאָסוּר לְהַנִּיחָן בִּמְקוֹם בִּזָּיוֹן. אֲבָל בַּחֲדָשׁוֹת אֵין מְחַלְּלִין שַׁבָּת עֲלֵיהֶן לְהַכְנִיסָן, דִּלְמָא קָמֵעַ בְּעָלְמָא נִינְהוּ, וְאֵין קְדֻשָּׁה אֶלָּא בַּעֲשׂוּיִין כְּהִלְכָתָן לִשְׁמָן:


בישנות – where the knot is recognized (i.e., in old Tefillin) that they are definitely Tefillin and they have sanctity, and it is prohibited to place them in a contemptable location, but new Tefillin, we do not desecrate the Sabbath for them to bring them in for perhaps there are a mere amulet, and there is no sanctity other when they are made according to Jewish law for their purpose [of being Tefillin].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:1:4)


**צְבוּתִים.** קְשׁוּרִים כָּל זוּג זוּג לְעַצְמוֹ:


צבותים – each pair of Tefillin is tied to itself.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:1:5)


**כְּרוּכִים.** הַרְבֵּה בְּיַחַד:


כרוכים – many pairs [of Tefillin are tied] together.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:1:6)


**מַחְשִׁיךְ עֲלֵיהֶן.** יוֹשֵׁב וּמְשַׁמְּרָם שָׁם עַד שֶׁתֶּחְשַׁךְ, וּמִשֶּׁחֲשֵׁכָה מַכְנִיסָן כֻּלָּם בְּיַחַד. וּכְגוֹן שֶׁהֵם מְרֻבִּים, שֶׁאִם יְבִיאֵם זוּג זוּג לֹא תִכְלֶה הֲבָאָתָן קֹדֶם הַלַּיְלָה, שֶׁאִם הֵם בְּעִנְיָן שֶׁתִּכְלֶה הַכְנָסָתָן לַבַּיִת קֹדֶם הַלַּיְלָה מַכְנִיסָן זוּג זוּג כִּדְאָמְרָן:


מחשיך עליהן – he sits and guards them until it becomes dark, and once it becomes dark, he brings all of them in together as in the case when they are many. For if he were to bring them pair by pair, he would not complete his bringing them in prior to the night, and if they are in a manner that he would complete bringing them in to the house before the night, he brings them in pair by pair as we stated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:1:7)


**וּבְסַכָּנָה.** שֶׁגָּזְרוּ שְׁמָד שֶׁלֹּא לְהָנִיחַ תְּפִלִּין. וּמַתְנִיתִין חַסּוֹרֵי מְחַסְרָא וְהָכִי קָתָנֵי, בַּמֶּה דְּבָרִים אֲמוּרִים, בְּסַכָּנָה שֶׁל שְׁמָד, אֲבָל אִם מִתְיָרֵא לִשְׁהוֹת שָׁם מִפְּנֵי הַלִּסְטִים, מוֹלִיכָן פָּחוֹת פָּחוֹת מֵאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת:


ובסכנה – when they decreed religious persecution [against the Jewish religion] to not wear Tefillin, and our Mishnah is deficient and should be read as follows: when is this said, in the danger of religious persecution, but if he fears to delay there because of robbers, he carries them less than four cubits.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:2:1)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר נוֹתְנָן לַחֲבֵרוֹ.** וְאֵינוֹ מוֹלִיכָן פָּחוֹת [פָּחוֹת] מֵאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת כְּשֶׁמִּתְיָרֵא מִפְּנֵי לִסְטִים, גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יַעֲבִירֵם מִתְּחִלַּת אַרְבַּע לְסוֹף אַרְבַּע. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ר"ש אומר נותנן לחבירו – and he doesn’t bring them in less [less] than four cupids for he fears from robbers, it was made a decree lest he cause them to pass from the beginning of four [cubits] to the end of four [cubits], and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Shimon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:2:2)


**וְכֵן בְּנוֹ.** שֶׁיְּלָדְתּוֹ אִמּוֹ בַּשָּׂדֶה בְּשַׁבָּת:


וכן בנו – that his mother gave birth to in the field on Shabbat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:2:3)


**נוֹתְנוֹ לַחֲבֵרוֹ.** וְהָא עֲדִיפָא מִלְּטַלְטְלוֹ פָּחוֹת פָּחוֹת מֵאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת:


נותנו לחבירו –one gives him to his fellow, for this is preferable than to carry him a bit less than four cubits.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:2:4)


**אֲפִלּוּ חוּץ לַתְּחוּם.** בְּחָבִית שֶׁל הֶפְקֵר מַיְרֵי, דְּאִי יֵשׁ לָהּ בְּעָלִים הָא קָיְמָא לָן הַבְּהֵמָה וְהַכֵּלִים כְּרַגְלֵי הַבְּעָלִים, וְסָבַר רַבִּי יְהוּדָה נִכְסֵי הֶפְקֵר לֹא קָנוּ שְׁבִיתָה:


אפילו חוץ לתחום – it speaks of an ownerless earthen wine jug, for if it has owners we surely hold that the animals and utensils are like legs of the animals, and Rabbi Yehuda holds that ownerless possessions do not acquire a place to be the center of Sabbath movements.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:2:5)


**אָמְרוּ לוֹ.** רַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן נוּרִי הוּא דְּאָמַר לַהּ, דְּשָׁמְעִינַן לֵיהּ דְּאָמַר נִכְסֵי הֶפְקֵר קוֹנִין שְׁבִיתָה בִּמְקוֹמָן:


אמרו לו – Rabbi Yohanan ben Nuri (see Talmud Eruvin 97b) is the one who states this for we understand from him that ownerless possessions do acquire a place to be the center of Sabbath movements in their place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:2:6)


**לֹא תְהַלֵּךְ זוֹ יוֹתֵר מֵרַגְלֵי בְעָלֶיהָ.** כְּלוֹמַר אִם הָיוּ לָהּ בְּעָלִים וְלֹא עֵרְבוּ לֹא הָיְתָה מְהַלֶּכֶת אֶלָּא אַלְפַּיִם אַמָּה, הַשְׁתָּא נַמִּי לֹא תֵלֵךְ אֶלָּא אַלְפַּיִם אַמָּה מִמְּקוֹם שְׁבִיתָתָהּ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


לא תהלך זה יותר מרגלי בעליה – that is to say, if they had owners and they did not create a symbolical community of residence by an Eruv, he would not walk more than two-thousand cubits; here too, he should not walk other than two-thousand cubits from the place where he appointed a place to be the center of Sabbath movements.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:3:1)


**הַקּוֹרֵא בַסֵּפֶר.** כָּל סְפָרִים שֶׁלָּהֶם הָיוּ נִגְלָלִים כְּסֵפֶר תּוֹרָה שֶׁלָּנוּ:


הקורא בספר – all of their books were rolled like our Torah scrolls.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:3:2)


**הָאַסְקֻפָּה.** כְּעֵין אִצְטַבָּא שֶׁלִּפְנֵי פֶּתַח הַבַּיִת, וְכַרְמְלִית הִיא:
