האסקופה – a kind of balcony/portico that is in front of the entrance of the house and it is a marked off plot in a public thoroughfare (which cannot be classified as either private property or as a public thoroughfare).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:3:3)


**גּוֹלְלוֹ אֶצְלוֹ.** הוֹאִיל וְרֹאשׁוֹ אֶחָד בְּיָדוֹ:


גוללו אצלו – since for one head is in his hand.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:3:4)


**לַעֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים.** הַתַּחְתּוֹנִים הַסְּמוּכִים לְקַרְקַע שֶׁל רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, דְּאִם אֵין אָגְדּוֹ בְיָדוֹ הָוֵי אִסּוּר דְּאוֹרַיְתָא, וְגָזְרִינַן אָגְדּוֹ בְּיָדוֹ אַטּוּ שֶׁאֵין אָגְדּוֹ בְיָדוֹ. וּבַגְּמָרָא פָּרֵיךְ וְהָא לֹא נָח, כְּלוֹמַר אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהִגִּיעַ לַעֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים הַתַּחְתּוֹנִים אֵין כָּאן אִסּוּרָא דְּאוֹרַיְתָא אֲפִלּוּ לֹא הָיָה אָגְדּוֹ בְיָדוֹ, שֶׁהֲרֵי לֹא נָח בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וּמְשָׁנֵי כְּגוֹן שֶׁהָיָה שָׁם כֹּתֶל מְשֻׁפָּע, וְנָח הַסֵּפֶר עַל בְּלִיטַת הַכֹּתֶל שֶׁל עֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים הַתַּחְתּוֹנִים, דַּהֲוֵי כְּאִלּוּ נָח בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וְאִם לֹא הָיָה אָגְדּוֹ בְיָדוֹ הָוֵי חִיּוּבָא דְּאוֹרַיְתָא אִם הָיָה מְבִיאוֹ אֶצְלוֹ. וּמַתְנִיתִין חַסּוֹרֵי מִחַסְּרָא וְהָכִי קָתָנֵי, הִגִּיעַ לַעֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים מִן הָאָרֶץ הוֹפְכוֹ עַל הַכְּתָב, בַּמֶּה דְּבָרִים אֲמוּרִים בְּכֹתֶל מְשֻׁפָּע דְּנָח, אֲבָל בְּכֹתֶל שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְשֻׁפָּע גּוֹלְלוֹ אֶצְלוֹ, דִּבְרֵי רַבִּי יְהוּדָה, שֶׁרַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר אֲפִלּוּ אֵינוֹ מְסֻלָּק מִן הָאָרֶץ אֶלָּא מְלֹא הַחוּט גּוֹלְלוֹ אֶצְלוֹ, דְּבָעִינַן הַנָּחָה עַל גַּבֵּי מַשֶּׁהוּ:


לעשרה טפחים – the lower parts that are near the ground of the public domain, for if the tie/knot is not in his hand, it is prohibited, according to the Torah (to roll it back), and we make the decree that the tie in his hand is on account of when the tie is not in his hand. But in the Gemara (Talmud Eruvin 98a), an object is raised for it does not rest, that is to say, even though it arrived at the ten lower handbreadths, there is no Torah prohibition here, even if he did not have the knot/tie in his hand, since it did not rest in the public domain, and we answer, such as case that there was there a slanting wall, and the scroll came to rest on the protruding part of the wall of the ten lower handbreadths, which is as if it came to rest in the public domain, but if the the knot was not in his hand, it would be a Torah obligation [that is violated] if he brings hit near him. But our Mishnah is taught deficiently and this is how it should be taught: if it arrived to the ten handbreadths from the ground, they turn over upon the written side. When is this said? With a slanting wall that rests, but with a wall that it not slanted, roll it back to himself, according to Rabbi Yehuda, for Rabbi Yehuda states that even if it is removed above the ground only a needle’s thickness, he may roll it back to himself, for we require resting upon something.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:3:5)


**הוֹפְכוֹ עַל הַכְּתָב.** הָאוֹתִיּוֹת כְּלַפֵּי הַכֹּתֶל, שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא מֻטָּל כָּל כָּךְ בְּבִזָּיוֹן, וּמַנִּיחוֹ שָׁם עַד שֶׁתֶּחְשַׁךְ:


הופכו על הכתב – that the letters will be towards the wall in order that it not lie so much in disrepute, and we leave it there until it becomes dark [on Saturday night].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:3:6)


**דָּבָר מִשּׁוּם שְׁבוּת.** כְּגוֹן זֶה שֶׁאָגְדּוֹ בְּיָדוֹ וְלֵיכָּא כִּי אִם שְׁבוּת דְּרַבָּנָן אִם בָּא לְגוֹלְלוֹ אֶצְלוֹ, שֶׁאֵין חַיָּב מִן הַתּוֹרָה אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן יָצָא הַסֵּפֶר כֻּלּוֹ מִיָּדוֹ וְנָח בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים וּבָא לְעָקְרוֹ מֵרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים וּלְהַנִּיחוֹ בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


דבר משום שבות – such as the case when the knot/tie is in his hand, and if it is not, this is the rest/abstention from any pursuit forbidden on Shabbat or a Jewish holy day/festival by the Rabbis as being out of keeping with the importance and sanctity of this day, if he comes to roll it up towards himself, for he is not liable from the Torah other than if the scroll left his hand completely and came to rest in the public domain, and he came to uproot it from the public domain and to place it in the private domain, but the Halakah is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:4:1)


**זִיז.** אֶבֶן אוֹ עֵץ הַבּוֹלֵט מִן הַכֹּתֶל עַל אֲוִיר רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, גָּבוֹהַּ עֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים מֵהַקַּרְקַע שֶׁל רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים:


זיז – a stone or wood that protrudes from the wall on the airspace of the public domain, ten handbreadths above the ground of the public domain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:4:2)


**נוֹתְנִין עָלָיו.** בְּנֵי עֲלִיָּה, וְנוֹטְלִים מִמֶּנּוּ, שֶׁאֲוִיר רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים אֵינָהּ הוֹלֶכֶת אֶלָּא עַד עֲשָׂרָה. וְדַוְקָא כֵּלִים הַנִּשְׁבָּרִים כְּגוֹן כּוֹסוֹת וּצְלוֹחִיּוֹת הוּא דְּנוֹתְנִים עָלָיו, אֲבָל כֵּלִים שֶׁאֵינָם נִשְׁבָּרִים לֹא, דִּלְמָא נָפְלוּ בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים וְאָתֵי לְאָתוֹיִינְהוּ:


נותנין עליו – those who live above and take them from him, for the airspace of the public domain does not extend other than up to ten [handbreadths] , and specifically for utensils that break like cups and glass which we place on it. But we do not do so for utensils that do not break, lest they fall into the public domain and they will go and carry them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:4:3)


**עוֹמֵד אָדָם בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד.** בַּבַּיִת אוֹ עַל הַגַּג, וְנוֹטֵל חֵפֶץ כָּאן וּמַנִּיחַ כָּאן בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין רֹאשׁוֹ וְרֻבּוֹ עוֹמֵד בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים בִּמְקוֹם הַחֵפֶץ, וְלֹא גָּזְרִינַן שֶׁמָּא יְבִיאֶנּוּ אֶצְלוֹ:


עומד אדם ברה"י – in the house or on the roof and he takes an object here and places it there in the public domain, and even though his head and most of his body stands in the public domain in the place of the object, we do not decree that perhaps he will bring it near him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:4:4)


**וּבִלְבַד שֶׁלֹּא יוֹצִיאֶנּוּ.** מֵאַרְבַּע אַמּוֹתָיו שֶׁהָיָה מֻנָּח בּוֹ:


ובלבד שלא יוציאוהו – from four cubits from where it was placed.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:5:1)


**וְיַשְׁתִּין בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים.** דְּמַפִּיק מֵרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים. וְאִם הִשְׁתִּין חַיָּב חַטָּאת. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּבָעִינַן עֲקִירָה מֵעַל גַּבֵּי מָקוֹם שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ אַרְבָּעָה עַל אַרְבָּעָה, הַשֶּׁתֶן וְכֵן הָרֹק הָווּ כְּמֻנָּחִים בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ אַרְבָּעָה עַל אַרְבָּעָה:


וישתין ברה"ר – for it goes forth from the private domain to the public domain, an d if he urinated, he is liable for a sin-offering, and even though we require uprooting from a place that has four cubits by four cubits, and urination and the spittle were as if they were lying in a place which has four cubits by four cubits.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:5:2)


**מִשֶּׁנִּתְלַשׁ רֻקּוֹ.** וְהוּא שֶׁנִּתְעַגֵּל וְנִתְהַפֵּךְ בְּפִיו. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


שנתלש רוקו – and it formed globules and rolled around in his mouth, but the Halakha Is not according to Rabbi Yehudah.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:6:1)


**וְיִשְׁתֶּה בִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים.** וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי בְּכֵלִים שֶׁהֵם צְרִיכִין לוֹ, דְּגָזְרִינַן דִּלְמָא אָתֵי לְאָתוֹיִינְהוּ, אֲבָל בְּכֵלִים שֶׁאֵין צְרִיכִין לוֹ מֻתָּר, וַאֲפִלּוּ לֹא הִכְנִיס רֹאשׁוֹ וְרֻבּוֹ בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים:


וישתה ברה"ר – and these words regard with utensils that require him, for we make the decree as perhaps a person will carry it, but with utensils that don’t require him, it is permissible, even if he didn’t bring in his head and the majority of his body into the public domain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:6:2)


**וְכֵן בְּגַת.** לְעִנְיַן מַעֲשֵׂר, שֶׁאִם הִכְנִיס רֹאשׁוֹ וְרֻבּוֹ בַּגַּת שׁוֹתֶה בְּלֹא מַעֲשֵׂר, דַּהֲוֵי שְׁתִיַּת עֲרַאי, וְחוּץ לַגַּת אֵינוֹ רַשַּׁאי לִשְׁתּוֹת בְּלֹא מַעֲשֵׂר, דַּהֲוֵי שְׁתִיַּת קֶבַע:


וכן בגת – regarding tithing, that if he brought his head and the majority of his body into the wine-pressing vat, he can drink without tithing, for it is considered a ‘chance’/incidental drinking but outside the wine-pressing vat, he is not allowed to drink without tithing for that would be regular drinking.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:6:3)


**קוֹלֵט.** תּוֹפֵס, כְּלוֹמַר מְקַבֵּל מִן הַמַּיִם הַנִּזְחָלִים וְשׁוֹתֶה:


קולט – catch, that is to say, receive from the running waters and drink.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:6:4)


**הַמַּזְחֵלָה.** כְּמִין בִּנְיָן מִדְרוֹן עָשׂוּי סָמוּךְ לַכֹּתֶל, שֶׁזּוֹחֲלִין בּוֹ הַמַּיִם. וּלְהָכִי נִקְרֵאת מַזְחֵלָה, שֶׁכָּל דָּבָר הַמְהַלֵּךְ וְרוֹחֵשׁ עַל גַּבֵּי הַכֹּתֶל אוֹ עַל גַּבֵּי קַרְקַע נִקְרָא זוֹחֵל, כִּדְאָמוּר (דברים לב) זוֹחֲלֵי עָפָר:


המזחלה – like a kind of sloping building made next to a wall where water is running or on top of the ground which is called “flowing”/זוחל as it is written (Deuteronomy 32:24): “[with venomous] creepers in dust.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:6:5)


**לְמַטָּה מֵעֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים.** גָּרְסִינַן. וְהָכִי קָאָמַר, עוֹמֵד אָדָם בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים וְקוֹלֵט בִּכְלִי לְמַטָּה מֵעֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים מִן הַמַּיִם הַיּוֹרְדִין מִן הַמַּזְחֵלָה. וְקוֹלֵט דַּוְקָא, כְּדִמְפָרֵשׁ בַּגְּמָרָא, אֲבָל לֹא יְצָרֵף וִיחַבֵּר פִּיו אוֹ הַכְּלִי לְמַזְחֵלָה שֶׁהִיא בְּפָחוֹת מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה סָמוּךְ לַגַּג, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהִיא לְמַטָּה מֵעֲשָׂרָה, מִכָּל מָקוֹם הוֹאִיל וּמֻטֶּלֶת לְאֹרֶךְ הַכֹּתֶל וּבְתוֹךְ שְׁלֹשָׁה לַגַּג הָוֵי כְּגַג, וְהָוֵי כְּמוֹצִיא מִן הַגַּג שֶׁהוּא רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים:


למטה מעשרה טפחים – is our reading, and this is how it should be understood: A person stands in the public domain and catches [liquid] in a vessel that is ten handbreadths lower from the water that is coming down from the gutter/spout and he catches it explicitly as it explains in the Gemara (Talmud Eruvin 99b) but he does not combine/join and attach to the mouth or the utensil to the spout which is less than three [handbreadths] nearest the roof, even though it is less than ten [handbreadths], nevertheless, for since it is lying on the length of the wall and within three [handbreadths] of the roof, it is like the roof and it is like removing from the roof which is the private domain to the public domain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:6:6)


**וּמִן הַצִּנּוֹר מִכָּל מָקוֹם.** כְּלוֹמַר בֵּין קוֹלֵט בֵּין מְצָרֵף, מִשּׁוּם דְּצִנּוֹר לְעוֹלָם בּוֹלֵט הוּא וְיוֹצֵא לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים. וּבַגְּמָרָא מוֹקֵי לַהּ כְּשֶׁאֵין בַּצִּנּוֹר אַרְבָּעָה עַל אַרְבָּעָה, שֶׁאֵינוֹ חוֹלֵק רְשׁוּת לְעַצְמוֹ:


ומן הצנור מ"מ – that is to say, whether it catches or whether it attaches because the spout always protrudes and goes out to the public domain, and in the Gemara (Talmud Eruvin 99b) it establishes that when the spout lacks four [handbreadths] by four [handbreadths] it doesn’t divide a domain to itself.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:7:1)


**וְחֻלְיָתוֹ עֲשָׂרָה, חַלּוֹן שֶׁעַל גַּבָּיו מְמַלְּאִין מִמֶּנָּה.** כֵּיוָן דְּחֻלְיָתוֹ שֶׁהִיא הַשָּׂפָה אֲשֶׁר סְבִיבוֹתֶיהָ גְּבוֹהָה עֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים, וְקַיְמָא לָן דְּאֵין רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים לְמַעְלָה מֵעֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים, אֶלָּא מָקוֹם פְּטוֹר, לְפִיכָךְ מְמַלְּאִין מֵחַלּוֹן שֶׁעַל גַּבָּהּ בְּשַׁבָּת, דַּהֲווּ מוֹצִיאִין מֵרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד לִרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד דֶּרֶךְ מָקוֹם פְּטוֹר. וַאֲפִלּוּ הַבּוֹר מֻפְלֶגֶת מִן הַכֹּתֶל שֶׁל חַלּוֹן אַרְבָּעָה טְפָחִים, מְמַלְּאִין מִמֶּנָּה, שֶׁאֵין כָּאן רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים מַפְסֶקֶת מִן הַבּוֹר לַחַלּוֹן בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁמְּמַלְּאִין מַיִם, אֶלָּא מָקוֹם פְּטוֹר בִּלְבַד:


וחולייתו י' חלון שעל גבה ממלאין ממנה – because its surrounding bank which is the rim that is around it is ten handbreadths high, and we hold that the public domain is not higher than ten handbreadths, other than in an exempt place, therefore, we fill up from the window that is over it (into the house) on the Sabbath, that entails removing from the private domain to the public domain through an exempt place, and even if a pit is removed from the wall of the window four handbreadths, we fill from it, for there is no public domain forming a partition from the pit to the window at the time when we are filling water other than the exempt place alone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:7:2)


**וְכֵן אַשְׁפָּה בִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים גְּבוֹהָה עֲשָׂרָה טְפָחִים.** רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד הִיא, וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא תִּנָּטֵל אַשְׁפָּה וְתַעֲמֹד עַל פָּחוֹת מֵעֲשָׂרָה וְאָתֵי לְמִשְׁרֵי כִּדְמֵעִקָּרָא. וְדַוְקָא בְּאַשְׁפָּה שֶׁל רַבִּים שֶׁאֵינָהּ עֲשׂוּיָה לְהִפָּנוֹת, אֲבָל אַשְׁפָּה שֶׁל יָחִיד הָעֲשׂוּיָה לְהִפָּנוֹת אֵין זוֹרְקִין לָהּ בְּשַׁבָּת, דְּחַיְשִׁינַן דִּלְמָא מְפַנֶּה לָהּ וְהַוְיָא לַהּ רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים:


וכן אשפה ברשות הרבים גבוהה י' טפחים – it is the private domain and we don’t suspect lest [someone] takes garbage and stands less than ten [handbreadths], and one comes to throw it, as at first, and specifically the garbage of the public, which is not made to be removed but garbage of the individual which is made to be removed we don’t throw it on the Sabbath for we are concerned that perhaps he vacates it/eases oneself on it, and it is like the public domain.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:8:1)


**מֵסֵךְ.** שֶׁנּוֹפוֹ נוֹטֶה לְמַטָּה מִכָּל צְדָדָיו סָבִיב:


המיסך – that its branches hang over downwards from all of its sides around.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:8:2)


**מְטַלְטְלִין תַּחְתָּיו.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּאֵין נוֹפוֹ גָּבוֹהַּ מִן הָאָרֶץ שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים הָוֵי לְהוּ כִּלְבוּדִים לָאָרֶץ, וְנִמְצְאוּ כִּמְחִצּוֹת, לְפִיכָךְ מֻתָּר לְטַלְטֵל תַּחְתָּיו. וּמִכָּל מָקוֹם צָרִיךְ לְמַלְאוֹת אוֹתוֹ אֲוִיר שֶׁבֵּין הַנּוֹף לָאָרֶץ בְּתֶבֶן וְקַשׁ וְכַיּוֹצֵא בּוֹ, וְלִקְשֹׁר הַנּוֹפוֹת שֶׁלֹּא יְזִיזֵם הָרוּחַ, דְּכָל מְחִצָּה שֶׁאֵינָהּ יְכוֹלָה לַעֲמֹד בְּרוּחַ מְצוּיָה אֵינָהּ מְחִצָּה. וְאֵין מְטַלְטְלִין בּוֹ אֶלָּא בְּבֵית סָאתַיִם שֶׁהֵם שִׁבְעִים אַמָּה וְשִׁירַיִם, שֶׁכָּל מְחִצָּה שֶׁתַּשְׁמִישָׁהּ לָאֲוִיר, כְּלוֹמַר לִשְׁמֹר אֲוִיר הַשָּׂדוֹת וְהַכְּרָמִים וְלֹא לָדוּר בָּהּ, אֵין מְטַלְטְלִין בָּהּ אֶלָּא בְּבֵית סָאתַיִם:


מטלטלין תחתיו – for since its branches are not three handbreadths or higher from the ground, they are like the legal fiction of considering separated parts as united and they are seen as partitions. Therefore, it is permitted to carry underneath it, but nevertheless, it is necessary to fill the airspace that is between the branches and the land with straw, stubble and similar things, and to tie up the branches so that the wind doesn’t move them for a partition that is unable to withstand a wind that is present is not a partition. But we don’t carry in it other than in a field requiring one Se’ah of seed (a square measure) which is seventy cubits and a balance (less than eighty) for every partition whose usage is for the airspace, that is to say, to protect the airspace of the fields and vineyards and not to dwell there, we don’t carry there other than with a field requiring one Se’ah of seed/בית סאתים.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:8:3)


**לֹא יֵשֵׁב עֲלֵיהֶן.** שֶׁאָסוּר לְהִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בְּאִילָן, גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יִתְלֹשׁ. וְאִי לֹא גְּבִיהֵי שְׁלֹשָׁה הֲרֵי הֵן כְּקַרְקַע וּמֻתָּר לֵישֵׁב עֲלֵיהֶן:


לא ישב עליהן – for it is prohibited to use a tree lest one detaches something, but if it is not higher than three [handbreadths], it is permitted to sit upon it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:8:4)


**הַדֶּלֶת שֶׁבַּמֻּקְצֶה.** רְחָבָה שֶׁאֲחוֹרֵי הַבָּתִּים, וְהַדֶּלֶת שֶׁלָּהּ אֵינָהּ קְבוּעָה בְּצִיר כִּשְׁאָר דְּלָתוֹת אֶלָּא זְקוּפָה כְּנֶגֶד הַפֶּתַח, וּכְשֶׁהוּא פּוֹתֵחַ מֵטִיל הַדֶּלֶת עַל הָאָרֶץ:


הדלת שבמוקצה – it is wide, that is behind the house, and its door is not affixed with a hinge like other doors but stands upright against the opening and when he opens it, the door attaches to the ground.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:8:5)


**וַחֲדָקִים.** קוֹצִים שֶׁהִתְקִינָן כְּדֵי לִסְתֹּם בָּהֶם פִּרְצָה:


חדקים – thorns that they set up to close with them a breach.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:8:6)


**וְכֵן מַחֲצָלוֹת.** שֶׁל קָנִים, כָּל אֵלּוּ אֵינָם קְשׁוּרִין וְאֵינָן קְבוּעִין בִּמְקוֹמָן, אֶלָּא כְּשֶׁבָּאִין לִפְתֹּחַ מְטִילִין אוֹתָן עַל הָאָרֶץ, לְפִיכָךְ אֵין נוֹעֲלִין בָּהֶם, דְּמֵחֲזֵי כְּבִנְיָן, אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן גְּבוֹהִים מִן הָאָרֶץ, דְּאָז לֹא מֵחֲזֵי כְּבִנְיָן וְנוֹעֲלִין בָּהֶן:


וכן מחלצות – [matting] of reeds – all of these are not attached and are not affixed in their places but when we come too open, we cast them on the ground; therefore, we don’t lock with them for it appears like “building,” other than if they are above the ground, for then, they do not appear like “building” and one can lock things with them.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:9:1)


**לֹא יַעֲמֹד אָדָם בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד.** וְיִטֹּל מַפְתֵּחַ הַמֻּנָּח בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים וְיִפְתַּח בּוֹ פֶּתַח הַחֲנוּת הַמֻּנָּח בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין מִמְּקוֹם הַמַּפְתֵּחַ עַד הַפֶּתַח אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת, גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יַכְנִיס הַמַּפְתֵּחַ אֶצְלוֹ:


לא יעמוד אדם ברשות היחיד – and take a key that is placed in the public domain and open with it the door to the store that is placed in the public domain and even though there isn’t from the place of the key to the opening four cubits, as decree lest he would bring in the key near him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:9:2)


**אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן עָשׂוּ לוֹ מְחִצָּה.** וְיַעֲמֹד בְּתוֹכָהּ וְיִפְתַּח וְיִנְעֹל:


אא"כ עשו לו מחיצה – and stand in it and open it and/or lock it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:9:3)


**אָמְרוּ לוֹ וַהֲלֹא מַעֲשֶׂה בְשׁוּק שֶׁל פַּטָּמִים.** שְׁמָעוּהוּ רַבָּנָן לְרַבִּי מֵאִיר דְּכִי הֵיכִי דְּאָמַר לֹא יַעֲמֹד בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד וְיִפְתַּח בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים וְכוּ'‏ הָכִי נַמִּי קָאָמַר לֹא יַעֲמֹד אָדָם בְּכַרְמְלִית וְיִפְתַּח בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד, בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד וְיִפְתַּח בְּכַרְמְלִית, וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי קָא מוֹתְבֵי לֵיהּ מִשּׁוּק שֶׁל פַּטָּמִים שֶׁהָיָה בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם, דְּכַרְמְלִית הִיא, כֵּיוָן שֶׁדַּלְתוֹתֶיהָ נְעוּלוֹת בַּלַּיְלָה לֹא מִקַּרְיָא רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וְהַחַלּוֹן שֶׁמַּנִּיחִין בּוֹ הַמַּפְתֵּחַ הָיָה רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד, וְהָיוּ עוֹמְדִין בַּכַּרְמְלִית וּפוֹתְחִין בִּרְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד:


אמרו לו ולהא מעשה בשוק של פטמים – the Rabbis heard Rabbi Meir for just as when he said that a person should not stand in the private domain and open [a door] that is in the public domain, this is how it should also be understood: a person should not stand in a Karmelit and open [a door] in the private domain and/or stand in the private domain and open in the Karmelit, and because of this when he gives him from the crammers’ market (where animals are fattened for sale or who deals in such) that was in Jerusalem which is a Karmelit, since its doors are locked at night, it is not a called a public domain, and the window where they place the key was in the private domain, they would stand in the Karmelit and open it the private domain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:9:4)


**פַּטָּמִין.** טַבָּחִים שֶׁמְּפַטְּמִים בְּהֵמוֹת לִשְׁחֹט. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר לֹא בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים וְלֹא בְּכַרְמְלִית:


פטמין – butchers who fatten animals to slaughter, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Meir, neither in the public domain nor in the Karmelit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:9:5)


**שֶׁל צַמָּרִים.** מוֹכְרֵי צֶמֶר:


של צמרים – sellers of wool.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:10:1)


**נֶגֶר.** כְּעֵין בְּרִיחַ שֶׁתּוֹחֲבִים בַּכֳּתָלִים אוֹ בָּאַסְקֻפָּה לִנְעֹל בּוֹ הַדֶּלֶת:


נגר – a kind of door-bolt that they insert in the walls or in the lintel to lock the door.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:10:2)


**גְּלֻסְטְרָא.** שֶׁרֹאשׁוֹ עָבֶה וְעָגֹל כְּמִין רִמּוֹן, וְעָשׂוּי כְּבוּכְנָא שֶׁרָאוּי לָדוּךְ בּוֹ פִּלְפְּלִין. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּתוֹרַת כְּלִי עָלָיו, אוֹסֵר רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן קָשׁוּר וְתָלוּי בַּדֶּלֶת, וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵי מַתִּיר, וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


גלוסטרא – that its top is wide and round like a pomegranate and is made like a club (with a handle used as a door-bar – see Talmud Eruvin 102a) that one uses to pound with it peppers, and even though it has the status of a utensil, Rabbi Eliezer forbids it other than it if is attached and hanging on the door. But Rabbi Yosi permits it and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yosi.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:11:1)


**נֶגֶר הַנִּגְרָר.** בְּרִיחַ הַקָּשׁוּר בַּדֶּלֶת אֲבָל אֵינוֹ תָּלוּי בּוֹ, שֶׁהַחֶבֶל הַקָּשׁוּר בּוֹ אָרֹךְ, וְהַבְּרִיחַ נִגְרָר עַל גַּבֵּי קַרְקַע. וּבְנֶגֶר שֶׁאֵין בְּרֹאשׁוֹ גְּלֻסְטְרָא עָסְקִינַן. דִּבְיֵשׁ בְּרֹאשׁוֹ גְּלֻסְטְרָא הָא שָׁרֵי לֵיהּ רַבִּי יוֹסֵי לְעֵיל אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ קָשׁוּר, וַהֲלָכָה כְּמוֹתוֹ:


נגר הנגרר – a bolt that is attached to the door, but it is not suspended from it, as the rope that is tied to it is long and the bolt drags on the ground, and we are dealing with a bolt that lacks at its head a knob/club. But if it has a club/knob, Rabbi Yosi permits it even though it is not attached, and the Halakha is according to him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:11:2)


**נוֹעֲלִים בּוֹ בַמִּקְדָּשׁ.** דִּשְׁבוּת בְּעָלְמָא הוּא וְאֵינוֹ בּוֹנֶה מַמָּשׁ הוֹאִיל וְקָשׁוּר הוּא, אֶלָּא מִפְּנֵי שֶׁנִּגְרָר מֵחֲזֵי כְּבוֹנֶה, וְאֵין שְׁבוּת בַּמִּקְדָּשׁ:


נועלים בו במקדש – for this is merely something forbidden by the Rabbis as being out of harmony with the celebration of the Sabbath (and Yom Tov), and it is not actually “building” for since it is fastened, but rather because it drags, it appears like building, but there is no Sh’vut in the Temple.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:11:3)


**וְהַמֻּנָּח.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ קָשׁוּר כְּלָל, הָוֵי בִּנְיָן מַמָּשׁ. וְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה סָבַר לֹא הָוֵי בִּנְיָן מַמָּשׁ אֶלָּא מֵחֲזֵי כְּבִנְיָן, וּבַמִּקְדָּשׁ לֹא גָּזְרוּ מִשּׁוּם שְׁבוּת. וְהַנִּגְרָר אַף בַּמְּדִינָה מֻתָּר הוֹאִיל וְקָשׁוּר, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ תָּלוּי. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


והמונח – it is not attached at all, it is really “building.” And Rabbi Yehuda holds that it is not really “building,” but rather looks like “building.” But in the Temple, they (i.e., the Rabbis) did not decree because of Sh’vut. But even one who drags in the country (i.e., outside of Jerusalem/the Temple), is permitted, since it is attached, and even though it is not suspended. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 12

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:12:1)


**מַחֲזִירִין צִיר הַתַּחְתּוֹן.** שֶׁל דֶּלֶת, שֶׁכָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא יָצָא הָעֶלְיוֹן נוֹחַ לְהַחְזִירוֹ וְאֵין כָּאן בִּנְיָן:


מחזירין ציר התחתון – of the door for all the time that the upper hinge/pivot had not gone out, it is easy to restore it, and there is no “building” involved.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:12:2)


**אֲבָל לֹא בַמְּדִינָה.** גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יִתְקַע, דַּהֲוֵי מְלָאכָה:


אבל לא במדינה – a decree lest he fasten it which is work.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:12:3)


**וְהָעֶלְיוֹן כָּאן וְכָאן אָסוּר.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּיָצָא עֶלְיוֹן כֻּלּוֹ נוֹפֵל, וַהֲוָה לֵיהּ כְּבוֹנֶה:


העליון כאן וכאן אסור – for since the top [hinge] came out, all of it falls and it would be to him like “building.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:12:4)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** דִּסְבִירָא לֵיהּ אֵין בִּנְיָן בְּכֵלִים וְלֵיכָּא אֶלָּא שְׁבוּת, וּבַמִּקְדָּשׁ לֹא גָּזְרוּ עַל הַשְּׁבוּת. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי יהודה אומר כו' – there is no building with utensils and there isn’t anything other than [the issue of] doing something forbidden by the Rabbis as being out of harmony with the celebration of the day. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 13

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:13:1)


**מַחֲזִירִין רְטִיָּה.** כֹּהֵן שֶׁהֻצְרַךְ לַעֲבֹד עֲבוֹדָה בְּשַׁבָּת, וְנָטַל רְטִיָּה שֶׁהָיְתָה עַל גַּבֵּי מַכָּה שֶׁעַל יָדוֹ כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא תְּהֵא חוֹצֶצֶת בֵּין בְּשָׂרוֹ לָעֲבוֹדָה, מַחֲזִירָהּ עַל גַּבֵּי מַכָּתוֹ לְאַחַר הָעֲבוֹדָה, דְּאִי לֹא שָׁרֵית לֵיהּ לְהַחְזִירָהּ מִמְּנַע וְלֹא עֲבִיד עֲבוֹדָה:


מחזירין רטיה – a Kohen that needed to perform the Temple service on Shabbat and took off an emollient that had been on his hand in order that there wouldn’t be an intervening object between his flesh and his Temple service, restores it on top of his wound after the Temple service performed, for if you did not permit him to restore it, he would be prevented and would not perform the Temple service.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:13:2)


**אֲבָל לֹא בַמְּדִינָה.** גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יִמְרַח הָרְטִיָּה, וְחַיָּב מִשּׁוּם מְמַחֵק:


אבל לא במדינה – a decree lest he would rub off the emollient and be liable because of rubbing/blotting out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:13:3)


**וְאִם בַּתְּחִלָּה.** שֶׁלֹּא הָיְתָה קְשׁוּרָה מִבְּעוֹד יוֹם וְכֹהֵן זֶה לֹא סִלְּקָהּ לְצֹרֶךְ עֲבוֹדָה:


ואם בתחלה – it was not attached while it was still daylight and this Kohen did not go up for the needs of the Temple service.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:13:4)


**כָּאן וְכָאן אָסוּר.** וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן הָכָא אֵין שְׁבוּת בַּמִּקְדָּשׁ, דְּלָאו צֹרֶךְ גָּבוֹהַּ הוּא אֶלָּא צֹרֶךְ עַצְמוֹ. וְהָא דְּאָסְרֵי רַבָּנָן הַחְזָרַת הָרְטִיָּה אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא כְּשֶׁפֵּרְשָׁה עַל גַּבֵּי קַרְקַע, אֲבָל אִם לְקָחָהּ בְּיָדוֹ, כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהִיא בְּיָדוֹ, אוֹ אֲפִלּוּ נְתָנָהּ עַל גַּבֵּי כְּלִי וְלֹא פֵּרְשָׁהּ עַל גַּבֵּי קַרְקַע, מֻתָּר לְהַחְזִירָהּ בְּכָל מָקוֹם:


כאן וכאן אסור – and we don’t say here that there is no issue of doing something forbidden by the Rabbis as being out of harmony with the celebration of the day in the Temple, for it is not for the need of the Most High (i.e., God), but his own need hat the Rabbis forbade restoring the emollient for it is not anything other than spreading it out on the ground, but if he took it in his hand, all the time that it is in his hand or even placed upon a utensil and he didn’t spread it on the ground, it is permissible to restore it, in every case.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:13:5)


**קוֹשְׁרִין נִימָא.** נִימַת כִּנּוֹר שֶׁל שִׁיר הַלְּוִיִּים שֶׁנִּפְסְקָה בְּשַׁבָּת, דְּקָסָבַר מַכְשִׁירֵי מִצְוָה שֶׁלֹּא יָכוֹל לַעֲשׂוֹתָם מֵאֶתְמוֹל, דְּהָא הַיּוֹם נִפְסְקָה, דּוֹחִין אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת. וְדַוְקָא כְּשֶׁנִּפְסְקָה בָּאֶמְצַע, שֶׁאֵינוֹ קוֹשְׁרָהּ אֶלָּא לְפִי שָׁעָה, אֲבָל סָמוּךְ לַיְּתֵדוֹת דַּהֲוֵי קֶשֶׁר שֶׁל קְיָמָא אָסוּר:


They may tie up a string - the string of a ‘kinor’ played by the Levites [in the Temple] which snapped on the Sabbath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:13:6)


**וְאִם בַּתְּחִלָּה.** שֶׁלֹּא הָיְתָה שָׁם מֵעוֹלָם, דְּיָכוֹל לַעֲשׂוֹתָהּ מֵאֶתְמוֹל, אָסוּר:


ואם בתחלה – he had never been there that he couldn’t have done it from the day before, it is prohibited.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:13:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:13:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:13:7)


**יַבֶּלֶת.** הִיא מוּם בַּקָּדָשִׁים, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (ויקרא כב) אוֹ יַבֶּלֶת, וְחוֹתְכִים אוֹתָהּ בַּמִּקְדָּשׁ בַּיָּד, דְּאֵין בָּזֶה אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם שְׁבוּת, דְּכִלְאַחַר יַד הוּא. אֲבָל לֹא בִּכְלִי, דִּמְלָאכָה גְּמוּרָה הִיא, שֶׁכָּךְ עוֹשִׂים בְּחֹל. וּכְשֶׁחוֹתֵךְ מִבַּעַל חַי הָוֵי תּוֹלָדָה דְּגוֹזֵז אֶת הַצֶּמֶר:


יבלת – it is a blemish on holy things, as it states (Leviticus 22:22): “[Anything blind, or injured, or maimed,] or with a wen, [boil-scar or scurvy – such you shall not offer to the LORD; you shall not put any of them on the altar as offerings by fire to the LORD],” and we cut it off in the Temple by hand for [this act] is nothing other than doing something forbidden by the Rabbis as being out of the harmony of the Sabbath day, and it is done in with the back of the hand/indirectly but not with a utensil, since that is complete work which is how it is accomplished on weekdays, and when you cut it off from a living being, that is a derivative of shearing the wool.



### Mishnah 14

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:14:1)


**כּוֹרֵךְ עָלֶיהָ גֶמִי.** וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַגֶּמִי מְרַפֵּא אֶת הַמַּכָּה, הוֹאִיל וְהַשְׁתָּא מִיהָא צֹרֶךְ עֲבוֹדָה הוּא, דְּלָאו אֹרַח אַרְעָא שֶׁתְּהֵא מַכָּתוֹ נִרְאֵית עִם הָעֲבוֹדָה, וּמְכַסֶּה אוֹתָהּ בְּגֶמִי:


כורך עליה גמי – even though the reed heals the wound for since now, however, it is the need of the Temple service that is not the custom that his wound should be seen with the Temple service and he covers it with reed/bulrushes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:14:2)


**אֲבָל לֹא בַמְּדִינָה.** דִּרְפוּאָה בְּשַׁבָּת שְׁבוּת הִיא וְאָסוּר:


אבל לא במדינה – for healing on the Sabbath is something forbidden by the Rabbis as being out of harmony of the Sabbath day and is prohibited.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:14:3)


**וְאִם לְהוֹצִיא דָם.** שֶׁמְּהַדְּקָהּ בְּגֶמִי כְּדֵי לְהוֹצִיא דָּמָהּ:


ואם להוציא דם – that he fastens it with red/bulrushes in order to remove its blood.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:14:4)


**כָּאן וְכָאן אָסוּר.** דַּהֲוָה לֵיהּ חוֹבֵל וְאַב מְלָאכָה לֹא אִשְׁתְּרִי בַּמִּקְדָּשׁ:


כאן וכאן אסור – for it is wounding a person and is one of the chief labors forbidden on the Sabbath and would not be permitted in the Temple.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:14:5)


**בּוֹזְקִים מֶלַח.** כּוֹתְתִים וּמְפַזְּרִים מֶלַח עַל גַּבֵּי הַכֶּבֶשׁ שֶׁעוֹלִים בּוֹ לַמִּזְבֵּחַ, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁחָלָק הוּא וּפְעָמִים נוֹפְלִים מִמֶּנּוּ, שֶׁמַּחְלִיקִים רַגְלֵיהֶם בּוֹ. בּוֹזְקִים, לְשׁוֹן כְּתִיתָה:


בוזקים מלח – crush and scatter salt on the ramp that they (i.e., the Kohanim) ascend on it to the altar because it is smooth and sometimes, they fall from it and their feet slip on it. בוזקים is the language of crushing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:14:6)


**אֲבָל לֹא בַמְּדִינָה.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא מְתַקֵּן:


אבל לא במדינה – because it is repairing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:14:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:14:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:14:7)


**מְמַלְּאִים מַיִם מִבּוֹר הַגּוֹלָה וּמִבּוֹר הַגָּדוֹל.** כָּךְ שְׁמָן, וּשְׁנֵיהֶם הָיוּ בִּלְשָׁכוֹת הָעֲזָרָה:


ממלאים מים מבור הגולה ומבור הגדול – this is their names and both of them are in the chamber of the Temple courtyard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:14:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:14:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:14:8)


**בַּגַּלְגַּל.** הֶעָשׂוּי לְהַעֲלוֹת בּוֹ הַדְּלִי עַל יְדֵי חֲבָלִים. וּמִשְּׁנֵי בּוֹרוֹת הַלָּלוּ בִּלְבַד הִתִּירוּ לְמַלְאוֹת מַיִם בַּגַּלְגַּל בְּשַׁבָּת, אֲבָל בִּשְׁאָר בּוֹרוֹת אָסוּר, גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יְמַלֵּא לְגִנָּתוֹ וּלְחֻרְבָתוֹ, מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁמְּמַלְּאִין בְּלֹא טֹרַח אָתֵי לְהַשְׁקוֹת בּוֹ גִּנָּתוֹ וְחֻרְבָתוֹ בְּשַׁבָּת. וּבְמָקוֹם דְּלֵיכָּא לְמִגְזַר שֶׁמָּא יַשְׁקֶה מַיִם לְגִנָּתוֹ וּלְחֻרְבָתוֹ מֻתָּר לְמַלְאוֹת בַּגַּלְגַּל, וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן מִפְּנֵי שֶׁמַּשְׁמִיעַ קוֹל, שֶׁלֹּא אָסְרוּ אֶלָּא קוֹל שֶׁל שִׁיר בִּלְבַד. וְהָכִי נַמִּי שָׁרֵי לְמִטְרַף אַבָּבָא וּמֵיקַשׁ עַל תַּרְעָא בְּשַׁבָּת וְאֵין בְּכָךְ כְּלוּם:


בגלגל – that is made to lift up the bucket by it through cables and from these two wells alone, they (i.e., the Rabbis) permitted to fill water with the wheel on Shabbat, but the other wells were forbidden as a decree lest he fill up his garden and his ruins because he fills it without a trouble, and he would end up watering with his garden and ruins on Shabbat, and in a place where there one doesn’t make a decree lest he provide water to his garden and his ruins, it is permitted to fill them on the wheel. But we don’t suspect because a public announcement is made as they only prohibited the sound of the song alone. And that is so that they permitted knocking at the door or striking the gate on Shabbat and there is nothing in this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:14:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:14:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:14:9)


**וּמִבּוֹר הַקַּר.** עַל שֵׁם שֶׁהָיָה בְּאֵר מַיִם חַיִּים קוֹרֵא לֵיהּ בּוֹר הַקַּר, לְשׁוֹן מָקוֹר. וּבוֹר זֶה הָיָה צָרִיךְ לִבְנֵי הַגּוֹלָה בָּרֶגֶל, וְהִתִּירוּ לָהֶם נְבִיאִים שֶׁבֵּינֵיהֶם חַגַּי זְכַרְיָה וּמַלְאָכִי לְמַלְאוֹת מִמֶּנּוּ בַּגַּלְגַּל בָּרֶגֶל, וְנִשְׁאַר בְּהֶתֵּרוֹ שֶׁמְּמַלְּאִים מִמֶּנּוּ בְּיוֹם טוֹב בְּגַלְגַּל אַף בַּמְּדִינָה, מַה שֶּׁאֵין כֵּן בִּשְׁאָר בְּאֵרוֹת הַנּוֹבְעִים:


ומבור הקר – on account of the fact that it was a well of living waters, they called it the Welling/Outpouring Well, from the language of “source”/מקור. And this well was needed for the people from the Diaspora on the Jewish holy day and the prophets that were among them, Zechariah and Malachi permitted them to fill from it with a wheel on the Jewish holy day and it remained when they permitted that they could fill from it with a wheel on the Jewish holy day even in the countryside (i.e., outside of Jerusalem in the land of Israel), which was not the case with the other wells that flow.



### Mishnah 15

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:15:1)


**כֹּהֵן מוֹצִיאוֹ בְהֶמְיָנוֹ.** בְּשַׁבָּת. וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן לְטִלְטוּל, דְּאֵין שְׁבוּת בַּמִּקְדָּשׁ:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:15:2)


**בְּהֶמְיָנוֹ.** בְּאַבְנֵטוֹ. וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּמְטַמֵּא לֵיהּ לָאַבְנֵט שֶׁהוּא קָדוֹשׁ, הָכִי עָדִיף, שֶׁלֹּא לְהַשְׁהוֹת אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה בָּעֲזָרָה לְחַזֵּר אַחַר הַצְּבָת שֶׁל עֵץ. וּבְיָדָיו לֹא נָגַע בֵּיהּ, דְּלֹא נִטְמֵי כֹהֵן גּוּפֵיהּ, הִלְכָּךְ בְּהֶמְיָנוֹ אוֹחֲזוֹ דְּלֹא נָגַע. וְשֶׁרֶץ אֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא בְּמַשָּׂא, וְהָאַבְנֵט שֶׁנִּטְמָא בַּשֶּׁרֶץ אֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא אֶת הַכֹּהֵן כְּשֶׁאוֹחֲזוֹ, דַּהֲוָה לֵיהּ אַבְנֵט רִאשׁוֹן לַטֻּמְאָה, וְאֵין אָדָם וְכֵלִים מְקַבְּלִין טֻמְאָה אֶלָּא מֵאַב הַטֻּמְאָה:


בהמיינו – with his belt and even though it ritually defiles his belt which is holy, this is preferable so that the defilement is not detained in the Temple courtyard to go to search after a pair of wooden tongs and that his hands didn’t come in contact with it so that the Kohen himself would not be defiled. Therefore, with his girdle he grabs it that he didn’t touch it and the unclean reptile does not defile through carrying and the belt which is ritually defiled by the unclean reptile does not defile the Kohen when he holds it for the belt is first level of Levitical defilement, but a person and utensils do not receive defilement other than from the original or direct causes of Levitical defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:15:3)


**צְבָת שֶׁל עֵץ.** שֶׁהִיא פְּשׁוּטֵי כְּלֵי עֵץ וְאֵין מְקַבְּלִים טֻמְאָה:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:15:4)


**שֶׁלֹּא לְרַבּוֹת אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה.** לְטַמֵּא אַבְנֵט הַטָּהוֹר, וְנוֹחַ לוֹ לְהַשְׁהוֹתָהּ שָׁם וּלְחַזֵּר אַחַר הַצְּבָת מִלְּרַבּוֹת אֶת הַטֻּמְאָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


שלא לרבות את הטומאה – to ritually defile a pure belt and it is appropriate for him to detain it there and to go after a pair of tongs rather than increase the defilement. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:15:5)


**מֵהֵיכָן מוֹצִיאִין אוֹתוֹ.** בְּשַׁבָּת:


מהיכן מוציאין אותו – on the Sabbath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:15:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:15:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:15:6)


**מִן הַהֵיכָל וּמִן הָאוּלָם.** אֲבָל בָּעֲזָרָה אִם נִמְצָא שָׁם מְכַסֶּה אוֹתוֹ בִּפְסַכְתֵּר וּמַנִּיחוֹ עַד שֶׁתֶּחְשַׁךְ:


מן ההיכל ומן האולם – but if it is found in the Temple courtyard, cover it with a large pot and leave it there until it gets dark.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:15:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:15:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:15:7)


**כָּל מָקוֹם שֶׁחַיָּבִין עַל זְדוֹנוֹ כָּרֵת.** אִם יִכָּנֵס לוֹ בְּטֻמְאָה, דְּהַיְנוּ כָּל הָעֲזָרָה, מִשָּׁם מוֹצִיאִין אוֹתוֹ:


כל מקום שחייבין על זדונו כרת – if he would enter it in a state of ritual defilement into the Temple courtyard, they remove him from there and all the other places they cover it with a large pot, a copper pot.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:15:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:15:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:15:8)


**וּשְׁאָר כָּל הַמְּקוֹמוֹת כּוֹפִין עָלָיו פְּסַכְתֵּר.** סִיר שֶׁל נְחֹשֶׁת. תַּרְגּוּם שֶׁל סִירוֹתָיו פְּסַכְתְּרָוָתֵיהּ. לְכַסּוֹתוֹ עַד שֶׁתֶּחְשַׁךְ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


ושאר כל המקומות כופין עליו פסכתר – The Aramaic translation of his pots is his pots to cover it until it gets dark, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiva.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Eruvin 10:15:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:15:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Eruvin/r/10:15:9)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר מְקוֹם שֶׁהִתִּירוּ לְךָ חֲכָמִים וְכוּ'.** רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן פָּלֵיג אַתַּנָּא קַמָּא דִּלְעֵיל דְּאָמַר קוֹשְׁרִים נִימָא בַּמִּקְדָּשׁ, וְסָבַר דְּאֵין קוֹשְׁרִים נִימַת כִּנּוֹר שֶׁנִּפְסְקָה אֶלָּא בַּעֲנִיבָה שֶׁהִיא אֲסוּרָה מִשּׁוּם שְׁבוּת בִּלְבַד וְאִי אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁיָּבֹא בָּהּ לִידֵי חִיּוּבָא דְּאוֹרַיְתָא, אֲבָל לֹא קוֹשְׁרִין בְּקֶשֶׁר שֶׁאֶפְשָׁר לָבֹא בּוֹ לִידֵי חִיּוּבָא דְּאוֹרַיְתָא. וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן לְתַנָּא קַמָּא, אַל תִּתְמַהּ עָלַי שֶׁאֲנִי מַחְמִיר כָּאן וְגַבֵּי תְּחוּמִין אֲנִי מֵקֵל טְפֵי וְאוֹמֵר שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ יָצָא חוּץ לַתְּחוּם חֲמֵשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה אַמָּה יִכָּנֵס (כדלעיל פ"ד מי"א), דְּגַבֵּי תְּחוּמִין מִשֶּׁלְּךָ נָתְנוּ לְךָ, מִשּׁוּם דְּיָדְעִינַן שֶׁאוֹתָם חֲמֵשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה אַמָּה אֵינָם חוּץ לַתְּחוּם, שֶׁמּוֹדְדֵי הַתְּחוּמִין הַקּוֹבְעִין סִימָנִין בְּסוֹף הַתְּחוּם קִים לָן בְּגַוַּיְהוּ שֶׁאֵין קוֹבְעִין הַתְּחוּם בְּסוֹף אַלְפַּיִם מַמָּשׁ אֶלָּא כּוֹנְסִין לְתוֹךְ הַתְּחוּם מִפְּנֵי הַטּוֹעִין שֶׁאֵין מַכִּירִין הַסִּימָן וּפְעָמִים יוֹצְאִים לְהַלָּן מִמֶּנּוּ מְעַט וְחוֹזְרִין, וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי אֲנִי מֵקֵל בַּתְּחוּמִים, אֲבָל לִקְשֹׁר נִימָא בַּמִּקְדָּשׁ, שֶׁלֹּא הִתִּירוּ בַּמִּקְדָּשׁ אֶלָּא אִסּוּר שְׁבוּת לֹא חִיּוּבָא דְּאוֹרַיְתָא, אֲנִי מַחְמִיר וְאוֹמֵר דְּדַוְקָא עֲנִיבָה שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהּ אִסּוּר שְׁבוּת הִתִּירוּ, אֲבָל לִקְשֹׁר קֶשֶׁר מַמָּשׁ שֶׁפְּעָמִים יֵשׁ בּוֹ חִיּוּבָא דְּאוֹרַיְתָא לֹא. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ר"ש אומר מקום שהתירו – Rabbi Shimon disputes the first Tanna/teacher of above (i.e., Mishnah 13) who said that we tie a string in the Temple and he holds that we do not tie a chord of harp that broke other than fastening with a loop (i.e., not making a knot) which is forbidden because only because of being an activity that the Rabbis forbade as not being in harmony with the observances of the day/Sh’vut. But it is impossible that it would come through it to an obligation from the Torah, but we don’t tie with a knot, for it is possible that it would come through it to an obligation from the Torah, and Rabbi Shimon says to the first Tanna/teacher: do not be amazed at me that I am stringent here and regarding Sabbath limits, I am more lenient. For I saw, that even if he went fifteen cubits outside the Sabbath limit, he can enter. For concerning Sabbath limits, they (i.e., the Sages) gave to you what is yours because they knew that those fifteen cubits are not outside of the Sabbath limits for those who measure the Sabbath limits establish fixed markers at the end of the limit, it is established regarding them that they don’t fix the limit at the end of two-thousand exactly, but rather enter into the limit because of those who err who don’t recognize the sign/symbol and sometimes they go out further than it a little bit and then return. For that reason, I am lenient with the Sabbath limits. But to tie a cord in the Temple which they did not permit in the Temple, other than a prohibition of Sh’vut/forbidding something as not being in harmony with the observance of the day of Shabbat which is not a Torah obligation I am strict and say that specifically fastening with a loop (i.e., not making a knot) which has the prohibition of not being in harmony with the observance of the day of Shabbat, but not to make an actual [permanent] knot, for sometimes there is an obligation from the Torah. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.
